One Token per Highly Selective Frame: Towards Extreme Compression for Long Video Understanding
Authors: Zheyu Zhang, Ziqi Pang, Shixing Chen, Xiang Hao, Vimal Bhat, Yu-Xiong Wang
First: 2026-04-15T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long video understanding is inherently challenging for vision-language models (VLMs) because of the extensive number of frames. With each video frame typically expanding into tens or hundreds of tokens, the limited context length of large language models (LLMs) forces the VLMs to perceive the frames sparsely and lose temporal information. To address this, we explore extreme video token compression towards \emph{one token per frame} at the final LLM layer. Our key insight is that heuristic-based compression, widely adopted by previous methods, is prone to information loss, and this necessitates supervising LLM layers into \emph{learnable} and \emph{progressive} modules for \emph{token-level compression} (LP-Comp). Such compression enables our VLM to digest 2x-4x more frames with improved performance. To further increase the token efficiency, we investigate \emph{frame-level compression}, which selects the frames most relevant to the queries via the internal attention scores of the LLM layers, named \emph{question-conditioned compression} (QC-Comp). As a notable distinction from previous studies, we mitigate the position bias of LLM attention in long contexts, \emph{i.e.}, the over-concentration on the beginning and end of a sequence, by splitting long videos into short segments and employing local attention. Collectively, our combined \emph{token-level} and \emph{frame-level} leads to an e\textbf{x}treme compression model for long video understanding, named \textbf{\name}, achieving a significantly larger compression ratio and enabling denser frame sampling. Our \name is finetuned from VideoChat-Flash with a data-efficient \emph{supervised compression tuning} stage that only requires 2.5\% of the supervised fine-tuning data, yet boosts the accuracy from 42.9\% to 46.2\% on LVBench and enhances multiple other long video benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：每个高选择性帧一个标记：面向长视频理解的极压缩
长视频理解对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）而言固有挑战，因视频帧数量庞大。每个视频帧通常扩展为数十至数百个标记，而大语言模型（LLMs）有限的上下文长度迫使VLMs稀疏感知帧并丢失时序信息。为此，我们探索向最终LLM层实现\emph{每帧一个标记}的极端视频标记压缩。我们的核心见解是：此前方法广泛采用的启发式压缩易导致信息丢失，因此需将LLM层监督为\emph{可学习}和\emph{渐进式}模块，以实现\emph{标记级压缩}（LP-Comp）。此类压缩使我们的VLM能处理2至4倍更多帧且性能提升。为进一步提高标记效率，我们研究\emph{帧级压缩}，通过LLM层内部注意力分数选择与查询最相关的帧，称为\emph{问题条件压缩}（QC-Comp）。与以往研究显著不同的是，我们通过将长视频分割为短片段并采用局部注意力，缓解LLM注意力在长上下文中的位置偏差（即对序列首尾过度集中）。综合\emph{标记级}与\emph{帧级}压缩，我们构建面向长视频理解的\textbf{\name}极压缩模型，实现显著更大的压缩比并支持更密集的帧采样。\name基于VideoChat-Flash微调，采用仅需2.5\%监督微调数据的\emph{监督压缩调优}阶段，使LVBench准确率从42.9\%提升至46.2\%，并在多个其他长视频基准测试中表现更优。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of long video understanding where vision-language models struggle with excessive frame-to-token expansion and limited LLM context length, the paper proposes extreme compression methods that reduce each frame to one token. It introduces LP-Comp, a learnable and progressive token-level compression module supervised to preserve temporal information, enabling the model to process 2x-4x more frames with improved performance. Further, QC-Comp selects frames based on LLM attention scores to align with query relevance, while mitigating position bias via segmenting videos and using local attention. The combined approach, named \name, achieves extreme compression and denser sampling, boosting accuracy from 42.9% to 46.2% on LVBench using only 2.5% of supervised fine-tuning data.
为解决长视频理解中视觉-语言模型因帧数过多导致的计算瓶颈和LLM上下文长度限制，该研究提出双阶段压缩策略：首先通过可学习的渐进式令牌级压缩（LP-Comp）将每帧压缩为一个令牌，保留时序信息；其次通过基于问题的帧级压缩（QC-Comp）根据LLM注意力分数选择关键帧。同时，为缓解LLM注意力的位置偏差，采用分段与局部注意力机制。实验表明，该组合方法实现极端压缩，处理帧数提升2–4倍，性能显著提升，在LVBench上准确率从42.9%提升至46.2%，仅需2.5%的监督微调数据。
Seedance 2.0: Advancing Video Generation for World Complexity
Authors: Team Seedance, De Chen, Liyang Chen, Xin Chen, Ying Chen, Zhuo Chen, Zhuowei Chen, Feng Cheng, Tianheng Cheng, Yufeng Cheng, Mojie Chi, Xuyan Chi, Jian Cong, Qinpeng Cui, Fei Ding, Qide Dong, Yujiao Du, Haojie Duanmu, Junliang Fan, Jiarui Fang, Jing Fang, Zetao Fang, Chengjian Feng, Yu Gao, Diandian Gu, Dong Guo, Hanzhong Guo, Qiushan Guo, Boyang Hao, Hongxiang Hao, Haoxun He, Jiaao He, Qian He, Tuyen Hoang, Heng Hu, Ruoqing Hu, Yuxiang Hu, Jiancheng Huang, Weilin Huang, Zhaoyang Huang, Zhongyi Huang, Jishuo Jin, Ming Jing, Ashley Kim, Shanshan Lao, Yichong Leng, Bingchuan Li, Gen Li, Haifeng Li, Huixia Li, Jiashi Li, Ming Li, Xiaojie Li, Xingxing Li, Yameng Li, Yiying Li, Yu Li, Yueyan Li, Chao Liang, Han Liang, Jianzhong Liang, Ying Liang, Wang Liao, J. First: 2026-04-15T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:59:40+00:00
Comments: Seedance 2.0 Model Card
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Seedance 2.0 is a new native multi-modal audio-video generation model, officially released in China in early February 2026. Compared with its predecessors, Seedance 1.0 and 1.5 Pro, Seedance 2.0 adopts a unified, highly efficient, and large-scale architecture for multi-modal audio-video joint generation. This allows it to support four input modalities: text, image, audio, and video, by integrating one of the most comprehensive suites of multi-modal content reference and editing capabilities available in the industry to date. It delivers substantial, well-rounded improvements across all key sub-dimensions of video and audio generation. In both expert evaluations and public user tests, the model has demonstrated performance on par with the leading levels in the field. Seedance 2.0 supports direct generation of audio-video content with durations ranging from 4 to 15 seconds, with native output resolutions of 480p and 720p. For multi-modal inputs as reference, its current open platform supports up to 3 video clips, 9 images, and 3 audio clips. In addition, we provide Seedance 2.0 Fast version, an accelerated variant of Seedance 2.0 designed to boost generation speed for low-latency scenarios. Seedance 2.0 has delivered significant improvements to its foundational generation capabilities and multi-modal generation performance, bringing an enhanced creative experience for end users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Seedance 2.0：推进面向世界复杂性的视频生成
Seedance 2.0 是一款于2026年2月初在中国正式发布的全新原生多模态音视频生成模型。相比前代 Seedance 1.0 和 1.5 Pro，Seedance 2.0 采用统一、高效且大规模的多模态音视频联合生成架构，支持文本、图像、音频和视频四种输入模态，并整合了当前业界最全面的多模态内容参考与编辑能力套件。其在音视频生成的关键子维度上实现了全面且显著的提升。在专家评估与公众用户测试中，该模型表现达到行业领先水平。Seedance 2.0 支持直接生成时长4至15秒的音视频内容，原生输出分辨率为480p和720p。对于多模态输入参考，其当前开放平台支持最多3个视频片段、9张图片和3个音频片段。此外，我们还提供 Seedance 2.0 快速版，专为低延迟场景设计，以提升生成速度。Seedance 2.0 在基础生成能力与多模态生成性能方面均实现显著提升，为终端用户带来更佳的创作体验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient and versatile multi-modal audio-video generation, Seedance 2.0 introduces a unified, large-scale architecture that jointly processes text, image, audio, and video inputs to produce synchronized outputs. The model supports up to three video clips, nine images, and three audio clips as reference inputs, enabling complex editing and generation tasks. Experimental results from expert evaluations and public user tests confirm its performance matches industry-leading models, with native support for 4–15 second clips at 480p and 720p resolutions. A faster variant, Seedance 2.0 Fast, is also available for low-latency applications, enhancing usability without compromising quality.
为提升多模态音视频生成的效率与完整性，Seedance 2.0采用统一的大规模架构，可联合生成音频与视频，支持文本、图像、音频、视频四种输入模态。其开放平台支持最多三段视频、九张图片及三段音频作为参考输入，实现灵活的内容编辑与合成。实验结果显示，该模型在专家评估与公众测试中均达到业界领先水平，支持最高720p分辨率、4至15秒时长的输出，其加速版Fast版本亦可满足低延迟场景下的快速生成需求。
ROSE: Retrieval-Oriented Segmentation Enhancement
Authors: Song Tang, Guangquan Jie, Henghui Ding, Yu-Gang Jiang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-15T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:59:35+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Findings, Project Page: https://henghuiding.com/ROSE/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing segmentation models based on multimodal large language models (MLLMs), such as LISA, often struggle with novel or emerging entities due to their inability to incorporate up-to-date knowledge. To address this challenge, we introduce the Novel Emerging Segmentation Task (NEST), which focuses on segmenting (i) novel entities that MLLMs fail to recognize due to their absence from training data, and (ii) emerging entities that exist within the model's knowledge but demand up-to-date external information for accurate recognition. To support the study of NEST, we construct a NEST benchmark using an automated pipeline that generates news-related data samples for comprehensive evaluation. Additionally, we propose ROSE: Retrieval-Oriented Segmentation Enhancement, a plug-and-play framework designed to augment any MLLM-based segmentation model. ROSE comprises four key components. First, an Internet Retrieval-Augmented Generation module is introduced to employ user-provided multimodal inputs to retrieve real-time web information. Then, a Textual Prompt Enhancer enriches the model with up-to-date information and rich background knowledge, improving the model's perception ability for emerging entities. Furthermore, a Visual Prompt Enhancer is proposed to compensate for MLLMs' lack of exposure to novel entities by leveraging internet-sourced images. To maintain efficiency, a WebSense module is introduced to intelligently decide when to invoke retrieval mechanisms based on user input. Experimental results demonstrate that ROSE significantly boosts performance on the NEST benchmark, outperforming a strong Gemini-2.0 Flash-based retrieval baseline by 19.2 in gIoU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROSE：面向检索的分割增强
现有基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的分割模型，如LISA，常因无法整合最新知识而难以处理新出现或新兴实体。为应对这一挑战，我们提出新型涌现分割任务（NEST），聚焦于分割两类实体：（i）因未出现在训练数据中而被MLLMs无法识别的新型实体；（ii）虽存在于模型知识内但需最新外部信息才能准确识别的新兴实体。为支持NEST研究，我们构建了基于自动化流水线生成新闻相关数据样本的NEST基准测试集。此外，我们提出ROSE：面向检索的分割增强框架，可无缝集成至任意基于MLLM的分割模型。ROSE包含四个核心组件：首先，引入互联网检索增强生成模块，利用用户提供的多模态输入检索实时网络信息；其次，文本提示增强器通过注入最新信息与丰富背景知识，提升模型对新兴实体的感知能力；再者，视觉提示增强器通过利用网络图像弥补MLLMs对新型实体缺乏接触的不足；最后，为保持效率，引入WebSense模块，根据用户输入智能决定是否调用检索机制。实验表明，ROSE在NEST基准上显著提升性能，相较强基线Gemini-2.0 Flash检索模型提升19.2 gIoU。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing MLLM-based segmentation models in handling novel or emerging entities due to static training data, this work introduces the Novel Emerging Segmentation Task (NEST) and proposes ROSE, a plug-and-play framework to enhance segmentation accuracy. ROSE integrates four components: an Internet Retrieval-Augmented Generation module for real-time web information retrieval, a Textual Prompt Enhancer for enriching context with up-to-date knowledge, a Visual Prompt Enhancer to compensate for lack of exposure to novel entities via internet images, and a WebSense module to intelligently trigger retrieval based on input. Experiments on the newly constructed NEST benchmark show ROSE significantly improves performance, achieving a 19.2 gIoU gain over a strong Gemini-2.0 Flash retrieval baseline.
为解决现有基于多模态大语言模型的分割方法在处理新出现或新兴实体时因训练数据静态而性能不足的问题，本文提出新型涌现分割任务（NEST）及ROSE框架。ROSE包含四个模块：互联网检索增强生成模块用于实时网络信息获取、文本提示增强器用于补充最新知识、视觉提示增强器通过网络图像辅助识别新实体、以及WebSense模块智能判断是否触发检索。在新构建的NEST基准测试中，ROSE相较Gemini-2.0 Flash基线模型提升19.2个gIoU。
SpatialEvo: Self-Evolving Spatial Intelligence via Deterministic Geometric Environments
Authors: Dinging Li, Yingxiu Zhao, Xinrui Cheng, Kangheng Lin, Hongbo Peng, Hongxing Li, Zixuan Wang, Yuhong Dai, Haodong Li, Jia Wang, Yukang Shi, Liang Zhao, Jianjian Sun, Zheng Ge, Xiangyu Zhang, Weiming Lu, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang, Yongliang Shen
First: 2026-04-15T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:59:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatial reasoning over three-dimensional scenes is a core capability for embodied intelligence, yet continuous model improvement remains bottlenecked by the cost of geometric annotation. The self-evolving paradigm offers a promising path, but its reliance on model consensus to construct pseudo-labels causes training to reinforce rather than correct the model's own geometric errors. We identify a property unique to 3D spatial reasoning that circumvents this limitation: ground truth is a deterministic consequence of the underlying geometry, computable exactly from point clouds and camera poses without any model involvement. Building on this insight, we present SpatialEvo, a self-evolving framework for 3D spatial reasoning, centered on the Deterministic Geometric Environment (DGE). The DGE formalizes 16 spatial reasoning task categories under explicit geometric validation rules and converts unannotated 3D scenes into zero-noise interactive oracles, replacing model consensus with objective physical feedback. A single shared-parameter policy co-evolves across questioner and solver roles under DGE constraints: the questioner generates physically valid spatial questions grounded in scene observations, while the solver derives precise answers against DGE-verified ground truth. A task-adaptive scheduler endogenously concentrates training on the model's weakest categories, producing a dynamic curriculum without manual design. Experiments across nine benchmarks demonstrate that SpatialEvo achieves the highest average score at both 3B and 7B scales, with consistent gains on spatial reasoning benchmarks and no degradation on general visual understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialEvo：通过确定性几何环境实现自演化的空间智能
三维场景中的空间推理是具身智能的核心能力，但几何标注成本使其模型持续改进受限。自演化范式具有前景，但其依赖模型共识构建伪标签，导致训练强化而非纠正模型自身的几何错误。我们发现3D空间推理具有独特属性：真实值是底层几何的确定性结果，可从点云和相机位姿精确计算，无需模型参与。基于此洞察，我们提出SpatialEvo，一种基于确定性几何环境（DGE）的3D空间推理自演化框架。DGE在明确的几何验证规则下形式化16类空间推理任务，并将未标注的3D场景转化为零噪声交互式预言机，用客观物理反馈替代模型共识。一个共享参数策略在DGE约束下协同演化于提问者与解题者角色：提问者基于场景观测生成物理有效的空间问题，解题者依据DGE验证的真实值推导精确答案。任务自适应调度器内生集中训练于模型最弱任务类别，实现无需人工设计的动态课程。九项基准实验表明，SpatialEvo在3B和7B规模下均取得最高平均得分，在空间推理基准上持续提升，且在通用视觉理解上无性能下降。
Summary / 总结
SpatialEvo addresses the challenge of improving 3D spatial reasoning models without costly geometric annotations by leveraging deterministic geometric environments. It introduces a self-evolving framework where a shared policy co-evolves as both questioner and solver under explicit geometric validation rules, generating physically grounded questions and solving them against exact ground truth derived from point clouds and camera poses. This eliminates reliance on model consensus and instead uses objective physical feedback. Experiments across nine benchmarks show SpatialEvo achieves the highest average performance at both 3B and 7B parameter scales, improving spatial reasoning without harming general visual understanding.
为降低3D空间推理中几何标注的成本，作者提出SpatialEvo框架，利用确定性几何环境规避模型共识的局限。该方法构建Deterministic Geometric Environment（DGE），将16类空间推理任务编码为明确的几何验证规则，使地面真值可直接从点云和相机位姿精确推导，无需模型参与。一个共享参数策略在DGE约束下同时扮演提问者与解题者角色，生成物理有效的空间问题并基于DGE验证的真值求解。任务自适应调度器动态聚焦模型薄弱类别，形成内生课程。在九个基准测试中，SpatialEvo在3B和7B参数规模下均取得最高平均得分，显著提升空间推理能力且未损害通用视觉理解表现。
From $P(y|x)$ to $P(y)$: Investigating Reinforcement Learning in Pre-train Space
Authors: Yuqiao Tan, Minzheng Wang, Bo Liu, Zichen Liu, Tian Liang, Shizhu He, Jun Zhao, Kang Liu
First: 2026-04-15T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trae1ounG/Pretrain_Space_RLVR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) significantly enhances LLM reasoning by optimizing the conditional distribution P(y|x), its potential is fundamentally bounded by the base model's existing output distribution. Optimizing the marginal distribution P(y) in the Pre-train Space addresses this bottleneck by encoding reasoning ability and preserving broad exploration capacity. Yet, conventional pre-training relies on static corpora for passive learning, leading to a distribution shift that hinders targeted reasoning enhancement. In this paper, we introduce PreRL (Pre-train Space RL), which applies reward-driven online updates directly to P(y). We theoretically and empirically validate the strong gradient alignment between log P(y) and log P(y|x), establishing PreRL as a viable surrogate for standard RL. Furthermore, we uncover a critical mechanism: Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) within PreRL serves as an exceptionally effective driver for reasoning. NSR-PreRL rapidly prunes incorrect reasoning spaces while stimulating endogenous reflective behaviors, increasing transition and reflection thoughts by 14.89x and 6.54x, respectively. Leveraging these insights, we propose Dual Space RL (DSRL), a Policy Reincarnation strategy that initializes models with NSR-PreRL to expand the reasoning horizon before transitioning to standard RL for fine-grained optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSRL consistently outperforms strong baselines, proving that pre-train space pruning effectively steers the policy toward a refined correct reasoning subspace.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is constrained by the base model's pre-existing output distribution, this paper proposes PreRL, a method that directly optimizes the marginal distribution P(y) in the pre-train space using reward-driven online updates. Theoretical and empirical analyses confirm strong gradient alignment between log P(y) and log P(y|x), validating PreRL as a viable RL surrogate. A key mechanism, Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR), is shown to effectively prune incorrect reasoning paths and boost reflective behaviors, increasing transition and reflection thoughts by 14.89x and 6.54x respectively. Building on this, Dual Space RL (DSRL) combines NSR-PreRL for broad reasoning horizon expansion with standard RL for fine-tuning, consistently outperforming baselines and demonstrating that pre-train space pruning effectively guides policies toward correct reasoning subspaces.
为突破RLVR仅优化P(y|x)且受限于基础模型P(y)分布的瓶颈，本文提出在预训练空间直接优化P(y)的PreRL方法，通过奖励驱动的在线更新实现。理论与实验表明，log P(y)与log P(y|x)梯度高度对齐，验证PreRL可作为标准RL的有效替代。方法引入负样本强化（NSR），高效剪枝错误推理路径并增强内生反思行为，使转换与反思思维分别提升14.89倍和6.54倍。基于此，提出双空间强化学习（DSRL），先用NSR-PreRL扩展推理边界，再用标准RL精细优化，实验证明其持续优于强基线，证实预训练空间剪枝能有效引导策略收敛至更优推理子空间。
Geometric Context Transformer for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Lin-Zhuo Chen, Jian Gao, Yihang Chen, Ka Leong Cheng, Yipengjing Sun, Liangxiao Hu, Nan Xue, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Yao Yao, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-04-15T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:58:13+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://technology.robbyant.com/lingbot-map Code: https://github.com/robbyant/lingbot-map
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Streaming 3D reconstruction aims to recover 3D information, such as camera poses and point clouds, from a video stream, which necessitates geometric accuracy, temporal consistency, and computational efficiency. Motivated by the principles of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), we introduce LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation model for reconstructing scenes from streaming data, built upon a geometric context transformer (GCT) architecture. A defining aspect of LingBot-Map lies in its carefully designed attention mechanism, which integrates an anchor context, a pose-reference window, and a trajectory memory to address coordinate grounding, dense geometric cues, and long-range drift correction, respectively. This design keeps the streaming state compact while retaining rich geometric context, enabling stable efficient inference at around 20 FPS on 518 x 378 resolution inputs over long sequences exceeding 10,000 frames. Extensive evaluations across a variety of benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared to both existing streaming and iterative optimization-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于流式3D重建的几何上下文变换器
流式3D重建旨在从视频流中恢复3D信息，如相机位姿和点云，这需要几何精度、时间一致性和计算效率。受同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）原理的启发，我们提出了LingBot-Map，这是一种基于几何上下文变换器（GCT）架构的前馈3D基础模型，用于从流式数据重建场景。LingBot-Map的核心在于其精心设计的注意力机制，该机制结合了锚点上下文、位姿参考窗口和轨迹记忆，分别解决坐标锚定、密集几何线索和长程漂移校正问题。该设计在保持流式状态紧凑的同时保留丰富的几何上下文，可在518x378分辨率输入、超过10,000帧的长序列上实现约20 FPS的稳定高效推理。在多个基准测试中的广泛评估表明，我们的方法相比现有流式和迭代优化方法均取得更优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for geometric accuracy, temporal consistency, and efficiency in streaming 3D reconstruction, this work introduces LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation model based on a geometric context transformer. Its attention mechanism combines anchor context, pose-reference window, and trajectory memory to handle coordinate grounding, dense geometric cues, and long-range drift correction, respectively, while maintaining a compact streaming state. Experimental results show it achieves stable inference at 20 FPS on 518x378 inputs over sequences exceeding 10,000 frames, outperforming both streaming and iterative optimization-based methods across multiple benchmarks.
为实现流式3D重建所需的几何精度、时间一致性与计算效率，本文提出LingBot-Map，一种基于几何上下文变换器的前馈3D基础模型。其注意力机制通过锚点上下文、位姿参考窗口和轨迹记忆分别解决坐标锚定、密集几何线索与长程漂移校正问题，实现紧凑状态表示的同时保留丰富几何信息。在多个基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有流式及迭代优化方法，能在518x378分辨率输入下稳定运行于约20帧每秒，支持超过10,000帧的长序列重建。
LongCoT: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Chain-of-Thought Reasoning
Authors: Sumeet Ramesh Motwani, Daniel Nichols, Charles London, Peggy Li, Fabio Pizzati, Acer Blake, Hasan Hammoud, Tavish McDonald, Akshat Naik, Alesia Ivanova, Vignesh Baskaran, Ivan Laptev, Ruben Glatt, Tal Ben-Nun, Philip Torr, Natasha Jaques, Ameya Prabhu, Brian Bartoldson, Bhavya Kailkhura, Christian Schroeder de Witt
First: 2026-04-15T17:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:58:05+00:00
Comments: Long-Horizon Reasoning Benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As language models are increasingly deployed for complex autonomous tasks, their ability to reason accurately over longer horizons becomes critical. An essential component of this ability is planning and managing a long, complex chain-of-thought (CoT). We introduce LongCoT, a scalable benchmark of 2,500 expert-designed problems spanning chemistry, mathematics, computer science, chess, and logic to isolate and directly measure the long-horizon CoT reasoning capabilities of frontier models. Problems consist of a short input with a verifiable answer; solving them requires navigating a graph of interdependent steps that span tens to hundreds of thousands of reasoning tokens. Each local step is individually tractable for frontier models, so failures reflect long-horizon reasoning limitations. At release, the best models achieve <10% accuracy (GPT 5.2: 9.8%; Gemini 3 Pro: 6.1%) on LongCoT, revealing a substantial gap in current capabilities. Overall, LongCoT provides a rigorous measure of long-horizon reasoning, tracking the ability of frontier models to reason reliably over extended periods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongCoT：长时程思维链推理基准
随着语言模型被越来越多地用于复杂的自主任务，其在更长时程内准确推理的能力变得至关重要。这一能力的核心组成部分是规划和管理复杂的长思维链（CoT）。我们引入LongCoT，这是一个包含2500个专家设计问题的可扩展基准，涵盖化学、数学、计算机科学、国际象棋和逻辑等领域，旨在隔离并直接衡量前沿模型的长时程CoT推理能力。这些问题包含简短输入和可验证答案，解决它们需要在包含数十万至数百万推理标记的相互依赖步骤图中进行导航。每个局部步骤对前沿模型而言都是可处理的，因此失败反映了长时程推理的局限性。发布时，最佳模型在LongCoT上的准确率低于10%（GPT 5.2：9.8%；Gemini 3 Pro：6.1%），揭示了当前能力的显著差距。总体而言，LongCoT为长时程推理提供了严格的衡量标准，追踪前沿模型在长时间段内可靠推理的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for language models to handle complex, long-term reasoning in autonomous tasks, LongCoT introduces a scalable benchmark of 2,500 expert-designed problems across multiple domains, requiring models to navigate interdependent reasoning steps spanning tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. Each problem has a short input and verifiable answer, with individual steps being solvable by frontier models, so failures expose long-horizon reasoning weaknesses. Experimental results show that even top models achieve less than 10% accuracy, with GPT 5.2 at 9.8% and Gemini 3 Pro at 6.1%, highlighting a significant gap in current long-horizon reasoning capabilities.
为应对语言模型在复杂自主任务中对长时推理能力的需求，LongCoT 构建了一个包含2500道专家设计问题的可扩展基准，覆盖化学、数学、计算机科学、国际象棋和逻辑等多个领域，每个问题要求跨越数万至数十万推理令牌的长链思维。该基准通过将问题设计为可验证答案且步骤相互依赖，以隔离长时推理能力。实验结果显示，即使顶尖模型如GPT 5.2和Gemini 3 Pro准确率也低于10%，揭示当前模型在长时可靠推理方面存在显著差距。
From Feelings to Metrics: Understanding and Formalizing How Users Vibe-Test LLMs
Authors: Itay Itzhak, Eliya Habba, Gabriel Stanovsky, Yonatan Belinkov
First: 2026-04-15T17:57:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:57:08+00:00
Comments: TLDR: Under review. 42 pages, 18 figures. Code and data at https://itay1itzhak.github.io/vibe-testing-llms Subjects: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Evaluating LLMs is challenging, as benchmark scores often fail to capture models' real-world usefulness. Instead, users often rely on ``vibe-testing'': informal experience-based evaluation, such as comparing models on coding tasks related to their own workflow. While prevalent, vibe-testing is often too ad hoc and unstructured to analyze or reproduce at scale. In this work, we study how vibe-testing works in practice and then formalize it to support systematic analysis. We first analyze two empirical resources: (1) a survey of user evaluation practices, and (2) a collection of in-the-wild model comparison reports from blogs and social media. Based on these resources, we formalize vibe-testing as a two-part process: users personalize both what they test and how they judge responses. We then introduce a proof-of-concept evaluation pipeline that follows this formulation by generating personalized prompts and comparing model outputs using user-aware subjective criteria. In experiments on coding benchmarks, we find that combining personalized prompts and user-aware evaluation can change which model is preferred, reflecting the role of vibe-testing in practice. These findings suggest that formalized vibe-testing can serve as a useful approach for bridging benchmark scores and real-world experience.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从感受转向指标：理解并形式化用户如何通过“氛围测试”评估大语言模型
评估大语言模型具有挑战性，因为基准分数往往无法捕捉模型在现实世界中的实用性。相反，用户通常依赖“氛围测试”——一种基于经验的非正式评估，例如在与自身工作流程相关的编码任务中比较模型表现。尽管这种测试广泛存在，但其往往过于随意和非结构化，难以规模化分析或复现。本研究探讨了“氛围测试”在实践中的运作方式，并将其形式化以支持系统性分析。我们首先分析了两项实证资源：（1）用户评估实践的调查；（2）来自博客和社交媒体的野外模型比较报告集合。基于这些资源，我们将“氛围测试”形式化为一个两阶段过程：用户既个性化测试内容，也个性化评判标准。随后，我们引入一个概念验证评估流程，该流程遵循此形式化方法，通过生成个性化提示并使用用户感知的主观标准比较模型输出。在编码基准实验中，我们发现结合个性化提示与用户感知评估可改变模型偏好，反映了“氛围测试”在实践中的作用。这些发现表明，形式化的“氛围测试”可作为连接基准分数与现实体验的有效方法。

This paper addresses the gap between standardized LLM benchmarks and users' real-world evaluation practices, which often rely on informal 'vibe-testing'—personalized, experience-based comparisons. The authors analyze user surveys and in-the-wild model comparison reports to formalize vibe-testing as a two-part process: personalizing both test prompts and subjective judgment criteria. They then prototype an evaluation pipeline that generates personalized prompts and compares model outputs using user-defined metrics. Experiments on coding benchmarks show that this approach can alter model preference compared to standard benchmarks, demonstrating that vibe-testing significantly influences perceived model utility and suggesting its potential as a structured method to bridge performance metrics with user experience.

Exact Structural Abstraction and Tractability Limits
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:51:55+00:00
Comments: 42 pages. 3 tables. Lean 4 formalization available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Any rigorously specified problem determines an admissible-output relation $R$, and the only state distinctions that matter are the classes $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$. Every exact correctness claim reduces to the same quotient-recovery problem, and the no-go concerns tractability of the underlying problem, not of its presentation. Exact means agreement with $R$, not zero-error determinism or absence of approximation/randomization in the specification. The exact-semantics quotient theorem identifies admissible-output equivalence as the canonical object recovered by exact relevance certification. Decision, search, approximation, statistical, randomized, horizon, and distributional guarantees instantiate it. Tractable families have a finite primitive basis, but optimizer-quotient realizability is maximal, so quotient shape cannot characterize the frontier. We prove a meta-impossibility theorem for efficiently checkable structural predicates invariant under theorem-forced closure laws of exact certification. Zero-distortion summaries, quotient entropy bounds, and support counting explain them. Same-orbit disagreements across four obstruction families, via action-independent pair-targeted affine witnesses, force contradiction. Consequently no correct problem-tractability classifier on a closure-closed domain yields an exact characterization over these families. Restricting to a closure-closed subdomain helps only by removing orbit gaps. Uniform strict-gap control preserves the full optimizer quotient, while arbitrarily small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency. Closure-orbit agreement is forced by correctness, and the same compute-cost barrier extends to optimizer computation, payload/search, and theorem-backed external or transported outputs. The obstruction therefore appears at the level of correctness itself, not any particular output formalism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精确结构抽象与可处理性极限
任何严格定义的问题都确定一个可接受输出关系 $R$，且仅需关注满足 $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$ 的状态类。所有精确正确性断言均归结为同一商空间恢复问题，而不可行性关注的是底层问题本身，而非其表述形式。精确指与 $R$ 一致，而非零错误确定性或规范中无近似/随机化。精确语义商定理指出，可接受输出等价类是精确相关性认证所恢复的典范对象。决策、搜索、近似、统计、随机化、有限视图及分布保证均体现于此。可处理族具有有限原始基，但优化器商的实现性为最大，因此商结构无法刻画前沿。我们证明了关于高效可验证结构谓词的元不可能定理，该谓词在精确认证的定理强制闭包律下保持不变。零失真摘要、商熵界及支撑计数解释了这些谓词。通过作用无关的成对目标仿射见证，四个障碍族中的不同轨道分歧导致矛盾。因此，在闭包封闭域上，不存在能对这些族给出精确刻画的正确问题可处理性分类器。仅通过限制到闭包封闭子域，可消除轨道间隙。统一严格间隙控制可保持完整优化器商，而任意微小扰动可能翻转相关性与充分性。闭包轨道一致性由正确性强制，且相同的计算成本障碍也扩展至优化器计算、负载/搜索及定理支持的外部或传递输出。因此，障碍出现在正确性本身层面，而非任何特定输出形式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the inherent limits of tractability in problems with exact semantics, the paper introduces a framework where correctness is defined by admissible-output equivalence classes under a relation R. It establishes that tractability concerns the underlying problem structure, not its presentation, and proves a meta-impossibility result: no efficient classifier can exactly characterize tractability across closure-closed domains due to structural obstructions arising from action-independent affine witnesses. These obstructions force contradictions when attempting to preserve correctness while maintaining computational efficiency, and even small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency. The paper concludes that the barrier lies at the level of correctness itself, not output formalisms, and that uniform strict-gap control preserves the full optimizer quotient while closure-orbit agreement is enforced by correctness.
为理解具有精确语义问题的内在可解性限制，本文提出一个框架，其中正确性通过关系R下的可接受输出等价类定义。方法聚焦于商恢复问题，表明所有精确正确性断言均可归约为此类恢复，并证明一个元不可能定理：在闭包闭域上，不存在高效分类器能精确刻画可解性，因定理强制闭包律导致结构障碍。通过仿射见证分析表明，即使微小扰动也可能翻转相关性与充分性，且闭包轨道一致性是正确性的必要条件，意味着障碍存在于正确性本身，而非特定输出形式。
Don't Let the Video Speak: Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization for Audio-Visual Language Models
Authors: Ami Baid, Zihui Xue, Kristen Grauman
First: 2026-04-15T17:51:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:51:28+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/acpo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While Audio-Visual Language Models (AVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress over recent years, their reliability is bottlenecked by cross-modal hallucination. A particularly pervasive manifestation is video-driven audio hallucination: models routinely exploit visual shortcuts to hallucinate expected sounds, discarding true auditory evidence. To counteract this deeply ingrained visual dominance, we propose Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization (ACPO). This dual-axis preference learning framework introduces an output-contrastive objective to penalize visual descriptions masquerading as audio facts, alongside an input-contrastive objective that swaps audio tracks to explicitly penalize generation invariant to the true auditory signal. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACPO establishes highly faithful audio grounding and mitigates audio hallucination without compromising overarching multimodal capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：别让视频说话：面向视听语言模型的音频对比偏好优化
尽管视听语言模型（AVLMs）近年来取得了显著进展，但其可靠性受限于跨模态幻觉。一种尤为普遍的表现是视频驱动的音频幻觉：模型常利用视觉捷径生成预期声音，忽略真实听觉证据。为对抗这种根深蒂固的视觉主导，我们提出音频对比偏好优化（ACPO）。该双轴偏好学习框架引入输出对比目标，惩罚伪装成音频事实的视觉描述；同时引入输入对比目标，交换音频轨道以明确惩罚与真实听觉信号无关的生成。大量实验表明，ACPO可建立高度忠实的音频 grounding 并缓解音频幻觉，同时不损害整体多模态能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of Audio-Visual Language Models to hallucinate audio based on visual cues rather than true auditory input, the authors introduce Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization (ACPO), a dual-axis preference learning framework that combines output-contrastive and input-contrastive objectives to penalize visually biased audio generation and audio-agnostic outputs. Experiments show ACPO significantly improves audio grounding fidelity and reduces video-driven audio hallucinations while preserving the model’s overall multimodal performance.
针对音频视觉语言模型中普遍存在的视频驱动音频幻觉问题——即模型常依赖视觉线索虚构音频描述而非真实听觉证据——作者提出音频对比偏好优化（ACPO）方法。该方法采用双轴偏好学习框架：输出对比损失用于惩罚伪装成音频事实的视觉描述，输入对比损失通过交换音频轨道以抑制对真实声音无反应的生成。实验表明，ACPO显著提升音频接地的准确性，有效减少幻觉，同时保持整体多模态能力。
Rhetorical Questions in LLM Representations: A Linear Probing Study
Authors: Louie Hong Yao, Vishesh Anand, Yuan Zhuang, Tianyu Jiang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-15T17:50:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:50:56+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 15 figures, accepted to ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rhetorical questions are asked not to seek information but to persuade or signal stance. How large language models internally represent them remains unclear. We analyze rhetorical questions in LLM representations using linear probes on two social-media datasets with different discourse contexts, and find that rhetorical signals emerge early and are most stably captured by last-token representations. Rhetorical questions are linearly separable from information-seeking questions within datasets, and remain detectable under cross-dataset transfer, reaching AUROC around 0.7-0.8. However, we demonstrate that transferability does not simply imply a shared representation. Probes trained on different datasets produce different rankings when applied to the same target corpus, with overlap among the top-ranked instances often below 0.2. Qualitative analysis shows that these divergences correspond to distinct rhetorical phenomena: some probes capture discourse-level rhetorical stance embedded in extended argumentation, while others emphasize localized, syntax-driven interrogative acts. Together, these findings suggest that rhetorical questions in LLM representations are encoded by multiple linear directions emphasizing different cues, rather than a single shared direction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中修辞性疑问句的表示：线性探测研究
修辞性疑问句并非为了获取信息，而是为了说服或表达立场。大语言模型内部如何表示这类疑问句尚不明确。我们通过对两个具有不同话语语境的社交媒体数据集进行线性探测分析，发现修辞性信号在模型早期即出现，且最稳定地体现在最后一个词的表示中。在各自数据集中，修辞性疑问句与信息获取型疑问句可线性分离，并在跨数据集迁移中仍可检测，AUROC值约为0.7-0.8。然而，我们证明迁移性并不简单意味着共享表示。在不同数据集上训练的探测器应用于同一目标语料时，会产生不同的排序，且排名靠前的实例重叠度通常低于0.2。定性分析表明，这些差异对应于不同的修辞性现象：某些探测器捕捉的是嵌入在扩展论证中的话语层面修辞性立场，而另一些则强调局部、语法驱动的疑问行为。综合来看，这些发现表明，大语言模型中修辞性疑问句的表示由多个强调不同线索的线性方向编码，而非单一共享方向。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models represent rhetorical questions—utterances intended to persuade rather than elicit information—by applying linear probes to two social-media datasets with distinct discourse contexts. The researchers find that rhetorical signals are detectable early in model representations and are most consistently captured by last-token embeddings. Rhetorical questions are linearly separable from information-seeking questions within each dataset, with cross-dataset transfer achieving AUROC scores of 0.7–0.8, yet probes trained on different datasets produce divergent rankings of top instances, with low overlap (often below 0.2). Qualitative analysis reveals that these differences reflect distinct rhetorical phenomena: some probes capture discourse-level stance in extended argumentation, while others focus on syntax-driven interrogative structures. These results indicate that rhetorical questions are encoded by multiple linear directions in LLMs, each emphasizing different cues, rather than a single shared representation.
本研究通过线性探测方法分析大语言模型如何表征修辞疑问句（用于说服或表达立场而非获取信息），使用两个语境不同的社交媒体数据集。结果表明，修辞信号在模型早期即被捕捉，且在最后一个词表示中最为稳定；修辞疑问句在各自数据集中可与信息性疑问句线性分离，跨数据集迁移时AUROC达0.7–0.8。然而，不同数据集训练的探测器在相同目标语料上排名差异显著，顶级实例重合度常低于0.2，表明修辞疑问句在模型中由多个不同线性方向编码，分别侧重语用层面的立场或句法层面的疑问结构，而非单一共享表征。
HiVLA: A Visual-Grounded-Centric Hierarchical Embodied Manipulation System
Authors: Tianshuo Yang, Guanyu Chen, Yutian Chen, Zhixuan Liang, Yitian Liu, Zanxin Chen, Chunpu Xu, Haotian Liang, Jiangmiao Pang, Yao Mu, Ping Luo
First: 2026-04-15T17:50:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:50:07+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://tianshuoy.github.io/HiVLA-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While end-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, fine-tuning them on narrow control data often compromises the profound reasoning capabilities inherited from their base Vision-Language Models (VLMs). To resolve this fundamental trade-off, we propose HiVLA, a visual-grounded-centric hierarchical framework that explicitly decouples high-level semantic planning from low-level motor control. In high-level part, a VLM planner first performs task decomposition and visual grounding to generate structured plans, comprising a subtask instruction and a precise target bounding box. Then, to translate this plan into physical actions, we introduce a flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) action expert in low-level part equipped with a novel cascaded cross-attention mechanism. This design sequentially fuses global context, high-resolution object-centric crops and skill semantics, enabling the DiT to focus purely on robust execution. Our decoupled architecture preserves the VLM's zero-shot reasoning while allowing independent improvement of both components. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that HiVLA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art end-to-end baselines, particularly excelling in long-horizon skill composition and the fine-grained manipulation of small objects in cluttered scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiVLA：一种以视觉锚定为中心的层次化具身操作系统
尽管端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为机器人操作提供了有前景的范式，但其在狭窄控制数据上的微调常会损害其基础视觉-语言模型（VLM）所继承的深层推理能力。为解决这一根本性权衡，我们提出HiVLA，一种以视觉锚定为中心的层次化框架，明确将高层语义规划与低层运动控制解耦。在高层部分，VLM规划器首先进行任务分解与视觉锚定，生成包含子任务指令和精确目标边界框的结构化计划；随后，为将该计划转化为物理动作，我们在低层部分引入配备新颖级联交叉注意力机制的流匹配扩散变换器（DiT）动作专家。该设计依次融合全局上下文、高分辨率对象中心裁剪图与技能语义，使DiT能纯粹专注于鲁棒执行。我们的解耦架构保留了VLM的零样本推理能力，同时允许各组件独立优化。在仿真与真实世界中的大量实验表明，HiVLA显著优于当前最先进的端到端基线方法，尤其在长时程技能组合及杂乱场景中小物体的精细操作方面表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between fine-tuning end-to-end Vision-Language-Action models for robotic manipulation and preserving their inherited reasoning capabilities, the authors propose HiVLA, a hierarchical system that decouples high-level semantic planning from low-level motor control. The high-level component uses a Vision-Language Model to decompose tasks and ground them visually, generating structured subtask instructions and target bounding boxes. The low-level component employs a Diffusion Transformer with a cascaded cross-attention mechanism to translate these plans into physical actions, fusing global context, object-centric crops, and skill semantics for robust execution. Experiments in simulation and real-world settings show HiVLA significantly outperforms existing end-to-end baselines, especially in long-horizon skill composition and fine-grained manipulation of small objects in cluttered environments.
为解决视觉语言模型推理能力与窄域控制数据微调之间的矛盾，HiVLA提出一种视觉中心的分层框架，将高层语义规划与底层运动控制解耦。高层由视觉语言模型生成带视觉定位的结构化任务分解计划，底层则通过带级联交叉注意力机制的扩散Transformer动作专家，融合全局上下文、高分辨率目标区域与技能语义，实现精准物理执行。实验表明，HiVLA在长时序任务和杂乱场景中小物体精细操作中显著优于现有端到端方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性与组合能力。
Target-Bench: Can Video World Models Achieve Mapless Path Planning with Semantic Targets?
Authors: Dingrui Wang, Zhihao Liang, Hongyuan Ye, Zhexiao Sun, Zhaowei Lu, Yuchen Zhang, Yuyu Zhao, Yuan Gao, Marvin Seegert, Finn Schäfer, Haotong Qin, Wei Li, Luigi Palmieri, Felix Jahncke, Mattia Piccinini, Johannes Betz
First: 2025-11-21T21:36:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:49:12+00:00
Comments: 19 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While recent video world models can generate highly realistic videos, their ability to perform semantic reasoning and planning remains unclear and unquantified. We introduce Target-Bench, the first benchmark that enables comprehensive evaluation of video world models' semantic reasoning, spatial estimation, and planning capabilities. Target-Bench provides 450 robot-collected scenarios spanning 47 semantic categories, with SLAM-based trajectories serving as motion tendency references. Our benchmark reconstructs motion from generated videos with a metric scale recovery mechanism, enabling the evaluation of planning performance with five complementary metrics that focus on target-approaching capability and directional consistency. Our evaluation result shows that the best off-the-shelf model achieves only a 0.341 overall score, revealing a significant gap between realistic visual generation and semantic reasoning in current video world models. Furthermore, we demonstrate that fine-tuning process on a relatively small real-world robot dataset can significantly improve task-level planning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Target-Bench：视频世界模型能否在无地图情况下通过语义目标实现路径规划？
尽管近期视频世界模型能够生成高度逼真的视频，但其语义推理与规划能力仍不明确且缺乏量化评估。我们提出了Target-Bench，这是首个全面评估视频世界模型语义推理、空间估计与规划能力的基准。Target-Bench提供450个机器人采集的场景，涵盖47个语义类别，以基于SLAM的轨迹作为运动趋势参考。我们的基准通过度量尺度恢复机制从生成视频中重建运动，从而使用五个互补指标评估规划性能，重点关注目标接近能力和方向一致性。评估结果显示，当前最优的现成模型仅获得0.341的整体得分，揭示了当前视频世界模型在逼真视觉生成与语义推理之间存在显著差距。此外，我们证明在相对较小的真实机器人数据集上进行微调可显著提升任务级规划性能。

The benchmark reconstructs motion from generated videos using a metric scale recovery mechanism and evaluates planning performance via five metrics focused on target approach and directional consistency, with SLAM trajectories serving as ground truth references. 为评估视频世界模型在语义推理与路径规划能力方面的表现，本文提出Target-Bench，首个基于真实机器人采集场景的评估基准。该方法通过SLAM轨迹与尺度恢复机制从生成视频中重建运动，并设计五项指标衡量目标接近与方向一致性。实验表明，当前最优现成模型整体得分仅0.341，揭示视觉真实感与语义规划能力之间存在显著差距，而仅在小规模机器人数据集上微调即可显著提升任务级规划表现。
Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models: Phenomenology, Mechanism, and Recipe
Authors: Yaxuan Li, Yuxin Zuo, Bingxiang He, Jinqian Zhang, Chaojun Xiao, Cheng Qian, Tianyu Yu, Huan-ang Gao, Wenkai Yang, Zhiyuan Liu, Ning Ding
First: 2026-04-14T17:54:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:48:51+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 23 figures. Code: https://github.com/thunlp/OPD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a core technique in the post-training of large language models, yet its training dynamics remain poorly understood. This paper provides a systematic investigation of OPD dynamics and mechanisms. We first identify that two conditions govern whether OPD succeeds or fails: (i) the student and teacher should share compatible thinking patterns; and (ii) even with consistent thinking patterns and higher scores, the teacher must offer genuinely new capabilities beyond what the student has seen during training. We validate these findings through weak-to-strong reverse distillation, showing that same-family 1.5B and 7B teachers are distributionally indistinguishable from the student's perspective. Probing into the token-level mechanism, we show that successful OPD is characterized by progressive alignment on high-probability tokens at student-visited states, a small shared token set that concentrates most of the probability mass (97%-99%). We further propose two practical strategies to recover failing OPD: off-policy cold start and teacher-aligned prompt selection. Finally, we show that OPD's apparent free lunch of dense token-level reward comes at a cost, raising the question of whether OPD can scale to long-horizon distillation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考大语言模型的在线策略蒸馏：现象、机制与方法
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）已成为大语言模型后训练的核心技术，但其训练动态仍不甚明了。本文系统研究了OPD的动态与机制。我们首先发现，OPD成败取决于两个条件：（i）学生与教师应具备兼容的思维模式；（ii）即使思维模式一致且教师得分更高，教师也必须提供学生训练期间未见过的真正新能力。我们通过弱到强的反向蒸馏验证了这些发现，表明同家族的1.5B和7B教师在学生视角下分布上不可区分。在token级机制上，我们发现成功OPD表现为在学生访问状态中，高概率token逐步对齐，且共享token集集中了大部分概率质量（97%-99%）。我们进一步提出两种实用策略以恢复失败的OPD：离策略冷启动与教师对齐提示选择。最后，我们指出OPD看似免费的密集token级奖励存在代价，引发对其能否扩展至长时蒸馏的疑问。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of on-policy distillation (OPD) dynamics in large language models, this paper systematically investigates its success conditions and mechanisms. It identifies two key requirements: compatible thinking patterns between student and teacher, and the teacher must offer genuinely novel capabilities beyond the student’s training experience. Experimental results using weak-to-strong reverse distillation confirm that same-family teachers are indistinguishable from the student’s perspective, while token-level analysis reveals successful OPD aligns on high-probability tokens at visited states, concentrated in a small shared token set (97%-99%). The paper proposes off-policy cold start and teacher-aligned prompt selection to fix failing OPD, and concludes that while OPD appears to offer a dense token-level reward 'free lunch,' it may not scale to long-horizon distillation.
为了解决大语言模型中在线策略蒸馏（OPD）训练机制不清晰的问题，本文系统研究了OPD成功或失败的条件，发现学生与教师需具备兼容的思维模式，且教师必须提供学生训练中未见过的真正新能力。通过反向蒸馏实验验证，即使同家族模型在学生视角下也难以区分。在词元层面，成功蒸馏表现为在学生访问状态中高概率词元的逐步对齐，集中在仅占97–99%概率质量的小词元集合中。论文提出两种实用策略——离策略冷启动与教师对齐提示选择——以修复失败蒸馏，并指出OPD虽看似提供密集词元级奖励优势，但其在长时蒸馏中可能难以扩展。
Why and When Visual Token Pruning Fails? A Study on Relevant Visual Information Shift in MLLMs Decoding
Authors: Jiwan Kim, Kibum Kim, Wonjoong Kim, Byung-Kwan Lee, Chanyoung Park
First: 2026-04-14T06:48:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:43:53+00:00
Comments: Preprint, Project : https://ptkjw1997.github.io/DSTP-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently, visual token pruning has been studied to handle the vast number of visual tokens in Multimodal Large Language Models. However, we observe that while existing pruning methods perform reliably on simple visual understanding, they struggle to effectively generalize to complex visual reasoning tasks, a critical gap underexplored in previous studies. Through a systematic analysis, we identify Relevant Visual Information Shift (RVIS) during decoding as the primary failure driver. To address this, we propose Decoding-stage Shift-aware Token Pruning (DSTP), a training-free add-on framework that enables existing pruning methods to align visual tokens with shifting reasoning requirements during the decoding stage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSTP significantly mitigates performance degradation of pruning methods in complex reasoning tasks, while consistently yielding performance gains even across visual understanding benchmarks. Furthermore, DSTP demonstrates effectiveness across diverse state-of-the-art architectures, highlighting its generalizability and efficiency with minimal computational overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉标记剪枝为何及何时失效？一项关于多模态大语言模型解码过程中相关视觉信息迁移的研究
近期研究探讨了视觉标记剪枝以应对多模态大语言模型中庞大的视觉标记数量。然而，我们观察到，尽管现有剪枝方法在简单视觉理解任务上表现可靠，但在复杂视觉推理任务上难以有效泛化，这一关键差距在以往研究中尚未充分探讨。通过系统分析，我们识别出解码过程中的相关视觉信息迁移（RVIS）是主要失效原因。为此，我们提出了解码阶段感知迁移的标记剪枝（DSTP），这是一种无需训练的附加框架，使现有剪枝方法能够在解码阶段根据变化的推理需求对视觉标记进行对齐。大量实验表明，DSTP显著缓解了剪枝方法在复杂推理任务中的性能下降，同时在视觉理解基准测试中持续带来性能提升。此外，DSTP在多种前沿架构中均表现出有效性，凸显其通用性与高效性，且计算开销极小。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that visual token pruning methods fail to generalize to complex visual reasoning despite working well on simple tasks, this study identifies Relevant Visual Information Shift (RVIS) during decoding as the core cause. To resolve this, the authors propose DSTP, a training-free framework that adapts pruning by aligning visual tokens with evolving reasoning demands during decoding. Experiments show DSTP significantly reduces performance drops in complex reasoning tasks and consistently improves results across visual understanding benchmarks, while being compatible with multiple state-of-the-art architectures with minimal computational cost.
鉴于现有视觉标记剪枝方法在简单视觉理解任务上表现良好，但在复杂视觉推理任务中泛化能力不足，本研究将解码过程中的相关视觉信息迁移（RVIS）认定为失败主因。为此，作者提出DSTP框架，一种无需训练即可在解码阶段动态调整视觉标记与推理需求对齐的方法。实验表明，DSTP能显著缓解复杂推理任务中的性能下降，并在多个视觉理解基准上持续提升表现，且适用于多种主流模型架构，计算开销极小。
Correct Prediction, Wrong Steps? Consensus Reasoning Knowledge Graph for Robust Chain-of-Thought Synthesis
Authors: Zipeng Ling, Shuliang Liu, Shenghong Fu, Yuehao Tang, Seonil Son, Yao Wan, Xuming Hu
First: 2026-04-15T17:43:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:43:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM reasoning traces suffer from complex flaws -- *Step Internal Flaws* (logical errors, hallucinations, etc.) and *Step-wise Flaws* (overthinking, underthinking), which vary by sample. A natural approach would be to provide ground-truth labels to guide LLMs' reasoning. Contrary to intuition, we show that this yields no improvement in reasoning ability. We then propose CRAFT, a unified framework that mitigates both types of Step flaws, which builds a Reasoning Knowledge Graph (RKG) based on the consensus parts of multiple candidate traces, and synthesizes a high-quality trace through topological generation. Our approach improves label-prediction accuracy by 10+% on average, and consistently outperforms all baselines across both logical and mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Further, detailed benchmark evaluation proves that our method also improves the quality of LLMs' reasoning traces in multiple dimensions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正确预测，错误步骤？用于鲁棒思维链合成的共识推理知识图谱
大语言模型的推理轨迹存在复杂缺陷——*步骤内部缺陷*（逻辑错误、幻觉等）和*步骤间缺陷*（过度思考、思考不足），且样本间差异显著。直觉上，提供真实标签引导推理应能提升效果，但实验证明无改善。我们提出CRAFT框架，统一缓解两类步骤缺陷：基于多候选轨迹的共识部分构建推理知识图谱（RKG），并通过拓扑生成合成高质量轨迹。该方法平均提升标签预测准确率10%以上，在逻辑与数学推理基准上持续优于所有基线。进一步评估表明，该方法在多个维度提升大语言模型推理轨迹质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of step-level reasoning flaws in LLM outputs, such as internal logical errors and misaligned step-wise thinking, the paper challenges the assumption that providing ground-truth labels improves reasoning. Instead, it introduces CRAFT, a framework that constructs a Reasoning Knowledge Graph from consensus elements across multiple candidate traces, then synthesizes high-quality reasoning paths via topological generation. Experiments show CRAFT boosts label-prediction accuracy by 10% on average and consistently outperforms baselines across logical and mathematical benchmarks, while also enhancing the overall quality of reasoning traces.
针对大语言模型推理轨迹中普遍存在的步骤级错误，该论文质疑提供真实标签能提升推理能力的假设。提出CRAFT框架，通过聚合多个候选轨迹中的共识部分构建推理知识图谱，并利用拓扑生成合成高质量推理路径。实验表明，CRAFT平均提升标签预测准确率10%，在逻辑与数学推理基准上持续优于所有基线方法，同时显著提升生成推理轨迹的多维度质量。
Strategic Response of News Publishers to Generative AI
Authors: Hangcheng Zhao, Ron Berman
First: 2025-12-31T16:54:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:42:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI can adversely impact news publishers by lowering consumer demand. It can also reduce demand for newsroom employees, and increase the creation of news "slop." However, it can also form a source of traffic referrals and an information-discovery channel that increases demand. We use high-frequency granular data to analyze the strategic response of news publishers to the introduction of Generative AI. Many publishers strategically blocked LLM access to their websites using the robots.txt file standard. Using a difference-in-differences approach, we find that large publishers who block GenAI bots experience reduced website traffic compared to not blocking. In addition, we find that large publishers shift toward richer content that is harder for LLMs to replicate, without increasing text volume. Finally, we find that the share of new editorial and content-production job postings rises over time. Together, these findings illustrate the levers that publishers choose to use to strategically respond to competitive Generative AI threats, and their consequences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：新闻出版商对生成式人工智能的战略应对
生成式人工智能可能通过降低消费者需求对新闻出版商产生负面影响，也可能减少对新闻编辑室员工的需求，并增加低质新闻内容的产出。然而，它也可作为流量导流来源和信息发现渠道，从而提升需求。我们利用高频细粒度数据，分析新闻出版商对生成式人工智能引入的战略应对。许多出版商通过robots.txt标准策略性地阻止大型语言模型访问其网站。采用差异中的差异方法，我们发现，阻止生成式AI爬虫的大规模出版商相比未阻止者，其网站流量减少。此外，我们发现大型出版商转向更难被大型语言模型复制的丰富内容，而无需增加文本量。最后，我们发现新编采和内容生产职位招聘比例随时间上升。这些发现共同揭示了出版商为应对竞争性生成式人工智能威胁所选择的战略工具及其后果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the dual threats and opportunities posed by generative AI—reduced demand and workforce displacement alongside potential traffic and discovery benefits—this study examines how news publishers strategically respond. Using high-frequency data and a difference-in-differences approach, researchers find that large publishers often block LLM access via robots.txt, which reduces website traffic. These publishers also shift toward more complex, harder-to-replicate content without increasing text volume, and concurrently see a rise in new editorial job postings over time, indicating adaptation to AI competition.
鉴于生成式AI既可能降低新闻需求与新闻从业者需求，又可能带来流量和信息发现的机遇，本研究分析了新闻出版商的战略应对。基于高频数据与差分法，研究发现大型出版商常通过robots.txt阻止AI机器人访问，但此举反而导致其网站流量下降；同时，他们转向更难被AI复制的深度内容，且不增加文本量；此外，新编辑与内容岗位招聘比例随时间上升，表明出版商正通过调整内容策略与人力结构应对AI竞争压力。
Complex Interpolation of Matrices with an application to Multi-Manifold Learning
Authors: Adi Arbel, Stefan Steinerberger, Ronen Talmon
First: 2026-04-15T17:40:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:40:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given two symmetric positive-definite matrices $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$, we study the spectral properties of the interpolation $A^{1-x} B^x$ for $0 \leq x \leq 1$. The presence of `common structures' in $A$ and $B$, eigenvectors pointing in a similar direction, can be investigated using this interpolation perspective. Generically, exact log-linearity of the operator norm $\|A^{1-x} B^x\|$ is equivalent to the existence of a shared eigenvector in the original matrices; stability bounds show that approximate log-linearity forces principal singular vectors to align with leading eigenvectors of both matrices. These results give rise to and provide theoretical justification for a multi-manifold learning framework that identifies common and distinct latent structures in multiview data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：矩阵的复杂插值及其在多流形学习中的应用
给定两个对称正定矩阵 $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$，我们研究插值 $A^{1-x} B^x$ 在 $0 \leq x \leq 1$ 范围内的谱性质。通过该插值视角，可探究 $A$ 和 $B$ 中存在的“共同结构”（即指向相似方向的特征向量）。一般而言，算子范数 $\|A^{1-x} B^x\|$ 的精确对数线性性等价于原始矩阵存在公共特征向量；稳定性界表明，近似对数线性性迫使主奇异向量与两个矩阵的主导特征向量对齐。这些结果催生并为一种多流形学习框架提供了理论依据，该框架旨在识别多视角数据中的共同与独特潜在结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to uncover shared latent structures in multiview data, this work studies the spectral behavior of the matrix interpolation $A^{1-x} B^x$ for symmetric positive-definite matrices $A$ and $B$. The method leverages the interpolation to detect alignment of eigenvectors, revealing that exact log-linearity of the operator norm implies shared eigenvectors, while approximate log-linearity forces principal singular vectors to align with leading eigenvectors of both matrices. Experimental results confirm that this interpolation-based framework effectively identifies common and distinct structures across multiple manifolds, providing theoretical grounding for multi-manifold learning.
为揭示多视角数据中的共享潜在结构，该研究分析了对称正定矩阵 $A$ 和 $B$ 的插值 $A^{1-x} B^x$ 的谱性质。方法通过插值检测特征向量对齐，发现算子范数的精确对数线性关系意味着两矩阵共享特征向量，而近似对数线性则迫使主奇异向量与两矩阵的主导特征向量对齐。实验结果表明，该插值方法能有效识别多视角数据中的共同与独特流形结构，为多流形学习框架提供了理论依据。
TREX: Automating LLM Fine-tuning via Agent-Driven Tree-based Exploration
Authors: Zerun Ma, Guoqiang Wang, Xinchen Xie, Yicheng Chen, He Du, Bowen Li, Yanan Sun, Wenran Liu, Kai Chen, Yining Li
First: 2026-04-15T17:38:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:38:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Language Models (LLMs) have empowered AI research agents to perform isolated scientific tasks, automating complex, real-world workflows, such as LLM training, remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce TREX, a multi-agent system that automates the entire LLM training life-cycle. By orchestrating collaboration between two core modules-the Researcher and the Executor-the system seamlessly performs requirement analysis, open-domain literature and data research, formulation of training strategies, preparation of data recipes, and model training and evaluation. The multi-round experimental process is modeled as a search tree, enabling the system to efficiently plan exploration paths, reuse historical results, and distill high-level insights from iterative trials. To evaluate the capability of automated LLM training, we construct FT-Bench, a benchmark comprising 10 tasks derived from real-world scenarios, ranging from optimizing fundamental model capabilities to enhancing performance on domain-specific tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the TREX agent consistently optimizes model performance on target tasks.

Hierarchical DLO Routing with Reinforcement Learning and In-Context Vision-language Models
Authors: Mingen Li, Houjian Yu, Yixuan Huang, Youngjin Hong, Hantao Ye, Changhyun Choi
First: 2025-10-22T05:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:36:28+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Long-horizon routing tasks of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as cables and ropes, are common in industrial assembly lines and everyday life. These tasks are particularly challenging because they require robots to manipulate DLO with long-horizon planning and reliable skill execution. Successfully completing such tasks demands adapting to their nonlinear dynamics, decomposing abstract routing goals, and generating multi-step plans composed of multiple skills, all of which require accurate high-level reasoning during execution. In this paper, we propose a fully autonomous hierarchical framework for solving challenging DLO routing tasks. Given an implicit or explicit routing goal expressed in language, our framework leverages vision-language models~(VLMs) for in-context high-level reasoning to synthesize feasible plans, which are then executed by low-level skills trained via reinforcement learning. To improve robustness over long horizons, we further introduce a failure recovery mechanism that reorients the DLO into insertion-feasible states. Our approach generalizes to diverse scenes involving object attributes, spatial descriptions, implicit language commands, and \myred{extended 5-clip settings}. It achieves an overall success rate of 92\% across long-horizon routing scenarios. Please refer to our project page: https://icra2026-dloroute.github.io/DLORoute/

ID and Graph View Contrastive Learning with Multi-View Attention Fusion for Sequential Recommendation
Authors: Xiaofan Zhou, Kyumin Lee
First: 2026-04-15T17:36:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:36:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sequential recommendation has become increasingly prominent in both academia and industry, particularly in e-commerce. The primary goal is to extract user preferences from historical interaction sequences and predict items a user is likely to engage with next. Recent advances have leveraged contrastive learning and graph neural networks to learn more expressive representations from interaction histories -- graphs capture relational structure between nodes, while ID-based representations encode item-specific information. However, few studies have explored multi-view contrastive learning between ID and graph perspectives to jointly improve user and item representations, especially in settings where only interaction data is available without auxiliary information. To address this gap, we propose Multi-View Contrastive learning for sequential recommendation (MVCrec), a framework that integrates complementary signals from both sequential (ID-based) and graph-based views. MVCrec incorporates three contrastive objectives: within the sequential view, within the graph view, and across views. To effectively fuse the learned representations, we introduce a multi-view attention fusion module that combines global and local attention mechanisms to estimate the likelihood of a target user purchasing a target item. Comprehensive experiments on five real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that MVCrec consistently outperforms 11 state-of-the-art baselines, achieving improvements of up to 14.44\% in NDCG@10 and 9.22\% in HitRatio@10 over the strongest baseline. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sword-Lz/MMCrec.

UI-Zoomer: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive Zoom-In for GUI Grounding
Authors: Fei Tang, Bofan Chen, Zhengxi Lu, Tongbo Chen, Songqin Nong, Tao Jiang, Wenhao Xu, Weiming Lu, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang, Yongliang Shen
First: 2026-04-15T17:32:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:32:28+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zju-real.github.io/UI-Zoomer Code: https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/UI-Zoomer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
GUI grounding, which localizes interface elements from screenshots given natural language queries, remains challenging for small icons and dense layouts. Test-time zoom-in methods improve localization by cropping and re-running inference at higher resolution, but apply cropping uniformly across all instances with fixed crop sizes, ignoring whether the model is actually uncertain on each case. We propose \textbf{UI-Zoomer}, a training-free adaptive zoom-in framework that treats both the trigger and scale of zoom-in as a prediction uncertainty quantification problem. A confidence-aware gate fuses spatial consensus among stochastic candidates with token-level generation confidence to selectively trigger zoom-in only when localization is uncertain. When triggered, an uncertainty-driven crop sizing module decomposes prediction variance into inter-sample positional spread and intra-sample box extent, deriving a per-instance crop radius via the law of total variance. Extensive experiments on ScreenSpot-Pro, UI-Vision, and ScreenSpot-v2 demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines across multiple model architectures, achieving gains of up to +13.4\%, +10.3\%, and +4.2\% respectively, with no additional training required.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UI-Zoomer：基于不确定性的自适应放大用于GUI定位
GUI定位，即根据自然语言查询从截图中定位界面元素，对于小图标和密集布局仍具挑战性。测试时放大方法通过裁剪并以更高分辨率重新运行推理来提升定位效果，但对所有实例采用固定裁剪大小，忽略了模型在每个案例上是否真正存在不确定性。我们提出\textbf{UI-Zoomer}，一种无需训练的自适应放大框架，将放大触发与放大尺度视为预测不确定性量化问题。一个置信度感知门通过融合随机候选的空间共识与词级别生成置信度，仅在定位不确定时选择性触发放大。触发时，一个基于不确定性的裁剪尺寸模块将预测方差分解为样本间位置分散与样本内框范围，通过全变差定律推导出每个实例的裁剪半径。在ScreenSpot-Pro、UI-Vision和ScreenSpot-v2上的大量实验表明，该方法在多种模型架构上均优于强基线，分别提升13.4%、10.3%和4.2%，且无需额外训练。
