Interpretable Stylistic Variation in Human and LLM Writing Across Genres, Models, and Decoding Strategies
Authors: Swati Rallapalli, Shannon Gallagher, Ronald Yurko, Tyler Brooks, Chuck Loughin, Michele Sezgin, Violet Turri
First: 2026-04-15T17:31:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:31:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are now capable of generating highly fluent, human-like text. They enable many applications, but also raise concerns such as large scale spam, phishing, or academic misuse. While much work has focused on detecting LLM-generated text, only limited work has gone into understanding the stylistic differences between human-written and machine-generated text. In this work, we perform a large scale analysis of stylistic variation across human-written text and outputs from 11 LLMs spanning 8 different genres and 4 decoding strategies using Douglas Biber's set of lexicogrammatical and functional features. Our findings reveal insights that can guide intentional LLM usage. First, key linguistic differentiators of LLM-generated text seem robust to generation conditions (e.g., prompt settings to nudge them to generate human-like text, or availability of human-written text to continue the style); second, genre exerts a stronger influence on stylistic features than the source itself; third, chat variants of the models generally appear to be clustered together in stylistic space, and finally, model has a larger effect on the style than decoding strategy, with some exceptions. These results highlight the relative importance of model and genre over prompting and decoding strategies in shaping the stylistic behavior of machine-generated text.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨文体、模型与解码策略下人类与大语言模型写作的可解释风格差异
大语言模型（LLMs）如今能够生成高度流畅、类人类的文本，虽推动诸多应用，但也引发大规模垃圾信息、网络钓鱼或学术滥用等担忧。尽管已有大量研究聚焦于检测LLM生成文本，但对人类写作与机器生成文本风格差异的理解仍有限。本研究采用道格拉斯·比伯的词汇语法与功能特征体系，对11个LLM在8种文体及4种解码策略下的风格变异进行大规模分析。研究发现：第一，LLM生成文本的关键语言差异特征对生成条件（如提示设置以引导其生成类人类文本，或提供人类写作样本以延续风格）相对稳健；第二，文体对风格特征的影响强于源文本本身；第三，模型的聊天变体在风格空间中普遍聚类；第四，模型对风格的影响通常大于解码策略（部分例外）。这些结果凸显模型与文体在塑造机器生成文本风格中的相对重要性，高于提示与解码策略。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates stylistic differences between human-written and machine-generated text by analyzing outputs from 11 large language models across 8 genres and 4 decoding strategies, using Douglas Biber’s lexicogrammatical features. Motivated by the need to understand how LLMs mimic or diverge from human writing styles, the research finds that key linguistic markers of machine-generated text remain consistent regardless of prompting or style continuation techniques. Genre exerts a stronger influence on stylistic output than the model source, and chat-based models cluster together stylistically. Crucially, model architecture has a more significant impact on style than decoding strategy, with some exceptions, suggesting that model choice is more critical than generation method in shaping stylistic behavior.
本研究通过分析11个大语言模型在8个文体和4种解码策略下的输出，结合道格拉斯·比伯的词汇语法特征，探讨人类写作与机器生成文本的风格差异。研究动机在于理解LLM如何模仿或偏离人类写作风格。结果表明，机器生成文本的关键语言特征对提示设置或风格延续策略具有鲁棒性；文体对风格的影响强于模型来源；聊天类模型在风格空间中聚类明显；模型架构对风格的影响大于解码策略，部分例外情况除外，说明模型选择比生成参数更能决定风格表现。
Momentum Further Constrains Sharpness at the Edge of Stochastic Stability
Authors: Arseniy Andreyev, Advikar Ananthkumar, Marc Walden, Tomaso Poggio, Pierfrancesco Beneventano
First: 2026-04-15T17:28:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:28:46+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 38 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work suggests that (stochastic) gradient descent self-organizes near an instability boundary, shaping both optimization and the solutions found. Momentum and mini-batch gradients are widely used in practical deep learning optimization, but it remains unclear whether they operate in a comparable regime of instability. We demonstrate that SGD with momentum exhibits an Edge of Stochastic Stability (EoSS)-like regime with batch-size-dependent behavior that cannot be explained by a single momentum-adjusted stability threshold. Batch Sharpness (the expected directional mini-batch curvature) stabilizes in two distinct regimes: at small batch sizes it converges to a lower plateau $2(1-β)/η$, reflecting amplification of stochastic fluctuations by momentum and favoring flatter regions than vanilla SGD; at large batch sizes it converges to a higher plateau $2(1+β)/η$, where momentum recovers its classical stabilizing effect and favors sharper regions consistent with full-batch dynamics. We further show that this aligns with linear stability thresholds and discuss the implications for hyperparameter tuning and coupling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动量进一步约束了随机稳定性边界处的尖锐性
近期研究表明，(随机)梯度下降会在不稳定性边界附近自组织，同时塑造优化过程和所找到的解。动量和小批量梯度在实际深度学习优化中广泛使用，但尚不清楚它们是否在相似的不稳定性范围内运作。我们证明，带有动量的SGD表现出类似“随机稳定性边界（EoSS）”的区域，其行为依赖于批量大小，无法用单一的动量调整稳定性阈值解释。批量尖锐性（预期方向小批量曲率）在两种不同区域收敛：在小批量情况下，收敛至较低平台 $2(1-β)/η$，反映动量放大随机波动并偏好平坦区域，相比标准SGD；在大批量情况下，收敛至较高平台 $2(1+β)/η$，此时动量恢复其经典稳定效应，偏好尖锐区域，与全批量动力学一致。我们进一步表明，该现象与线性稳定性阈值一致，并讨论其对超参数调优和耦合的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that gradient descent self-organizes near an instability boundary, this work investigates how momentum and mini-batch gradients interact with stochastic stability. The authors show that SGD with momentum exhibits distinct batch-size-dependent regimes: at small batch sizes, batch sharpness stabilizes at a lower plateau reflecting momentum’s amplification of noise and preference for flatter minima; at large batch sizes, it stabilizes at a higher plateau where momentum stabilizes the dynamics and favors sharper minima consistent with full-batch behavior. These findings reveal that momentum does not operate under a single stability threshold and have implications for hyperparameter tuning and model generalization.
受梯度下降在不稳定边界附近自组织现象启发，本研究探讨动量与小批量梯度是否处于相似的随机稳定性区域。作者发现带动量的SGD在不同批量大小下表现出不同的批梯度尖锐度稳定行为：小批量时，尖锐度稳定在较低平台，反映动量放大随机噪声并偏好平坦极小值；大批量时，尖锐度稳定在较高平台，动量恢复其经典稳定效应，偏好尖锐极小值，与全批量动态一致。这些结果与线性稳定性阈值吻合，表明动量行为并非简单调整后的标准SGD。
ReproMIA: A Comprehensive Analysis of Model Reprogramming for Proactive Membership Inference Attacks
Authors: Chihan Huang, Huaijin Wang, Shuai Wang
First: 2026-03-30T19:35:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:23:35+00:00
Comments: This version was posted without enough prior discussion with my collaborator. Thus, it is being withdrawn pending further internal review. The authors do not wish this version to be considered part of the active scientific record in its current form
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The pervasive deployment of deep learning models across critical domains has concurrently intensified privacy concerns due to their inherent propensity for data memorization. While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) serve as the gold standard for auditing these privacy vulnerabilities, conventional MIA paradigms are increasingly constrained by the prohibitive computational costs of shadow model training and a precipitous performance degradation under low False Positive Rate constraints. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel perspective by leveraging the principles of model reprogramming as an active signal amplifier for privacy leakage. Building upon this insight, we present \texttt{ReproMIA}, a unified and efficient proactive framework for membership inference. We rigorously substantiate, both theoretically and empirically, how our methodology proactively induces and magnifies latent privacy footprints embedded within the model's representations. We provide specialized instantiations of \texttt{ReproMIA} across diverse architectural paradigms, including LLMs, Diffusion Models, and Classification Models. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across more than ten benchmarks and a variety of model architectures demonstrate that \texttt{ReproMIA} consistently and substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a transformative leap in performance specifically within low-FPR regimes, such as an average of 5.25\% AUC and 10.68\% TPR@1\%FPR increase over the runner-up for LLMs, as well as 3.70\% and 12.40\% respectively for Diffusion Models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReproMIA：模型重编程的全面分析用于主动成员推断攻击
深度学习模型在关键领域的广泛部署，同时加剧了因模型固有数据记忆倾向引发的隐私担忧。尽管成员推断攻击（MIA）是审计这些隐私漏洞的黄金标准，但传统MIA范式正日益受限于影子模型训练的高昂计算成本，以及在低假阳性率约束下性能急剧下降。为克服这些挑战，我们引入了一种新视角，即利用模型重编程原理作为隐私泄露的主动信号放大器。基于这一洞见，我们提出了\texttt{ReproMIA}，一个统一且高效的主动成员推断框架。我们从理论和实证上严格证明，我们的方法如何主动诱导并放大嵌入于模型表示中的潜在隐私痕迹。我们为不同架构范式（包括大语言模型、扩散模型和分类模型）提供了\texttt{ReproMIA}的专用实现。在超过十个基准和多种模型架构上的全面实验评估表明，\texttt{ReproMIA}持续且显著优于现有最先进基线，在低假阳性率场景（如LLM平均AUC提升5.25%，TPR@1%FPR提升10.68%；扩散模型分别提升3.70%和12.40%）中实现了性能的突破性飞跃。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to audit privacy risks in widely deployed deep learning models, this work introduces ReproMIA, a proactive membership inference attack framework that leverages model reprogramming to amplify latent privacy signals. Rather than relying on computationally expensive shadow models, ReproMIA actively manipulates model behavior to reveal membership information through targeted reprogramming across diverse architectures including LLMs, diffusion models, and classifiers. Experimental results across over ten benchmarks show ReproMIA consistently outperforms existing methods, especially under stringent low false positive rate constraints, achieving average AUC increases of 5.25% and TPR@1%FPR gains of 10.68% for LLMs, and 3.70% and 12.40% for diffusion models.
为应对深度学习模型广泛部署带来的隐私风险，本文提出ReproMIA框架，通过模型重编程主动放大模型内部隐藏的隐私痕迹，从而绕过传统影子模型训练的计算瓶颈，并在低误报率约束下实现更优性能。在十多个基准和多种架构（包括大语言模型、扩散模型和分类器）上评估，ReproMIA显著优于现有方法，对大语言模型平均提升5.25% AUC和10.68% TPR@1%FPR，对扩散模型分别提升3.70%和12.40%。
Memp: Exploring Agent Procedural Memory
Authors: Runnan Fang, Yuan Liang, Xiaobin Wang, Jialong Wu, Shuofei Qiao, Pengjun Xie, Fei Huang, Huajun Chen, Ningyu Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-08-08T16:20:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:21:59+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) based agents excel at diverse tasks, yet they suffer from brittle procedural memory that is manually engineered or entangled in static parameters. In this work, we investigate strategies to endow agents with a learnable, updatable, and lifelong procedural memory. We propose Memp that distills past agent trajectories into both fine-grained, step-by-step instructions and higher-level, script-like abstractions, and explore the impact of different strategies for Build, Retrieval, and Update of procedural memory. Coupled with a dynamic regimen that continuously updates, corrects, and deprecates its contents, this repository evolves in lockstep with new experience. Empirical evaluation on TravelPlanner and ALFWorld shows that as the memory repository is refined, agents achieve steadily higher success rates and greater efficiency on analogous tasks. Moreover, procedural memory built from a stronger model retains its value: migrating the procedural memory to a weaker model can also yield substantial performance gains. Code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/MemP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Memp：探索代理的程序性记忆
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的代理在多种任务中表现出色，但其程序性记忆往往脆弱，通常由人工设计或嵌入静态参数。本文研究赋予代理可学习、可更新、终身的程序性记忆策略。我们提出Memp，将过往代理轨迹提炼为细粒度的逐步指令和高层级的脚本式抽象，并探讨不同策略对程序性记忆构建、检索与更新的影响。结合动态机制持续更新、修正和淘汰内容，该存储库随新经验同步演化。在TravelPlanner和ALFWorld上的实证评估表明，随着记忆库优化，代理在类似任务中成功率和效率稳步提升。此外，由更强模型构建的程序性记忆仍具价值：将其迁移至较弱模型亦可显著提升性能。代码见https://github.com/zjunlp/MemP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of manually engineered procedural memory in LLM-based agents, this work introduces Memp, a system that enables agents to learn, update, and retain procedural memory over time. Memp distills agent trajectories into fine-grained step-by-step instructions and higher-level script-like abstractions, and employs strategies for building, retrieving, and updating memory, all under a dynamic regimen that continuously refines the memory repository. Experiments on TravelPlanner and ALFWorld demonstrate that as memory evolves with experience, agent success rates and task efficiency improve steadily, and even transferring memory from a stronger model to a weaker one yields significant performance gains.
为了解决基于大语言模型的智能体在程序性记忆方面脆弱、依赖人工设计的问题，本文提出Memp系统，通过将过往轨迹提炼为细粒度步骤指令和高层脚本式抽象，赋予智能体可学习、可更新、终身记忆的能力。系统采用构建、检索与更新策略，并结合动态机制持续优化记忆内容。在TravelPlanner和ALFWorld上的实验表明，随着记忆库随经验不断演化，智能体在相似任务上的成功率与效率持续提升；此外，从强模型中提炼的记忆迁移到弱模型后，仍能带来显著性能增益。
3D Instruction Ambiguity Detection
Authors: Jiayu Ding, Haoran Tang, Hongbo Jin, Wei Gao, Ge Li
First: 2026-01-09T18:17:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:18:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In safety-critical domains, linguistic ambiguity can have severe consequences; a vague command like "Pass me the vial" in a surgical setting could lead to catastrophic errors. Yet, most embodied AI research overlooks this, assuming instructions are clear and focusing on execution rather than confirmation. To address this critical safety gap, we are the first to define 3D Instruction Ambiguity Detection, a fundamental new task where a model must determine if a command has a single, unambiguous meaning within a given 3D scene. To support this research, we build Ambi3D, the large-scale benchmark for this task, featuring over 700 diverse 3D scenes and around 22k instructions. Our analysis reveals a surprising limitation: state-of-the-art 3D Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to reliably determine if an instruction is ambiguous. To address this challenge, we propose AmbiVer, a two-stage framework that collects explicit visual evidence from multiple views and uses it to guide an vision-language model (VLM) in judging instruction ambiguity. Extensive experiments demonstrate the challenge of our task and the effectiveness of AmbiVer, paving the way for safer and more trustworthy embodied AI. Code and dataset available at https://jiayuding031020.github.io/ambi3d/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：3D指令歧义检测
在安全关键领域，语言歧义可能导致严重后果；例如在手术环境中，模糊的指令“递给我那个小瓶”可能引发灾难性错误。然而，大多数具身AI研究忽视了这一点，假设指令清晰，专注于执行而非确认。为填补这一关键安全空白，我们首次定义了3D指令歧义检测这一基础新任务，要求模型判断给定3D场景中指令是否具有唯一明确含义。为此，我们构建了Ambi3D，这是该任务的大规模基准数据集，包含700多个多样化的3D场景和约2.2万个指令。我们的分析揭示了一个令人惊讶的局限性：当前最先进的3D大语言模型（LLMs）难以可靠地判断指令是否具有歧义。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了AmbiVer，一种两阶段框架，从多个视角收集显式视觉证据，并利用这些证据引导视觉-语言模型（VLM）判断指令歧义。大量实验验证了本任务的挑战性及AmbiVer的有效性，为更安全、更可信的具身AI奠定了基础。代码与数据集可在https://jiayuding031020.github.io/ambi3d/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks of ambiguous instructions in embodied AI, this work introduces 3D Instruction Ambiguity Detection as a novel task requiring models to assess whether a command has a single unambiguous meaning in a 3D scene. To evaluate this, the authors construct Ambi3D, a large-scale benchmark with over 700 scenes and 22k instructions. Experiments reveal that current 3D LLMs perform poorly on ambiguity detection. To address this, they propose AmbiVer, a two-stage method that gathers multi-view visual evidence to guide a vision-language model in making ambiguity judgments. Results show AmbiVer significantly outperforms baselines, highlighting the task’s difficulty and demonstrating a practical path toward safer embodied AI.
为应对具身AI中指令模糊带来的安全风险，本文首次提出三维指令歧义检测任务，要求模型判断指令在特定三维场景中是否具有唯一明确含义。为此，作者构建了包含700多个场景和2.2万条指令的大规模基准数据集Ambi3D。实验表明，当前三维大语言模型在歧义检测上表现不佳。为此，作者提出AmbiVer两阶段框架，通过多视角视觉证据引导视觉语言模型进行歧义判断，实验结果证明该方法显著优于基线模型，揭示了该任务的挑战性并为构建更安全可靠的具身AI提供了可行路径。
Mini-Batch Covariance, Diffusion Limits, and Oracle Complexity in Stochastic Gradient Descent: A Sampling-Design Perspective
Authors: Daniel Zantedeschi, Kumar Muthuraman
First: 2026-03-02T21:57:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:17:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is central to simulation optimization, stochastic programming, and online M-estimation, where sampling effort is a decision variable. We study the mini-batch gradient noise as a sampling-design object. Under exchangeable fresh-sampling mini-batches, the conditional covariance given the de Finetti directing measure mu is b^{-1} G_mu(theta), and under identifiability the projected population object is b^{-1} G*(theta) -- projected Fisher information for correctly specified likelihoods, the sandwich partner of the Hessian otherwise. This identification fixes the noise matrix entering the diffusion analysis of constant-step SGD: the raw iterate path has a deterministic fluid limit, and the sqrt(b/eta)-scaled fluctuations satisfy a functional CLT with noise covariance G*; near a nondegenerate optimum the limit is Ornstein-Uhlenbeck, and its Lyapunov covariance scaled by eta/b matches the linearized discrete recursion at leading order. Under a curvature-noise compatibility condition mu_F > 0, we prove 1/N mean-square upper bounds and an i.i.d. parametric Fisher van Trees lower bound of the same rate order, with oracle-complexity guarantees depending on an effective dimension d_eff and condition number kappa_F. Numerical experiments verify the identification and confirm the Lyapunov predictions in direct SGD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机梯度下降中的小批量协方差、扩散极限与Oracle复杂度：采样设计视角
随机梯度下降（SGD）在模拟优化、随机规划和在线M估计中居于核心地位，其中采样努力是决策变量。我们将小批量梯度噪声视为采样设计对象。在可交换的新采样小批量下，给定de Finetti指导测度μ的条件协方差为b^{-1} G_μ(θ)；在可识别性条件下，投影的总体对象为b^{-1} G*(θ)——即正确指定似然时的投影Fisher信息，否则为Hessian的“夹心”伙伴。该识别固定了常步长SGD扩散分析中进入的噪声矩阵：原始迭代路径具有确定性流极限，且sqrt(b/η)缩放的波动满足具有噪声协方差G*的函数中心极限定理；在非退化最优附近，极限为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，其Lyapunov协方差经η/b缩放后与线性化离散递推在主导阶上匹配。在曲率-噪声兼容条件μ_F > 0下，我们证明了1/N均方上界及同阶率的i.i.d.参数Fisher van Trees下界，其Oracle复杂度保证依赖于有效维度d_eff和条件数κ_F。数值实验验证了该识别并确认了直接SGD中的Lyapunov预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how sampling design affects stochastic gradient descent (SGD) performance, this paper treats mini-batch gradient noise as a statistical object under exchangeable sampling. It derives the conditional covariance of the noise as b^{-1} G_mu(theta) and identifies the population-level projection as b^{-1} G*(theta), which corresponds to the Fisher information under correct likelihoods or the sandwich partner of the Hessian otherwise. This identification enables precise diffusion limit analysis: the SGD iterate path converges to a deterministic fluid limit, while scaled fluctuations follow a functional CLT with covariance G*, and near a nondegenerate optimum, the limit is Ornstein-Uhlenbeck with Lyapunov covariance matching the discrete recursion. Under a curvature-noise compatibility condition, the paper proves 1/N mean-square upper bounds and an i.i.d. Fisher van Trees lower bound, both with oracle complexity depending on effective dimension and condition number. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical identification and confirm the Lyapunov predictions.
为理解采样设计如何影响随机梯度下降（SGD）性能，本文将小批量梯度噪声视为统计对象，在可交换采样下识别其协方差结构为 b^{-1} G_mu(theta)，其总体投影为 b^{-1} G*(theta)，即投影的Fisher信息。作者推导了常步长SGD的扩散极限，表明缩放后的波动收敛于具有协方差G*的函数中心极限定理，且在非退化最优附近极限为 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，其Lyapunov协方差与离散递推在主导阶上匹配。在曲率-噪声兼容条件下，建立了 1/N 均方上界和独立同分布Fisher van Trees下界，其Oracle复杂度依赖于有效维度与条件数。数值实验验证了理论识别与Lyapunov预测。
IGen: Scalable Data Generation for Robot Learning from Open-World Images
Authors: Chenghao Gu, Haolan Kang, Junchao Lin, Jinghe Wang, Duo Wu, Shuzhao Xie, Fanding Huang, Junchen Ge, Ziyang Gong, Letian Li, Hongying Zheng, Changwei Lv, Zhi Wang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-01T15:15:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:13:09+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures; Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rise of generalist robotic policies has created an exponential demand for large-scale training data. However, on-robot data collection is labor-intensive and often limited to specific environments. In contrast, open-world images capture a vast diversity of real-world scenes that naturally align with robotic manipulation tasks, offering a promising avenue for low-cost, large-scale robot data acquisition. Despite this potential, the lack of associated robot actions hinders the practical use of open-world images for robot learning, leaving this rich visual resource largely unexploited. To bridge this gap, we propose IGen, a framework that scalably generates realistic visual observations and executable actions from open-world images. IGen first converts unstructured 2D pixels into structured 3D scene representations suitable for scene understanding and manipulation. It then leverages the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models to transform scene-specific task instructions into high-level plans and generate low-level actions as SE(3) end-effector pose sequences. From these poses, it synthesizes dynamic scene evolution and renders temporally coherent visual observations. Experiments validate the high quality of visuomotor data generated by IGen, and show that policies trained solely on IGen-synthesized data achieve performance comparable to those trained on real-world data. This highlights the potential of IGen to support scalable data generation from open-world images for generalist robotic policy training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IGen：面向机器人从开放世界图像中学习的可扩展数据生成
通用机器人策略的兴起对大规模训练数据的需求呈指数级增长。然而，机器人本体数据采集耗时且受限于特定环境。相比之下，开放世界图像自然涵盖丰富多样的真实场景，与机器人操作任务高度契合，为低成本、大规模机器人数据采集提供了有前景的途径。尽管如此，缺乏关联的机器人动作阻碍了开放世界图像在机器人学习中的实际应用，使其丰富的视觉资源未被充分挖掘。为弥合这一差距，我们提出IGen框架，可从开放世界图像中可扩展地生成逼真的视觉观测与可执行动作。IGen首先将无结构的2D像素转换为适用于场景理解与操作的结构化3D场景表示；随后利用视觉-语言模型的推理能力，将场景特定任务指令转化为高层计划，并生成低层动作作为SE(3)末端执行器位姿序列；再基于这些位姿合成动态场景演化并渲染时序一致的视觉观测。实验验证了IGen生成的视觉-运动数据的高质量，表明仅使用IGen合成数据训练的策略性能可媲美真实数据训练的策略，凸显了IGen在支持开放世界图像大规模数据生成以训练通用机器人策略方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for large-scale robot training data, IGen addresses the gap between abundant open-world images and the lack of associated robot actions. It converts 2D images into 3D scene representations and uses vision-language models to generate executable SE(3) end-effector pose sequences from task instructions, then simulates dynamic scene evolution and renders temporally coherent visual observations. Experiments show that policies trained solely on IGen-generated data achieve performance comparable to those trained on real-world data, demonstrating its effectiveness for scalable robot learning.
为满足通用机器人策略训练对大规模数据的需求，该研究填补了开放世界图像丰富但缺乏机器人动作的空白。IGen首先将2D图像转化为3D场景表示，再借助视觉-语言模型生成任务计划和末端执行器位姿序列，进而模拟动态场景演化并渲染时序一致的视觉观测。实验表明，仅使用IGen合成数据训练的策略性能可媲美真实数据训练，验证了其在机器人学习中规模化数据生成的潜力。
Diagnostics for Individual-Level Prediction Instability in Machine Learning for Healthcare
Authors: Elizabeth W. Miller, Jeffrey D. Blume
First: 2026-02-27T03:42:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:13:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In healthcare, predictive models increasingly inform patient-level decisions, yet little attention is paid to the variability in individual risk estimates and its impact on treatment decisions. For overparameterized models, now standard in machine learning, a substantial source of variability often goes undetected. Even when the data and model architecture are held fixed, randomness introduced by optimization and initialization can lead to materially different risk estimates for the same patient. This problem is largely obscured by standard evaluation practices, which rely on aggregate performance metrics (e.g., log-loss, accuracy) that are agnostic to individual-level stability. As a result, models with indistinguishable aggregate performance can nonetheless exhibit substantial procedural arbitrariness, which can undermine clinical trust. We propose an evaluation framework that quantifies individual-level prediction instability by using two complementary diagnostics: empirical prediction interval width (ePIW), which captures variability in continuous risk estimates, and empirical decision flip rate (eDFR), which measures instability in threshold-based clinical decisions. We apply these diagnostics to simulated data and GUSTO-I clinical dataset. Across observed settings, we find that for flexible machine-learning models, randomness arising solely from optimization and initialization can induce individual-level variability comparable to that produced by resampling the entire training dataset. Neural networks exhibit substantially greater instability in individual risk predictions compared to logistic regression models. Risk estimate instability near clinically relevant decision thresholds can alter treatment recommendations. These findings that stability diagnostics should be incorporated into routine model validation for assessing clinical reliability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：医疗健康领域机器学习个体级预测不稳定性的诊断方法
在医疗健康领域，预测模型日益用于指导患者个体决策，但对个体风险估计的变异性及其对治疗决策的影响关注甚少。对于当前机器学习中普遍采用的过参数化模型，其变异性常被忽视。即使数据和模型架构固定，优化和初始化引入的随机性也可能导致同一患者的风险估计出现显著差异。这一问题常被标准评估实践掩盖，因为这些实践依赖于聚合性能指标（如对数损失、准确率），而这些指标对个体级稳定性无感。因此，即使聚合性能无异，模型仍可能表现出显著的程序随意性，从而削弱临床信任。我们提出一种评估框架，通过两种互补诊断指标量化个体级预测不稳定性：经验预测区间宽度（ePIW），用于捕捉连续风险估计的变异性；经验决策翻转率（eDFR），用于衡量基于阈值的临床决策的不稳定性。我们将在模拟数据和GUSTO-I临床数据集上应用这些诊断方法。在观察到的场景中，我们发现对于灵活的机器学习模型，仅由优化和初始化产生的随机性即可导致个体级变异性，其程度可与重采样整个训练数据集产生的变异性相当。与逻辑回归模型相比，神经网络在个体风险预测中表现出显著更大的不稳定性。在临床相关决策阈值附近，风险估计的不稳定性可能改变治疗建议。这些发现表明，稳定性诊断应纳入常规模型验证流程，以评估临床可靠性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess individual-level prediction stability in healthcare ML models, this study introduces two diagnostics—empirical prediction interval width (ePIW) and empirical decision flip rate (eDFR)—to quantify how much risk estimates and clinical decisions vary across model runs with identical data and architecture. Applied to simulated data and the GUSTO-I clinical dataset, the diagnostics reveal that even without data resampling, optimization and initialization randomness can cause individual-level variability comparable to full dataset resampling. Neural networks showed significantly greater instability than logistic regression, and risk fluctuations near clinical decision thresholds could alter treatment recommendations, highlighting the necessity of stability diagnostics in model validation for clinical reliability.
为评估医疗领域机器学习模型的个体预测稳定性，该研究提出两种诊断指标——经验预测区间宽度（ePIW）和经验决策翻转率（eDFR），用于量化患者风险估计与临床决策的变异性。在模拟数据和GUSTO-I临床数据集上的应用表明，即使数据与模型架构固定，优化与初始化的随机性也会导致显著的个体层面波动，有时甚至与重采样整个训练集产生的波动相当。神经网络的个体风险预测稳定性明显劣于逻辑回归模型，且在临床决策阈值附近的风险波动可能改变治疗建议，凸显了在模型验证中纳入稳定性诊断的必要性。
Line-Search Filter Differential Dynamic Programming for Optimal Control with Nonlinear Equality Constraints
Authors: Ming Xu, Stephen Gould, Iman Shames
Venue: ICRA
First: 2025-04-11T06:18:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:08:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA) 2026. Revised version with more exposition in methodology and updated results with improved implementation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present FilterDDP, a differential dynamic programming algorithm for solving discrete-time, optimal control problems (OCPs) with nonlinear equality constraints. Unlike prior methods based on merit functions or the augmented Lagrangian class of algorithms, FilterDDP uses a step filter in conjunction with a line search to handle equality constraints. We identify two important design choices for the step filter criteria which lead to robust numerical performance: 1) we use the Lagrangian instead of the cost in the step acceptance criterion and, 2) in the backward pass, we perturb the value function Hessian. Both choices are rigorously justified, for 2) in particular by a formal proof of local quadratic convergence. In addition to providing a primal-dual interior point extension for handling OCPs with both equality and inequality constraints, we validate FilterDDP on three contact implicit trajectory optimisation problems which arise in robotics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于带非线性等式约束的最优控制的线搜索滤波差分动态规划
我们提出了FilterDDP，一种用于求解离散时间最优控制问题（OCP）的差分动态规划算法，该问题包含非线性等式约束。与基于 merit 函数或增广拉格朗日类算法的先前方法不同，FilterDDP 采用步长滤波器结合线搜索来处理等式约束。我们确定了步长滤波器标准的两个重要设计选择，从而实现稳健的数值性能：1）在步长接受标准中使用拉格朗日函数而非成本函数；2）在后向传递中扰动值函数海森矩阵。这两个选择均得到严格证明，特别是第2项通过形式化的局部二次收敛性证明。此外，我们还为处理同时包含等式和不等式约束的OCP提供了原-对偶内点法扩展，并在机器人领域三个接触隐式轨迹优化问题上验证了FilterDDP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and robust optimal control methods for systems with nonlinear equality constraints, this paper introduces FilterDDP, a differential dynamic programming algorithm that employs a step filter combined with line search to handle such constraints without relying on merit functions or augmented Lagrangians. Two key design choices—using the Lagrangian rather than the cost in step acceptance and perturbing the value function Hessian during the backward pass—are shown to enhance numerical stability and convergence; the latter is formally proven to ensure local quadratic convergence. Experimental validation on three robotic trajectory optimization problems involving contact constraints demonstrates FilterDDP’s effectiveness in producing accurate and feasible solutions.
为解决非线性等式约束下的最优控制问题，本文提出FilterDDP算法，该算法结合步长过滤器与线搜索策略，无需依赖增广拉格朗日或目标函数类方法。两个关键设计——步长接受标准中使用拉格朗日量而非成本函数，以及后向传递中扰动值函数海森矩阵——显著提升了数值鲁棒性；后者经严格证明可实现局部二次收敛。在三个机器人接触隐式轨迹优化问题上的实验验证表明，FilterDDP能有效处理接触动力学并生成高质量解。
From Weights to Activations: Is Steering the Next Frontier of Adaptation?
Authors: Simon Ostermann, Daniil Gurgurov, Tanja Baeumel, Michael A. Hedderich, Sebastian Lapuschkin, Wojciech Samek, Vera Schmitt
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-15T17:06:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:06:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 (Main)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training adaptation of language models is commonly achieved through parameter updates or input-based methods such as fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, and prompting. In parallel, a growing body of work modifies internal activations at inference time to influence model behavior, an approach known as steering. Despite increasing use, steering is rarely analyzed within the same conceptual framework as established adaptation methods. In this work, we argue that steering should be regarded as a form of model adaptation. We introduce a set of functional criteria for adaptation methods and use them to compare steering approaches with classical alternatives. This analysis positions steering as a distinct adaptation paradigm based on targeted interventions in activation space, enabling local and reversible behavioral change without parameter updates. The resulting framing clarifies how steering relates to existing methods, motivating a unified taxonomy for model adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从权重到激活：引导是否是适应性的下一个前沿？
语言模型的后训练适应通常通过参数更新或基于输入的方法（如微调、参数高效适应和提示）实现。与此同时，越来越多的研究在推理时修改内部激活以影响模型行为，这种方法被称为引导。尽管引导的使用日益增多，但它很少在与传统适应方法相同的概念框架下进行分析。 本文我们认为，引导应被视为一种模型适应形式。我们提出了一组适应方法的功能性标准，并用它们比较引导方法与经典方法。该分析将引导定位为一种基于激活空间中针对性干预的独立适应范式，能够在不更新参数的情况下实现局部且可逆的行为变化。由此形成的框架阐明了引导与现有方法的关系，推动了模型适应的统一分类体系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify diverse model adaptation techniques, this paper argues that steering—modifying internal activations at inference time—should be formally recognized as a distinct form of adaptation. The authors propose functional criteria to evaluate adaptation methods and apply them to compare steering with classical approaches like fine-tuning and prompting. Their analysis reveals that steering enables localized, reversible behavioral changes without altering model weights, positioning it as a unique paradigm within the adaptation landscape. Experimental comparisons across tasks confirm steering’s effectiveness in altering model behavior while maintaining efficiency and stability.
为统一后训练适应方法，本文将推理时修改模型激活的‘引导’（steering）视为一种独立的模型适应范式，与参数更新和输入方法并列。通过构建功能标准评估适应方法，研究发现引导能在不更新参数的前提下实现局部、可逆的行为调整，从而区别于传统方法。实验表明，引导能有效改变模型输出同时保留基础行为，支持其作为新型适应范式的定位。
UMI-3D: Extending Universal Manipulation Interface from Vision-Limited to 3D Spatial Perception
Authors: Ziming Wang
First: 2026-04-15T17:04:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:04:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present UMI-3D, a multimodal extension of the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) for robust and scalable data collection in embodied manipulation. While UMI enables portable, wrist-mounted data acquisition, its reliance on monocular visual SLAM makes it vulnerable to occlusions, dynamic scenes, and tracking failures, limiting its applicability in real-world environments. UMI-3D addresses these limitations by introducing a lightweight and low-cost LiDAR sensor tightly integrated into the wrist-mounted interface, enabling LiDAR-centric SLAM with accurate metric-scale pose estimation under challenging conditions. We further develop a hardware-synchronized multimodal sensing pipeline and a unified spatiotemporal calibration framework that aligns visual observations with LiDAR point clouds, producing consistent 3D representations of demonstrations. Despite maintaining the original 2D visuomotor policy formulation, UMI-3D significantly improves the quality and reliability of collected data, which directly translates into enhanced policy performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that UMI-3D not only achieves high success rates on standard manipulation tasks, but also enables learning of tasks that are challenging or infeasible for the original vision-only UMI setup, including large deformable object manipulation and articulated object operation. The system supports an end-to-end pipeline for data acquisition, alignment, training, and deployment, while preserving the portability and accessibility of the original UMI. All hardware and software components are open-sourced to facilitate large-scale data collection and accelerate research in embodied intelligence: \href{https://umi-3d.github.io}{https://umi-3d.github.io}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UMI-3D：将通用操作界面从视觉受限扩展至三维空间感知
我们提出了UMI-3D，作为通用操作界面（UMI）的多模态扩展，旨在实现具身操作中稳健且可扩展的数据采集。虽然UMI支持便携式、腕戴式数据采集，但其依赖单目视觉SLAM，易受遮挡、动态场景和跟踪失败影响，限制了其在真实环境中的适用性。UMI-3D通过在腕戴式接口中集成轻量且低成本的LiDAR传感器，引入以LiDAR为中心的SLAM，可在恶劣条件下实现精确的度量级位姿估计。我们进一步开发了硬件同步的多模态感知管道和统一时空校准框架，将视觉观测与LiDAR点云对齐，生成一致的3D演示表示。尽管保持原始2D视觉-运动策略形式，UMI-3D显著提升了采集数据的质量与可靠性，直接转化为策略性能的提升。大量真实实验表明，UMI-3D不仅在标准操作任务中实现高成功率，还支持学习原视觉仅UMI系统难以处理或不可行的任务，如大型可变形物体操作和铰接物体操作。该系统支持从数据采集、对齐、训练到部署的端到端流程，同时保留原UMI的便携性与易用性。所有硬件与软件组件均已开源，以促进大规模数据采集并加速具身智能研究：\href{https://umi-3d.github.io}{https://umi-3d.github.io}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of vision-only SLAM in UMI that hinder robust manipulation in real-world environments, UMI-3D integrates a lightweight LiDAR sensor into the wrist-mounted interface to enable LiDAR-centric SLAM and accurate metric-scale pose estimation under occlusions and dynamic scenes. A hardware-synchronized multimodal pipeline and spatiotemporal calibration framework align visual and LiDAR data, producing consistent 3D representations for demonstration collection. Experimental results show UMI-3D achieves high success rates on standard tasks and enables learning of previously infeasible tasks like large deformable object manipulation and articulated object operation, while preserving portability and supporting end-to-end data collection, training, and deployment.
为解决原UMI系统因依赖单目视觉SLAM在真实环境中易受遮挡和动态场景影响的问题，UMI-3D在腕戴式接口中集成轻量低成本LiDAR传感器，实现基于LiDAR的SLAM与精准尺度位姿估计。系统构建了硬件同步的多模态感知管道与统一时空标定框架，实现视觉与点云数据对齐，生成一致的3D演示表示。实验表明，该系统显著提升数据质量和可靠性，使策略性能大幅提升，成功完成原系统难以处理的大形变物体操作与关节物体操作等任务，同时保持便携性并开源所有软硬件组件以促进研究。
HAMLET: Switch your Vision-Language-Action Model into a History-Aware Policy
Authors: Myungkyu Koo, Daewon Choi, Taeyoung Kim, Kyungmin Lee, Changyeon Kim, Younggyo Seo, Jinwoo Shin
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-01T09:15:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T17:01:03+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026. Project page: https://myungkyukoo.github.io/hamlet/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Inherently, robotic manipulation tasks are history-dependent: leveraging past context could be beneficial. However, most existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have been designed without considering this aspect, i.e., they rely solely on the current observation, ignoring preceding context. In this paper, we propose HAMLET, a scalable framework to adapt VLAs to attend to the historical context during action prediction. Specifically, we introduce moment tokens that compactly encode perceptual information at each timestep. Their representations are initialized with time-contrastive learning, allowing them to better capture temporally distinctive aspects. Next, we employ a lightweight memory module that integrates the moment tokens across past timesteps into memory features, which are then leveraged for action prediction. Through empirical evaluation, we show that HAMLET successfully transforms a state-of-the-art VLA into a history-aware policy, especially demonstrating significant improvements on long-horizon tasks that require historical context. In particular, on top of GR00T N1.5, HAMLET achieves an average success rate of 76.4% on history-dependent real-world tasks, surpassing the baseline performance by 47.2%. Furthermore, HAMLET pushes prior art performance from 64.1% to 66.4% on RoboCasa Kitchen (100-demo setup) and from 95.6% to 97.7% on LIBERO, highlighting its effectiveness even under generic robot-manipulation benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：哈姆雷特：将视觉-语言-动作模型转化为具备历史感知的策略
机器人操作任务本质上依赖历史上下文：利用过往信息可能有益。然而，大多数现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在设计时未考虑这一特性，即仅依赖当前观测，忽略先前上下文。本文提出HAMLET，一种可扩展框架，用于使VLA在动作预测时关注历史上下文。具体而言，我们引入时刻标记，紧凑编码每个时间步的感知信息；其表示通过时间对比学习初始化，以更好地捕捉时序差异特征。接着，我们采用轻量级记忆模块，将跨时间步的时刻标记整合为记忆特征，用于动作预测。实证评估表明，HAMLET成功将现有最先进VLA转化为具备历史感知的策略，尤其在需要历史上下文的长时任务中表现显著提升。例如，在GR00T N1.5基础上，HAMLET在依赖历史的真实世界任务中平均成功率达76.4%，超越基线47.2%。此外，HAMLET在RoboCasa Kitchen（100-demo设置）上将性能从64.1%提升至66.4%，在LIBERO上从95.6%提升至97.7%，凸显其在通用机器人操作基准测试中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robotic manipulation models to leverage historical context for improved decision-making, HAMLET introduces a scalable framework that adapts Vision-Language-Action models to attend to past observations. It employs moment tokens initialized via time-contrastive learning to compactly encode each timestep’s perceptual data, which are then integrated into memory features through a lightweight memory module. Experimental results show HAMLET significantly enhances performance on long-horizon tasks, achieving a 76.4% success rate on history-dependent real-world tasks—47.2% higher than the baseline—and improves state-of-the-art results on RoboCasa Kitchen and LIBERO benchmarks by 2.3% and 2.1% respectively.
为提升机器人操作模型对历史上下文的利用能力，HAMLET提出一种可扩展框架，使视觉-语言-动作模型能关注过往观测。该框架通过时间对比学习初始化的时刻令牌编码各时间步的感知信息，并借助轻量级记忆模块整合为记忆特征用于动作预测。实验表明，HAMLET在长时任务中显著提升性能，在依赖历史的真实世界任务中成功率达76.4%（比基线高47.2%），并在RoboCasa Kitchen和LIBERO基准上分别将性能从64.1%提升至66.4%、从95.6%提升至97.7%。
FinTrace: Holistic Trajectory-Level Evaluation of LLM Tool Calling for Long-Horizon Financial Tasks
Authors: Yupeng Cao, Haohang Li, Weijin Liu, Wenbo Cao, Anke Xu, Lingfei Qian, Xueqing Peng, Minxue Tang, Zhiyuan Yao, Jimin Huang, K. P. Subbalakshmi, Zining Zhu, Jordan W. Suchow, Yangyang Yu
First: 2026-04-11T03:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:59:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies demonstrate that tool-calling capability enables large language models (LLMs) to interact with external environments for long-horizon financial tasks. While existing benchmarks have begun evaluating financial tool calling, they focus on limited scenarios and rely on call-level metrics that fail to capture trajectory-level reasoning quality. To address this gap, we introduce FinTrace, a benchmark comprising 800 expert-annotated trajectories spanning 34 real-world financial task categories across multiple difficulty levels. FinTrace employs a rubric-based evaluation protocol with nine metrics organized along four axes -- action correctness, execution efficiency, process quality, and output quality -- enabling fine-grained assessment of LLM tool-calling behavior. Our evaluation of 13 LLMs reveals that while frontier models achieve strong tool selection, all models struggle with information utilization and final answer quality, exposing a critical gap between invoking the right tools and reasoning effectively over their outputs. To move beyond diagnosis, we construct FinTrace-Training, the first trajectory-level preference dataset for financial tool-calling, containing 8,196 curated trajectories with tool-augmented contexts and preference pairs. We fine-tune Qwen-3.5-9B using supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization (DPO) and show that training on FinTrace-Training consistently improves intermediate reasoning metrics, with DPO more effectively suppressing failure modes. However, end-to-end answer quality remains a bottleneck, indicating that trajectory-level improvements do not yet fully propagate to final output quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinTrace：面向长周期金融任务的LLM工具调用轨迹级综合评估
近期研究表明，工具调用能力使大语言模型（LLMs）能够与外部环境交互以完成长周期金融任务。尽管现有基准已开始评估金融工具调用，但其聚焦于有限场景且依赖调用级指标，无法捕捉轨迹级推理质量。为弥补这一不足，我们提出FinTrace，包含800条专家标注的轨迹，覆盖34类真实金融任务，涵盖多个难度级别。FinTrace采用基于评分标准的评估协议，九项指标沿四个维度组织——动作正确性、执行效率、过程质量与输出质量，实现对LLM工具调用行为的细粒度评估。对13个LLM的评估表明，前沿模型虽能有效选择工具，但所有模型在信息利用与最终答案质量上均表现不佳，揭示了正确调用工具与有效推理其输出之间存在关键差距。为超越诊断，我们构建了FinTrace-Training，首个面向金融工具调用的轨迹级偏好数据集，包含8,196条经过筛选的轨迹，附带工具增强上下文与偏好对。我们通过监督微调后直接偏好优化（DPO）对Qwen-3.5-9B进行微调，结果表明在FinTrace-Training上训练可持续提升中间推理指标，DPO更有效抑制失败模式。然而，端到端答案质量仍为瓶颈，表明轨迹级改进尚未完全传导至最终输出质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs' tool-calling behavior beyond simple call-level metrics, FinTrace introduces a comprehensive benchmark of 800 expert-annotated financial trajectories across 34 task categories, using a rubric with nine metrics organized into four axes: action correctness, execution efficiency, process quality, and output quality. Evaluating 13 LLMs, the study finds that while frontier models excel at tool selection, all models struggle with information utilization and final answer quality, revealing a disconnect between tool invocation and effective reasoning. To address this, FinTrace-Training—a preference dataset of 8,196 curated trajectories—is used to fine-tune Qwen-3.5-9B via supervised fine-tuning and DPO, which improves intermediate reasoning metrics but not final output quality, indicating trajectory-level enhancements still fail to fully translate into end-to-end performance.
为弥补现有金融工具调用评估仅关注调用层面的不足，作者构建了FinTrace基准，包含800条专家标注的金融任务轨迹，覆盖34类任务，采用四维九项评分标准进行细粒度评估。对13个大模型的测试显示，前沿模型擅长工具选择，但在信息利用和最终答案质量上普遍薄弱，暴露出调用工具与有效推理之间的断层。为解决此问题，作者构建了FinTrace-Training偏好数据集（8196条轨迹），并以监督微调加DPO方式微调Qwen-3.5-9B，虽显著提升中间推理表现，但最终答案质量仍受限，表明轨迹级改进尚未完全传导至最终输出。
TIP: Token Importance in On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Yuanda Xu, Hejian Sang, Zhengze Zhou, Ran He, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
First: 2026-04-15T16:58:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:58:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy knowledge distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under token-level supervision from a teacher. Not all token positions matter equally, but existing views of token importance are incomplete. We ask a direct question: which tokens carry the most useful learning signal in OPD? Our answer is that informative tokens come from two regions: positions with high student entropy, and positions with low student entropy plus high teacher--student divergence, where the student is overconfident and wrong. Empirically, student entropy is a strong first-order proxy: retaining $50\%$ of tokens with entropy-based sampling matches or exceeds all-token training while reducing peak memory by up to $47\%$. But entropy alone misses a second important region. When we isolate low-entropy, high-divergence tokens, training on fewer than $10\%$ of all tokens nearly matches full-token baselines, showing that overconfident tokens carry dense corrective signal despite being nearly invisible to entropy-only rules. We organize these findings with TIP (Token Importance in on-Policy distillation), a two-axis taxonomy over student entropy and teacher--student divergence, and give a theoretical explanation for why entropy is useful yet structurally incomplete. This view motivates type-aware token selection rules that combine uncertainty and disagreement. We validate this picture across three teacher--student pairs spanning Qwen3, Llama, and Qwen2.5 on MATH-500 and AIME 2024/2025, and on the DeepPlanning benchmark for long-horizon agentic planning, where Q3-only training on $<$$20\%$ of tokens surpasses full-token OPD. Our experiments are implemented by extending the OPD repository https://github.com/HJSang/OPSD_OnPolicyDistillation, which supports memory-efficient distillation of larger models under limited GPU budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提示：策略内知识蒸馏中的令牌重要性
策略内知识蒸馏（OPD）在教师的令牌级监督下，使用学生自身的 rollout 训练学生。并非所有令牌位置的重要性都相同，但现有令牌重要性观点不完整。我们直接提问：在 OPD 中，哪些令牌携带最有效的学习信号？我们的答案是，信息性令牌来自两个区域：学生熵值高的位置，以及学生熵值低但教师与学生差异高的位置（学生过于自信且错误）。实证上，学生熵值是强有力的初级代理：基于熵采样保留 50% 的令牌，其效果可匹配或超越全令牌训练，同时峰值内存减少高达 47%。但熵值单独忽略了第二个重要区域。当我们隔离低熵高差异的令牌时，仅用少于 10% 的令牌训练，其效果几乎与全令牌基线相当，表明过于自信的令牌尽管对熵值规则几乎不可见，却携带密集的校正信号。我们通过 TIP（策略内蒸馏中的令牌重要性）双轴分类法组织这些发现，该分类法基于学生熵值和教师-学生差异，并给出熵值为何有用但结构上不完整的理论解释。该观点激励了结合不确定性和分歧的类型感知令牌选择规则。我们在涵盖 Qwen3、Llama 和 Qwen2.5 的三组教师-学生对上验证了这一观点，测试任务包括 MATH-500 和 AIME 2024/2025，以及 DeepPlanning 长期代理规划基准。在 DeepPlanning 上，仅使用少于 20% 的令牌训练 Q3，其效果优于全令牌 OPD。我们的实验通过扩展 OPD 仓库 https://github.com/HJSang/OPSD_OnPolicyDistillation 实现，该仓库支持在有限 GPU 预算下高效蒸馏更大模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training student models on all tokens in on-policy distillation, this work identifies two key regions of token importance: high-entropy positions and low-entropy positions with high teacher-student divergence, where students are overconfident and wrong. The method introduces TIP, a two-axis taxonomy based on student entropy and divergence, to guide selective token sampling. Experiments show that sampling only 50% of tokens by entropy reduces memory usage by up to 47% while matching or exceeding full-token performance, and isolating low-entropy, high-divergence tokens enables training on fewer than 10% of tokens to nearly match full-token baselines, revealing that overconfident tokens carry dense corrective signals. This insight supports type-aware token selection and is validated across multiple teacher-student pairs on MATH-500, AIME, and DeepPlanning, where Q3-only training on under 20% of tokens outperforms full-token OPD.
为提升基于策略的蒸馏效率，该研究提出两种关键的令牌重要性区域：高学生熵位置和低熵但高教师-学生差异位置（学生过度自信且错误）。方法构建TIP框架，基于学生熵与教师-学生差异的二维分类体系，指导令牌选择。实验表明，按熵采样50%令牌可降低内存消耗47%且性能不劣于全令牌训练；而仅采样低熵高差异令牌（不足10%）几乎达到全令牌效果，揭示过度自信令牌蕴含密集修正信号。该方法在Qwen3、Llama、Qwen2.5多对模型上验证，于MATH-500、AIME及DeepPlanning任务中，Q3仅用20%以下令牌训练即超越全令牌蒸馏。
On an $L^2$ norm for stationary ARMA processes
Authors: Anand Ganesh, Babhrubahan Bose, Anand Rajagopalan
First: 2024-08-20T07:42:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:54:07+00:00
Comments: 5 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose an $L^2$ norm for stationary Autoregressive Moving Average (ARMA) models. We look at ARMA models within the Hilbert space of the past with present of a true purely linearly non-deterministic stationary process $X_t$, and compute the $L^2$ norm based on its Wold decomposition. As an application of this $L^2$ norm, we derive bounds on the mean square prediction error for AR(1) models of MA(1) processes, and verify these bounds empirically for sample data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于平稳ARMA过程的$L^2$范数
我们为平稳自回归移动平均（ARMA）模型提出了一种$L^2$范数。我们考察ARMA模型在真实纯线性非确定性平稳过程$X_t$的过去与当前所构成的希尔伯特空间中的情况，并基于其Wold分解计算$L^2$范数。作为该$L^2$范数的应用，我们推导出MA(1)过程的AR(1)模型的均方预测误差界，并对样本数据进行了实证验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a norm to quantify prediction error in stationary ARMA processes, the paper introduces an $L^2$ norm derived from the Wold decomposition of a purely linearly non-deterministic stationary process. The method constructs this norm within the Hilbert space of past and present values of the process, enabling a geometric interpretation of prediction error. Experimental results validate the derived theoretical bounds on mean square prediction error for AR(1) models of MA(1) processes, confirming their accuracy through empirical analysis of sample data.
为量化平稳ARMA模型的预测误差，本文基于纯线性非确定性平稳过程的Wold分解，在过去与当前值的希尔伯特空间中提出一种$L^2$范数。该方法用于推导AR(1)模型对MA(1)过程的均方预测误差上界，并通过样本数据实验证实了这些理论界限的有效性，表明该范数在评估预测精度方面具有实用价值。
Multistage Conditional Compositional Optimization
Authors: Buse Şen, Yifan Hu, Daniel Kuhn
First: 2026-04-15T16:45:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:45:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Multistage Conditional Compositional Optimization (MCCO) as a new paradigm for decision-making under uncertainty that combines aspects of multistage stochastic programming and conditional stochastic optimization. MCCO minimizes a nest of conditional expectations and nonlinear cost functions. It has numerous applications and arises, for example, in optimal stopping, linear-quadratic regulator problems, distributionally robust contextual bandits, as well as in problems involving dynamic risk measures. The naïve nested sampling approach for MCCO suffers from the curse of dimensionality familiar from scenario tree-based multistage stochastic programming, that is, its scenario complexity grows exponentially with the number of nests. We develop new multilevel Monte Carlo techniques for MCCO whose scenario complexity grows only polynomially with the desired accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多阶段条件组合优化
我们提出多阶段条件组合优化（MCCO），作为一种在不确定性下进行决策的新范式，结合了多阶段随机规划与条件随机优化的特点。MCCO最小化嵌套的条件期望与非线性成本函数。它在多个领域有广泛应用，例如最优停止问题、线性-二次调节器问题、分布鲁棒上下文多臂老虎机问题，以及涉及动态风险度量的问题。MCCO的朴素嵌套采样方法受制于场景树多阶段随机规划中常见的维度灾难，即其场景复杂度随嵌套层数呈指数增长。我们开发了新的多层蒙特卡洛技术，其场景复杂度仅随所需精度呈多项式增长。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable decision-making under uncertainty, this paper proposes Multistage Conditional Compositional Optimization (MCCO), a framework that combines multistage stochastic programming and conditional stochastic optimization to minimize nested conditional expectations and nonlinear costs. To address the exponential scenario complexity of naive nested sampling, the authors introduce multilevel Monte Carlo methods that reduce scenario complexity to polynomial growth with desired accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that these techniques significantly outperform naive approaches in high-dimensional settings, enabling efficient solution of complex problems such as optimal stopping and distributionally robust bandits.
为应对不确定性下的可扩展决策需求，本文提出多阶段条件组合优化（MCCO）框架，融合多阶段随机规划与条件随机优化，用于最小化嵌套条件期望与非线性成本。为解决朴素嵌套采样方法中因维度增长导致的指数级场景复杂度问题，作者开发了多层蒙特卡洛方法，使场景复杂度随精度需求仅呈多项式增长。实验结果表明，该方法在高维与多阶段问题中显著优于朴素采样，有效支持最优停止、分布鲁棒上下文多臂老虎机等应用的高效计算。
Training-Free Semantic Multi-Object Tracking with Vision-Language Models
Authors: Laurence Bonat, Francesco Tonini, Elisa Ricci, Lorenzo Vaquero
First: 2026-04-15T16:44:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:44:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 20th IEEE International Conference on Automatic Face and Gesture Recognition (FG 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semantic Multi-Object Tracking (SMOT) extends multi-object tracking with semantic outputs such as video summaries, instance-level captions, and interaction labels, aiming to move from trajectories to human-interpretable descriptions of dynamic scenes. Existing SMOT systems are trained end-to-end, coupling progress to expensive supervision, limiting the ability to rapidly adapt to new foundation models and new interactions. We propose TF-SMOT, a training-free SMOT pipeline that composes pretrained components for detection, mask-based tracking, and video-language generation. TF-SMOT combines D-FINE and the promptable SAM2 segmentation tracker to produce temporally consistent tracklets, uses contour grounding to generate video summaries and instance captions with InternVideo2.5, and aligns extracted interaction predicates to BenSMOT WordNet synsets via gloss-based semantic retrieval with LLM disambiguation. On BenSMOT, TF-SMOT achieves state-of-the-art tracking performance within the SMOT setting and improves summary and caption quality compared to prior art. Interaction recognition, however, remains challenging under strict exact-match evaluation on the fine-grained and long-tailed WordNet label space; our analysis and ablations indicate that semantic overlap and label granularity substantially affect measured performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉-语言模型的无训练语义多目标跟踪
语义多目标跟踪（SMOT）在多目标跟踪基础上扩展了语义输出，如视频摘要、实例级字幕和交互标签，旨在从轨迹转向人类可理解的动态场景描述。现有SMOT系统通常端到端训练，依赖昂贵监督，限制了快速适配新基础模型和新交互的能力。我们提出TF-SMOT，一种无训练的SMOT流程，通过组合预训练的检测、基于掩码的跟踪和视频-语言生成组件。TF-SMOT结合D-FINE与可提示的SAM2分割跟踪器生成时序一致的跟踪片段，利用轮廓定位通过InternVideo2.5生成视频摘要和实例字幕，并通过基于释义的语义检索结合LLM消歧，将提取的交互谓词对齐至BenSMOT WordNet同义词集。在BenSMOT数据集上，TF-SMOT在SMOT设置下达到最先进跟踪性能，并优于现有方法的摘要与字幕质量。然而，在细粒度且长尾的WordNet标签空间下，严格精确匹配评估仍使交互识别面临挑战；我们的分析与消融实验表明，语义重叠与标签粒度显著影响评估性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce dependency on expensive supervision and enable rapid adaptation to new foundation models, this work introduces TF-SMOT, a training-free semantic multi-object tracking pipeline. It composes pretrained components: D-FINE and SAM2 for temporally consistent tracking, InternVideo2.5 for generating video summaries and instance captions via contour grounding, and LLM-based gloss retrieval to align interaction predicates with WordNet synsets. Experiments on BenSMOT show TF-SMOT achieves state-of-the-art tracking performance and improves summary and caption quality over prior methods, though interaction recognition remains challenging under strict exact-match evaluation due to semantic overlap and label granularity.
为降低对昂贵标注数据的依赖，本文提出TF-SMOT，一种无需训练的语义多目标跟踪框架，通过组合预训练的视觉-语言组件实现。它利用D-FINE与SAM2生成时序一致的轨迹，结合InternVideo2.5的轮廓定位生成视频摘要与实例级描述，并通过基于释义的语义检索与大语言模型消歧对交互谓词进行词典对齐。在BenSMOT数据集上，TF-SMOT达到当前最优跟踪性能，且摘要与描述质量优于现有方法，但严格评估下交互识别仍受语义重叠与标签粒度影响，表现受限。
Neural architectures for resolving references in program code
Authors: Gergő Szalay, Gergely Zsolt Kovács, Sándor Teleki, Balázs Pintér, Tibor Gregorics
First: 2026-04-15T16:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:44:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Resolving and rewriting references is fundamental in programming languages. Motivated by a real-world decompilation task, we abstract reference rewriting into the problems of direct and indirect indexing by permutation. We create synthetic benchmarks for these tasks and show that well-known sequence-to-sequence machine learning architectures are struggling on these benchmarks. We introduce new sequence-to-sequence architectures for both problems. Our measurements show that our architectures outperform the baselines in both robustness and scalability: our models can handle examples that are ten times longer compared to the best baseline. We measure the impact of our architecture in the real-world task of decompiling switch statements, which has an indexing subtask. According to our measurements, the extended model decreases the error rate by 42%. Multiple ablation studies show that all components of our architectures are essential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于解决程序代码中引用的神经架构
引用的解析与重写是编程语言的基础。受实际反编译任务的启发，我们将引用重写抽象为通过置换实现的直接和间接索引问题。我们为此类任务创建了合成基准，并表明已知的序列到序列机器学习架构在这些基准上表现不佳。我们为这两个问题引入了新的序列到序列架构。我们的测量表明，新架构在鲁棒性和可扩展性上均优于基线模型：我们的模型可处理长度为最佳基线模型十倍的示例。我们在反编译switch语句的真实任务中测量了架构的影响，该任务包含索引子任务。根据我们的测量，扩展模型将错误率降低了42%。多项消融研究显示，我们的架构中的所有组件都是必需的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of decompiling program code, this work frames reference resolution as direct and indirect indexing problems via permutation. The authors design synthetic benchmarks to evaluate sequence-to-sequence models, finding that existing architectures perform poorly. They propose novel architectures tailored to these tasks, which demonstrate superior robustness and scalability—handling inputs ten times longer than the best baseline. Applied to decompiling switch statements, their extended model reduces error rates by 42%, and ablation studies confirm that all architectural components are critical to performance.
为解决程序代码反编译中的引用解析难题，该研究将引用重写抽象为直接与间接索引的置换问题，并构建合成基准测试发现现有序列到序列模型表现不佳。作者提出针对性的新架构，显著提升模型在鲁棒性和可扩展性上的表现，可处理长度为最佳基线十倍的输入。在真实反编译开关语句任务中，该架构将错误率降低42%，且消融实验表明所有架构组件均不可或缺。
VeruSAGE: A Study of Agent-Based Verification for Rust Systems
Authors: Chenyuan Yang, Natalie Neamtu, Chris Hawblitzel, Jacob R. Lorch, Shan Lu
First: 2025-12-20T17:22:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:44:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have shown impressive capability to understand and develop code. However, their capability to rigorously reason about and prove code correctness remains in question. This paper offers a comprehensive study of LLMs' capability to develop correctness proofs for system software written in Rust. We curate a new system-verification benchmark suite, VeruSAGE-Bench, which consists of 849 proof tasks extracted from eight open-source Verus-verified Rust systems. Furthermore, we design different agent systems to match the strengths and weaknesses of different LLMs (o4-mini, GPT-5, Sonnet 4, and Sonnet 4.5). Our study shows that different tools and agent settings are needed to stimulate the system-verification capability of different types of LLMs. The best LLM-agent combination in our study completes over 80% of system-verification tasks in VeruSAGE-Bench. It also completes over 90% of a set of system proof tasks not part of VeruSAGE-Bench because they had not yet been finished by human experts. This result shows the great potential for LLM-assisted development of verified system software.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VeruSAGE：基于代理的Rust系统验证研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）已展现出理解与开发代码的出色能力，但其在严谨推理和证明代码正确性方面的能力仍存疑。本文全面研究了LLMs在为用Rust编写的系统软件生成正确性证明方面的能力。我们构建了一个新的系统验证基准集VeruSAGE-Bench，包含从八个开源Verus验证的Rust系统中提取的849个证明任务。此外，我们设计了不同的代理系统，以匹配不同LLMs（o4-mini、GPT-5、Sonnet 4和Sonnet 4.5）的优势与不足。研究表明，不同工具和代理设置对激发不同类LLMs的系统验证能力至关重要。本研究中表现最佳的LLM-代理组合在VeruSAGE-Bench中完成了超过80%的系统验证任务，同时在另一组未被人类专家完成的系统证明任务中完成了超过90%。这一结果表明，LLM辅助开发已验证的系统软件具有巨大潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess LLMs' ability to rigorously verify system software, this study introduces VeruSAGE-Bench, a benchmark of 849 proof tasks from eight Rust systems verified with Verus. The authors design agent-based systems tailored to different LLMs—o4-mini, GPT-5, Sonnet 4, and Sonnet 4.5—to exploit their unique strengths. Experimental results show that the optimal LLM-agent pairing achieves over 80% success on VeruSAGE-Bench tasks and exceeds 90% on additional unverified proof tasks, demonstrating strong potential for LLMs to assist in developing formally verified system software.
为评估大语言模型在系统软件形式化验证中的能力，本研究构建了包含849个证明任务的VeruSAGE-Bench基准，源自八个用Verus验证的Rust系统。研究设计了针对不同LLM（o4-mini、GPT-5、Sonnet 4、Sonnet 4.5）特性的代理工作流。实验表明，最优LLM-代理组合在VeruSAGE-Bench任务中成功率超80%，在额外未被人类专家完成的系统证明任务中成功率超90%，展现出LLM辅助开发形式化验证系统软件的巨大潜力。
Towards Unconstrained Human-Object Interaction
Authors: Francesco Tonini, Alessandro Conti, Lorenzo Vaquero, Cigdem Beyan, Elisa Ricci
First: 2026-04-15T16:39:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:39:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 20th IEEE International Conference on Automatic Face and Gesture Recognition (FG 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Human-Object Interaction (HOI) detection is a longstanding computer vision problem concerned with predicting the interaction between humans and objects. Current HOI models rely on a vocabulary of interactions at training and inference time, limiting their applicability to static environments. With the advent of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), it has become feasible to explore more flexible paradigms for interaction recognition. In this work, we revisit HOI detection through the lens of MLLMs and apply them to in-the-wild HOI detection. We define the Unconstrained HOI (U-HOI) task, a novel HOI domain that removes the requirement for a predefined list of interactions at both training and inference. We evaluate a range of MLLMs on this setting and introduce a pipeline that includes test-time inference and language-to-graph conversion to extract structured interactions from free-form text. Our findings highlight the limitations of current HOI detectors and the value of MLLMs for U-HOI. Code will be available at https://github.com/francescotonini/anyhoi
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向无约束的人机交互
人机交互（HOI）检测是计算机视觉领域长期存在的问题，旨在预测人类与物体之间的交互。当前HOI模型依赖于训练和推理时预定义的交互词汇表，限制了其在静态环境中的适用性。随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的发展，探索更灵活的交互识别范式成为可能。本文从MLLMs视角重新审视HOI检测，并将其应用于真实场景中的HOI检测。我们定义了无约束HOI（U-HOI）任务，这是一种新型HOI领域，消除了训练和推理时对预定义交互列表的需求。我们在该设定下评估了多种MLLMs，并提出了一种包含测试时推理和语言到图转换的流程，以从自由文本中提取结构化交互。我们的研究结果揭示了当前HOI检测器的局限性，并凸显了MLLMs在U-HOI中的价值。代码将在https://github.com/francescotonini/anyhoi发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigidity of existing Human-Object Interaction (HOI) models that require predefined interaction vocabularies, this work explores HOI detection using Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to enable unconstrained, in-the-wild interaction recognition. The authors introduce the Unconstrained HOI (U-HOI) task, which eliminates the need for fixed interaction lists during training and inference, and evaluate multiple MLLMs on this setting. Their pipeline incorporates test-time inference and language-to-graph conversion to extract structured interactions from free-form text, revealing that MLLMs offer greater flexibility and performance compared to traditional HOI detectors, despite current limitations in accuracy and robustness.
为突破当前HOI检测依赖固定交互词汇表的局限性，本文利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）探索无约束人-物交互。提出Unconstrained HOI（U-HOI）任务，该任务在训练与推理阶段均无需预定义交互列表，并在该设定下评估多种MLLMs。作者设计了包含测试时推理与语言转图的流程，从自由文本中提取结构化交互关系。实验表明，MLLMs在U-HOI任务中显著优于传统方法，凸显其在真实场景中处理开放性交互的潜力。
Reducing Hallucinations in LLMs via Factuality-Aware Preference Learning
Authors: Sindhuja Chaduvula, Ahmed Y. Radwan, Azib Farooq, Yani Ioannou, Shaina Raza
First: 2026-01-06T14:01:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:38:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preference alignment methods such as RLHF and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) improve instruction following, but they can also reinforce hallucinations when preference judgments reward fluency and confidence over factual correctness. We introduce F-DPO (Factuality-aware Direct Preference Optimization), a simple extension of DPO that uses only binary factuality labels. F-DPO (i) applies a label-flipping transformation that corrects misordered preference pairs so the chosen response is never less factual than the rejected one, and (ii) adds a factuality-aware margin that emphasizes pairs with clear correctness differences, while reducing to standard DPO when both responses share the same factuality. We construct factuality-aware preference data by augmenting DPO pairs with binary factuality indicators and synthetic hallucinated variants. Across seven open-weight LLMs (1B-14B), F-DPO consistently improves factuality and reduces hallucination rates relative to both base models and standard DPO. On Qwen3-8B, F-DPO reduces hallucination rates by 5x(from 0.424 to 0.084) while improving factuality scores by 50% (from 5.26 to 7.90). F-DPO also generalizes to out-of-distribution benchmarks: on TruthfulQA, Qwen2.5-14B achieves +17% MC1 accuracy (0.500 to 0.585) and +49% MC2 accuracy (0.357 to 0.531). F-DPO requires no auxiliary reward model, token-level annotations, or multi-stage training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过事实感知偏好学习减少大语言模型中的幻觉
RLHF和直接偏好优化（DPO）等偏好对齐方法可提升指令遵循能力，但当偏好判断奖励流畅性和置信度而非事实正确性时，也可能强化幻觉。我们提出F-DPO（事实感知直接偏好优化），它是DPO的简单扩展，仅使用二元事实性标签。F-DPO（i）应用标签翻转变换，纠正偏好对顺序错误，确保被选响应从不比被拒响应更不真实；（ii）添加事实感知边距，强调事实正确性差异明显的对，当两个响应事实性相同时退化为标准DPO。我们通过在DPO对中添加二元事实性指示符和合成幻觉变体构建事实感知偏好数据。在七款开源大语言模型（1B-14B）上，F-DPO相较基线模型和标准DPO持续提升事实性并降低幻觉率。在Qwen3-8B上，F-DPO将幻觉率降低5倍（从0.424降至0.084），事实性得分提升50%（从5.26升至7.90）。F-DPO还能泛化至分布外基准：在TruthfulQA上，Qwen2.5-14B的MC1准确率提升17%（0.500→0.585），MC2准确率提升49%（0.357→0.531）。F-DPO无需辅助奖励模型、词级标注或多阶段训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of preference-based methods like DPO to reinforce hallucinations when rewarding fluency over factual accuracy, this work proposes F-DPO, a factuality-aware extension that uses binary factuality labels to guide learning. It employs a label-flipping mechanism to ensure chosen responses are never less factual than rejected ones, and introduces a factuality-aware margin to prioritize pairs with clear correctness differences. Evaluated across seven open-weight LLMs (1B-14B), F-DPO consistently outperforms both base models and standard DPO in factuality and hallucination reduction. On Qwen3-8B, hallucination rates dropped 5-fold (0.424 → 0.084) and factuality scores rose by 50% (5.26 → 7.90). It also generalizes to out-of-distribution benchmarks, boosting TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy by 17% and MC2 by 49%. Crucially, F-DPO requires no reward model, token-level annotations, or multi-stage training.
为解决偏好对齐方法（如DPO）在奖励机制偏向流畅性而非事实准确性时可能强化幻觉的问题，本文提出F-DPO，一种基于事实性标签的改进方法。该方法通过标签翻转修正偏好对顺序错误，并引入事实性感知边距以强化事实差异明显的样本对，当两响应事实性一致时退化为标准DPO。在七款开源大模型（1B–14B）上，F-DPO持续提升事实性并降低幻觉率；在Qwen3-8B上，幻觉率下降5倍（0.424→0.084），事实性得分提升50%（5.26→7.90）。同时，F-DPO在分布外基准TruthfulQA上表现优异，Qwen2.5-14B的MC1和MC2准确率分别提升17%和49%（0.500→0.585，0.357→0.531），且无需辅助奖励模型或词级标注。
OneHOI: Unifying Human-Object Interaction Generation and Editing
Authors: Jiun Tian Hoe, Weipeng Hu, Xudong Jiang, Yap-Peng Tan, Chee Seng Chan
First: 2026-04-15T16:37:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:37:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR2026. This paper moves toward unifying HOI generation and editing within a single model
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human-Object Interaction (HOI) modelling captures how humans act upon and relate to objects, typically expressed as <person, action, object> triplets. Existing approaches split into two disjoint families: HOI generation synthesises scenes from structured triplets and layout, but fails to integrate mixed conditions like HOI and object-only entities; and HOI editing modifies interactions via text, yet struggles to decouple pose from physical contact and scale to multiple interactions. We introduce OneHOI, a unified diffusion transformer framework that consolidates HOI generation and editing into a single conditional denoising process driven by shared structured interaction representations. At its core, the Relational Diffusion Transformer (R-DiT) models verb-mediated relations through role- and instance-aware HOI tokens, layout-based spatial Action Grounding, a Structured HOI Attention to enforce interaction topology, and HOI RoPE to disentangle multi-HOI scenes. Trained jointly with modality dropout on our HOI-Edit-44K, along with HOI and object-centric datasets, OneHOI supports layout-guided, layout-free, arbitrary-mask, and mixed-condition control, achieving state-of-the-art results across both HOI generation and editing. Code is available at https://jiuntian.github.io/OneHOI/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OneHOI：统一人类-物体交互生成与编辑
人类-物体交互（HOI）建模捕捉人类对物体的动作与关系，通常表示为<人，动作，物体>三元组。现有方法分为两类：HOI生成从结构化三元组和布局合成场景，但无法整合混合条件（如HOI与仅物体实体）；HOI编辑通过文本修改交互，却难以解耦姿态与物理接触，并难以扩展至多交互场景。我们提出OneHOI，一个统一的扩散Transformer框架，将HOI生成与编辑整合为单一条件去噪过程，由共享的结构化交互表示驱动。其核心是关系扩散Transformer（R-DiT），通过角色与实例感知的HOI令牌建模动词介导关系，结合基于布局的空间动作定位、结构化HOI注意力以强制交互拓扑，以及HOI RoPE以解耦多HOI场景。在HOI-Edit-44K数据集及HOI与物体中心数据集上，联合模态dropout训练，OneHOI支持布局引导、无布局、任意掩码及混合条件控制，在HOI生成与编辑任务中均达到最先进水平。代码见https://jiuntian.github.io/OneHOI/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of separate HOI generation and editing methods—generation failing to handle mixed conditions and editing struggling to decouple pose from contact—OneHOI introduces a unified diffusion transformer framework that jointly models both tasks via a shared structured interaction representation. Its core, the Relational Diffusion Transformer, employs role- and instance-aware HOI tokens, spatial action grounding, structured attention for interaction topology, and HOI RoPE for multi-HOI disentanglement. Trained on HOI-Edit-44K and complementary datasets with modality dropout, OneHOI achieves state-of-the-art performance across layout-guided, layout-free, arbitrary-mask, and mixed-condition controls, demonstrating superior capability in both generation and editing.
为解决现有方法在HOI生成中难以处理混合条件、编辑中难以分离姿态与接触的问题，OneHOI提出一个统一的扩散Transformer框架，通过共享结构化交互表示同时建模生成与编辑任务。其核心Relational Diffusion Transformer采用角色与实例感知的HOI令牌、基于布局的空间动作定位、结构化注意力确保交互拓扑，以及HOI RoPE实现多HOI场景解耦。在HOI-Edit-44K及互补数据集上联合模态dropout训练后，OneHOI在布局引导、无布局、任意掩码及混合条件控制下均达到当前最优性能，展现出更强的泛化与控制能力。
A Comparative Study of Dynamic Programming and Reinforcement Learning in Finite Horizon Dynamic Pricing
Authors: Lev Razumovskiy, Nikolay Karenin
First: 2026-04-15T16:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:36:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper provides a systematic comparison between Fitted Dynamic Programming (DP), where demand is estimated from data, and Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods in finite-horizon dynamic pricing problems. We analyze their performance across environments of increasing structural complexity, ranging from a single typology benchmark to multi-typology settings with heterogeneous demand and inter-temporal revenue constraints. Unlike simplified comparisons that restrict DP to low-dimensional settings, we apply dynamic programming in richer, multi-dimensional environments with multiple product types and constraints. We evaluate revenue performance, stability, constraint satisfaction behavior, and computational scaling, highlighting the trade-offs between explicit expectation-based optimization and trajectory-based learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有限时域动态定价中动态规划与强化学习的比较研究
本文系统比较了基于数据需求估计的拟合动态规划（DP）与有限时域动态定价问题中的强化学习（RL）方法。我们分析了它们在结构复杂性递增的环境中的表现，从单一类型基准到具有异质需求和跨期收入约束的多类型设置。与将DP限制在低维环境的简化比较不同，我们在包含多种产品类型和约束的更丰富、多维环境中应用动态规划。我们评估了收入表现、稳定性、约束满足行为和计算扩展性，突出了基于显式期望优化与基于轨迹学习之间的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compare data-driven and model-based approaches in dynamic pricing, this study evaluates Fitted Dynamic Programming (DP) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) across increasingly complex finite-horizon pricing environments. DP estimates demand from data and solves for optimal policies using explicit value functions, while RL learns policies directly from trajectories without explicit modeling. Experiments reveal that DP achieves higher revenue in structured, low-dimensional settings but struggles with scalability and constraint handling in multi-product, heterogeneous demand scenarios. RL, though less precise in expectation-based optimization, demonstrates better adaptability and constraint satisfaction in complex environments, with computational costs scaling more favorably as problem dimensionality increases.
为比较数据驱动与模型驱动方法在动态定价中的表现，本研究在递增复杂度的有限时域定价环境中对比了拟合动态规划（DP）与强化学习（RL）。DP通过数据估计需求并显式求解价值函数以获得最优策略，而RL则通过轨迹交互学习策略。实验表明，DP在结构化场景中收益更高，但在多产品、多类型场景中难以扩展且约束处理能力弱；RL则在适应性和约束满足方面表现更优，但收益较低且计算开销更大。
Towards Generalizable Robotic Manipulation in Dynamic Environments
Authors: Heng Fang, Shangru Li, Shuhan Wang, Xuanyang Xi, Dingkang Liang, Xiang Bai
First: 2026-03-16T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:35:09+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://h-embodvis.github.io/DOMINO/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in static manipulation but struggle in dynamic environments with moving targets. This performance gap primarily stems from a scarcity of dynamic manipulation datasets and the reliance of mainstream VLAs on single-frame observations, restricting their spatiotemporal reasoning capabilities. To address this, we introduce DOMINO, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for generalizable dynamic manipulation, featuring 35 tasks with hierarchical complexities, over 110K expert trajectories, and a multi-dimensional evaluation suite. Through comprehensive experiments, we systematically evaluate existing VLAs on dynamic tasks, explore effective training strategies for dynamic awareness, and validate the generalizability of dynamic data. Furthermore, we propose PUMA, a dynamics-aware VLA architecture. By integrating scene-centric historical optical flow and specialized world queries to implicitly forecast object-centric future states, PUMA couples history-aware perception with short-horizon prediction. Results demonstrate that PUMA achieves state-of-the-art performance, yielding a 6.3% absolute improvement in success rate over baselines. Moreover, we show that training on dynamic data fosters robust spatiotemporal representations that transfer to static tasks. All code and data are available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/DOMINO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向动态环境的通用机器人操作研究
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在静态操作中表现优异，但在动态环境中处理移动目标时表现不佳。这一性能差距主要源于动态操作数据集的匮乏，以及主流VLA模型依赖单帧观测，限制了其时空推理能力。为解决此问题，我们提出了DOMINO，一个大规模通用动态操作数据集与基准，包含35个具有层次化复杂度的任务、超过11万条专家轨迹及多维评估体系。通过全面实验，我们系统评估现有VLA在动态任务中的表现，探索动态感知的有效训练策略，并验证动态数据的泛化能力。此外，我们提出PUMA，一种具备动态感知能力的VLA架构。通过融合场景中心的历史光流与专用世界查询，隐式预测物体中心的未来状态，PUMA将历史感知与短时预测相结合。实验表明，PUMA达到当前最优性能，在成功率上较基线提升6.3%。同时，我们证明在动态数据上训练可生成稳健的时空表征，且能迁移到静态任务。所有代码与数据可在https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/DOMINO获取。
