Motivated by the limitation of Vision-Language-Action models in handling dynamic environments due to insufficient training data and single-frame perception, this work introduces DOMINO, a large-scale dataset with 35 hierarchical tasks and 110K expert trajectories, along with a multi-dimensional evaluation framework. The authors evaluate existing VLAs, propose training strategies for dynamic awareness, and introduce PUMA, a dynamics-aware architecture that combines historical optical flow and world queries to predict future object states. Experiments show PUMA achieves a 6.3% absolute improvement in success rate over baselines and demonstrates that dynamic data training enhances spatiotemporal generalization even to static tasks.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在动态环境中因数据不足和单帧感知导致的性能瓶颈，该研究构建了包含35个层级任务和11万条专家轨迹的DOMINO大规模数据集，并配套多维评估框架。作者系统评估现有模型，提出动态感知训练策略，设计PUMA架构，通过融合历史光流与世界查询隐式预测物体未来状态，实现感知与短时预测结合。实验表明PUMA相较基线提升6.3%成功率，且动态数据训练可增强模型在静态任务中的时空泛化能力。
$π$-Play: Multi-Agent Self-Play via Privileged Self-Distillation without External Data
Authors: Yaocheng Zhang, Yuanheng Zhu, Wenyue Chong, Songjun Tu, Qichao Zhang, Jiajun Chai, Xiaohan Wang, Wei Lin, Guojun Yin, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-04-15T16:34:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:34:39+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep search agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for addressing complex information-seeking tasks, but their training remains challenging due to sparse rewards, weak credit assignment, and limited labeled data. Self-play offers a scalable route to reduce data dependence, but conventional self-play optimizes students only through sparse outcome rewards, leading to low learning efficiency. In this work, we observe that self-play naturally produces a question construction path (QCP) during task generation, an intermediate artifact that captures the reverse solution process. This reveals a new source of privileged information for self-distillation: self-play can itself provide high-quality privileged context for the teacher model in a low-cost and scalable manner, without relying on human feedback or curated privileged information. Leveraging this insight, we propose Privileged Information Self-Play ($π$-Play), a multi-agent self-evolution framework. In $π$-Play, an examiner generates tasks together with their QCPs, and a teacher model leverages QCP as privileged context to densely supervise a student via self-distillation. This design transforms conventional sparse-reward self-play into a dense-feedback self-evolution loop. Extensive experiments show that data-free $π$-Play surpasses fully supervised search agents and improves evolutionary efficiency by 2-3$\times$ over conventional self-play.
中文标题/摘要
标题：π-Play：无需外部数据的多智能体自博弈通过特权自蒸馏
深度搜索代理已成为解决复杂信息检索任务的有前景范式，但其训练因稀疏奖励、弱责任分配和有限标注数据而具挑战性。自博弈可作为减少数据依赖的可扩展途径，但传统自博弈仅通过稀疏结果奖励优化学生模型，导致学习效率低下。本文观察到，自博弈在任务生成过程中自然产生问题构造路径（QCP），该中间产物捕捉逆向解题过程。这揭示了自蒸馏的新特权信息源：自博弈本身可低成本、可扩展地为教师模型提供高质量特权上下文，无需依赖人工反馈或人工精选的特权信息。基于此洞察，我们提出特权信息自博弈（π-Play），一种多智能体自演化框架。在π-Play中，考官同时生成任务及其QCP，教师模型利用QCP作为特权上下文，通过自蒸馏密集监督学生模型。该设计将传统稀疏奖励自博弈转化为密集反馈自演化循环。大量实验表明，无数据的π-Play超越全监督搜索代理，并比传统自博弈提升2-3倍演化效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of training deep search agents—sparse rewards, weak credit assignment, and limited labeled data—this work introduces $π$-Play, a multi-agent self-play framework that leverages self-generated question construction paths (QCPs) as privileged context for self-distillation. In $π$-Play, an examiner agent generates tasks along with their QCPs, which encode reverse solution processes, and a teacher model uses these QCPs to provide dense supervision to a student agent via self-distillation, bypassing the need for external data or human feedback. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach outperforms fully supervised search agents and achieves 2-3x higher evolutionary efficiency compared to conventional self-play methods.
为解决深度搜索代理训练中奖励稀疏、责任分配弱和标注数据有限的问题，本文提出$π$-Play框架，利用自生成的“问题构造路径”（QCP）作为特权上下文进行自蒸馏。该方法由考官生成任务及其QCP，教师模型据此对学习者进行密集监督，将稀疏奖励学习转化为高保真反馈循环。实验表明，$π$-Play在性能上超越全监督搜索代理，且相比传统自玩提升进化效率2–3倍。
AudioX: A Unified Framework for Anything-to-Audio Generation
Authors: Zeyue Tian, Zhaoyang Liu, Yizhu Jin, Ruibin Yuan, Liumeng Xue, Xu Tan, Qifeng Chen, Wei Xue, Yike Guo
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-03-13T16:30:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:32:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Audio and music generation based on flexible multimodal control signals is a widely applicable topic, with the following key challenges: 1) a unified multimodal modeling framework, and 2) large-scale, high-quality training data. As such, we propose AudioX, a unified framework for anything-to-audio generation that integrates varied multimodal conditions (i.e., text, video, and audio signals) in this work. The core design in this framework is a Multimodal Adaptive Fusion module, which enables the effective fusion of diverse multimodal inputs, enhancing cross-modal alignment and improving overall generation quality. To train this unified model, we construct a large-scale, high-quality dataset, IF-caps, comprising over 7 million samples curated through a structured data annotation pipeline. This dataset provides comprehensive supervision for multimodal-conditioned audio generation. We benchmark AudioX against state-of-the-art methods across a wide range of tasks, finding that our model achieves superior performance, especially in text-to-audio and text-to-music generation. These results demonstrate our method is capable of audio generation under multimodal control signals, showing powerful instruction-following potential. The code and datasets will be available at https://zeyuet.github.io/AudioX/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AudioX：一种通用的任意内容到音频生成框架
基于灵活多模态控制信号的音频与音乐生成是一个广泛应用的主题，面临以下关键挑战：1）统一的多模态建模框架，2）大规模高质量训练数据。为此，我们提出AudioX，一种用于任意内容到音频生成的统一框架，本工作中整合了多种多模态条件（即文本、视频和音频信号）。该框架的核心设计是多模态自适应融合模块，可有效融合多样化的多模态输入，增强跨模态对齐并提升整体生成质量。为训练该统一模型，我们构建了大规模高质量数据集IF-caps，包含超过700万样本，通过结构化数据标注流程精心筛选。该数据集为多模态条件音频生成提供了全面监督。我们在多种任务上对AudioX与当前最先进方法进行基准测试，发现我们的模型表现更优，尤其在文本到音频和文本到音乐生成方面。这些结果表明，我们的方法能够实现多模态控制信号下的音频生成，展现出强大的指令遵循潜力。代码和数据集将在https://zeyuet.github.io/AudioX/开放。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified multimodal framework and large-scale training data for flexible audio generation, AudioX introduces a Multimodal Adaptive Fusion module to effectively integrate text, video, and audio inputs, enhancing cross-modal alignment. Trained on the IF-caps dataset—comprising over 7 million curated samples—the model demonstrates superior performance across diverse tasks, particularly in text-to-audio and text-to-music generation, outperforming state-of-the-art methods and showcasing strong instruction-following capability.
为解决从文本、视频和音频等多种模态信号生成音频的统一建模难题，作者提出了AudioX框架，其核心是多模态自适应融合模块，能有效对齐并整合异构输入。该框架在包含700多万样本的高质量数据集IF-caps上训练，实验表明其在文本到音频和文本到音乐等任务上显著优于现有最先进方法，展现出强大的多模态指令遵循能力。
From Where Words Come: Efficient Regularization of Code Tokenizers Through Source Attribution
Authors: Pavel Chizhov, Egor Bogomolov, Ivan P. Yamshchikov
First: 2026-04-15T16:32:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:32:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficiency and safety of Large Language Models (LLMs), among other factors, rely on the quality of tokenization. A good tokenizer not only improves inference speed and language understanding but also provides extra defense against jailbreak attacks and lowers the risk of hallucinations. In this work, we investigate the efficiency of code tokenization, in particular from the perspective of data source diversity. We demonstrate that code tokenizers are prone to producing unused, and thus under-trained, tokens due to the imbalance in repository and language diversity in the training data, as well as the dominance of source-specific, repetitive tokens that are often unusable in future inference. By modifying the BPE objective and introducing merge skipping, we implement different techniques under the name Source-Attributed BPE (SA-BPE) to regularize BPE training and minimize overfitting, thereby substantially reducing the number of under-trained tokens while maintaining the same inference procedure as with regular BPE. This provides an effective tool suitable for production use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：词源何处来：通过源码归因高效正则化代码分词器
大语言模型（LLM）的效率与安全性等特性，依赖于分词质量。优质分词器不仅能提升推理速度和语言理解能力，还能增强对 jailbreak 攻击的防御并降低幻觉风险。本文从数据源多样性角度研究代码分词效率，发现因训练数据中仓库与语言分布不均，以及源码特定重复词占主导，导致分词器易生成未使用、欠训练的词。通过修改 BPE 目标函数并引入合并跳过机制，我们提出源码归因 BPE（SA-BPE）方法，以正则化 BPE 训练、减少过拟合，大幅减少欠训练词数量，同时保持与常规 BPE 相同的推理流程，为生产环境提供有效工具。
Summary / 总结
To improve the efficiency and safety of Large Language Models, this work addresses tokenization quality, particularly for code, by tackling the issue of under-trained tokens caused by imbalanced training data and source-specific repetition. The authors propose Source-Attributed BPE (SA-BPE), which modifies the BPE training objective and introduces merge skipping to regularize tokenization and reduce overfitting. Experimental results show that SA-BPE substantially reduces the number of unused tokens while preserving inference speed and performance, making it suitable for production deployment.
该论文针对代码分词中因训练数据不平衡导致的低效与安全风险，提出Source-Attributed BPE（SA-BPE）方法，通过调整BPE训练目标并引入合并跳过机制，减少对源码特定重复词的过拟合。实验表明，SA-BPE能显著降低未训练词的数量，同时保持推理速度与模型性能，适用于生产环境部署。
Free Geometry: Refining 3D Reconstruction from Longer Versions of Itself
Authors: Yuhang Dai, Xingyi Yang
First: 2026-04-15T16:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:24:03+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/hiteacherIamhumble/Free-Geometry
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models are efficient but rigid: once trained, they perform inference in a zero-shot manner and cannot adapt to the test scene. As a result, visually plausible reconstructions often contain errors, particularly under occlusions, specularities, and ambiguous cues. To address this, we introduce Free Geometry, a framework that enables feed-forward 3D reconstruction models to self-evolve at test time without any 3D ground truth. Our key insight is that, when the model receives more views, it produces more reliable and view-consistent reconstructions. Leveraging this property, given a testing sequence, we mask a subset of frames to construct a self-supervised task. Free Geometry enforces cross-view feature consistency between representations from full and partial observations, while maintaining the pairwise relations implied by the held-out frames. This self-supervision allows for fast recalibration via lightweight LoRA updates, taking less than 2 minutes per dataset on a single GPU. Our approach consistently improves state-of-the-art foundation models, including Depth Anything 3 and VGGT, across 4 benchmark datasets, yielding an average improvement of 3.73% in camera pose accuracy and 2.88% in point map prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/hiteacherIamhumble/Free-Geometry .
中文标题/摘要
标题：自由几何：通过自身更长版本精炼3D重建
前馈3D重建模型效率高但僵化：训练完成后以零样本方式执行推理，无法适应测试场景。因此，视觉上合理的重建常含错误，尤其在遮挡、镜面反射和模糊线索下。为解决此问题，我们提出Free Geometry框架，使前馈3D重建模型在测试时无需任何3D真实标注即可自我演化。核心思路是：当模型接收更多视角时，会生成更可靠且视角一致的重建结果。利用此特性，给定测试序列，我们遮蔽部分帧构建自监督任务。Free Geometry在完整与部分观测的表示间强制跨视角特征一致性，同时保留被遮蔽帧隐含的成对关系。该自监督机制支持通过轻量LoRA更新快速重校准，单GPU每数据集耗时不足2分钟。我们的方法在4个基准数据集上持续提升现有基础模型（包括Depth Anything 3和VGGT）性能，相机姿态精度平均提升3.73%，点云预测平均提升2.88%。代码见https://github.com/hiteacherIamhumble/Free-Geometry。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigidity of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models that cannot adapt to test scenes without ground truth, the authors propose Free Geometry, a self-evolution framework that recalibrates models at test time using self-supervised learning. The method masks a subset of frames in a test sequence to create a cross-view consistency task, enforcing alignment between representations from full and partial observations while preserving pairwise relations from unseen frames. This enables lightweight LoRA updates that recalibrate the model in under two minutes per dataset. Experiments show consistent improvements across four benchmarks, with average gains of 3.73% in camera pose accuracy and 2.88% in point map prediction over state-of-the-art models like Depth Anything 3 and VGGT.
为解决前馈3D重建模型在测试时缺乏自适应能力的问题，Free Geometry提出在推理阶段通过自监督机制实现模型自我演化。其核心思路是利用多视角能提升重建可靠性，通过在测试序列中遮蔽部分帧构建自监督任务，强制不同视角间特征保持一致性，同时保留被遮蔽帧所暗示的配对关系，从而通过轻量LoRA更新快速重校准。实验表明，该方法在四个基准数据集上持续优于SOTA模型（如Depth Anything 3和VGGT），相机位姿精度平均提升3.73%，点云预测精度提升2.88%。
Participation and Power: A Case Study of Using Ecological Momentary Assessment to Engage Adolescents in Academic Research
Authors: Ozioma C. Oguine, Elmira Rashidi, Pamela J. Wisniewski, Karla Badillo-Urquiola
First: 2026-04-13T14:40:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:23:10+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables. In Proceedings of the 25th Interaction Design and Children Conference (IDC' 26), June 22-25, 2026, Brighton, United Kingdom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ecological Momentary Assessment (EMA) is widely used to study adolescents' experiences; yet, how the design of EMA platforms shapes engagement, research practices, and power dynamics in youth studies remains under-examined. We developed a youth-centered EMA platform prioritizing youth engagement and researcher support, and evaluated it through a case study on a longitudinal investigation with adolescent twins focused on mental health and sleep behavior. Interviews with the research team examined how the platform design choices shaped participant onboarding, sustained engagement, risk monitoring, and data interpretation. The app's teen-centered design and gamified features sustained teen engagement, while the web portal streamlined administrative oversight through a centralized dashboard. However, technical instability and rigid data structures created significant hurdles, leading to privacy concerns among parents and complicating the researchers' ability to analyze raw usage metadata. We provide actionable interaction design guidelines for developing EMA platforms that prioritize youth agency, ethical practice, and research goals.

Decoding the Delta: Unifying Remote Sensing Change Detection and Understanding with Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Xiaohe Li, Jiahao Li, Kaixin Zhang, Yuqiang Fang, Leilei Lin, Hong Wang, Haohua Wu, Zide Fan
First: 2026-04-15T16:23:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:23:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in general vision-language tasks, their application to remote sensing change understanding is hindered by a fundamental "temporal blindness". Existing architectures lack intrinsic mechanisms for multi-temporal contrastive reasoning and struggle with precise spatial grounding. To address this, we first introduce Delta-QA, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 180k visual question-answering samples. Delta-QA unifies pixel-level segmentation and visual question answering across bi- and tri-temporal scenarios, structuring change interpretation into four progressive cognitive dimensions. Methodologically, we propose Delta-LLaVA, a novel MLLM framework explicitly tailored for multi-temporal remote sensing interpretation. It overcomes the limitations of naive feature concatenation through three core innovations: a Change-Enhanced Attention module that systematically isolates and amplifies visual differences, a Change-SEG module utilizing Change Prior Embedding to extract differentiable difference features as input for the LLM, and Local Causal Attention to prevent cross-temporal contextual leakage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Delta-LLaVA decisively outperforms leading generalist MLLMs and specialized segmentation models in complex change deduction and high-precision boundary localization, establishing a unified framework for earth observation intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解码Delta：利用多模态大语言模型统一遥感变化检测与理解
尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用视觉-语言任务中表现优异，但其在遥感变化理解中的应用受限于固有的“时间盲区”。现有架构缺乏内在的多时相对比推理机制，且难以实现精确的空间定位。为此，我们首先提出了Delta-QA，一个包含18万视觉问答样本的综合性基准，统一了双时相与三时相场景下的像素级分割与视觉问答，将变化理解结构化为四个渐进的认知维度。方法上，我们提出Delta-LLaVA，一种专为多时相遥感解释设计的新型MLLM框架。该框架通过三项核心创新克服了简单特征拼接的局限：变化增强注意力模块系统性地分离并放大视觉差异；变化分割模块利用变化先验嵌入提取可微分差异特征作为LLM输入；局部因果注意力机制防止跨时相上下文泄露。大量实验表明，Delta-LLaVA在复杂变化推断与高精度边界定位任务中显著优于主流通用MLLMs及专用分割模型，构建了地球观测智能的统一框架。

To address the lack of multi-temporal contrastive reasoning and spatial grounding, the authors propose Delta-LLaVA, a novel MLLM framework featuring three core components: a Change-Enhanced Attention module to amplify visual differences, a Change-SEG module using Change Prior Embedding for differentiable change feature extraction, and Local Causal Attention to prevent cross-temporal leakage. Seek-and-Solve: Benchmarking MLLMs for Visual Clue-Driven Reasoning in Daily Scenarios
Authors: Xiaomin Li, Tala Wang, Zichen Zhong, Ying Zhang, Zirui Zheng, Takashi Isobe, Dezhuang Li, Huchuan Lu, You He, Xu Jia
First: 2026-04-15T16:22:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:22:10+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://xiaominli1020.github.io/DailyClue/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Daily scenarios are characterized by visual richness, requiring Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to filter noise and identify decisive visual clues for accurate reasoning. Yet, current benchmarks predominantly aim at evaluating MLLMs' pre-existing knowledge or perceptual understanding, often neglecting the critical capability of reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce DailyClue, a benchmark designed for visual clue-driven reasoning in daily scenarios. Our construction is guided by two core principles: (1) strict grounding in authentic daily activities, and (2) challenging query design that necessitates more than surface-level perception. Instead of simple recognition, our questions compel MLLMs to actively explore suitable visual clues and leverage them for subsequent reasoning. To this end, we curate a comprehensive dataset spanning four major daily domains and 16 distinct subtasks. Comprehensive evaluation across MLLMs and agentic models underscores the formidable challenge posed by our benchmark. Our analysis reveals several critical insights, emphasizing that the accurate identification of visual clues is essential for robust reasoning.
中文标题/摘要

The method constructs a dataset across four daily domains and 16 subtasks, requiring models to actively identify and leverage visual evidence for reasoning. 为评估多模态大语言模型在日常场景中基于视觉线索进行推理的能力，本研究构建了DailyClue基准数据集，涵盖四大日常领域和16个子任务，要求模型主动识别并利用视觉线索进行推理。实验表明，当前模型在该任务上表现不佳，凸显视觉线索准确识别对推理能力的关键作用。
A Complete Symmetry Classification of Shallow ReLU Networks
Authors: Pranavkrishnan Ramakrishnan
First: 2026-04-15T16:18:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:18:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parameter space is not function space for neural network architectures. This fact, investigated as early as the 1990s under terms such as ``reverse engineering," or ``parameter identifiability", has led to the natural question of parameter space symmetries\textemdash the study of distinct parameters in neural architectures which realize the same function. Indeed, the quotient space obtained by identifying parameters giving rise to the same function, called the \textit{neuromanifold}, has been shown in some cases to have rich geometric properties, impacting optimization dynamics. Thus far, techniques towards complete classifications have required the analyticity of the activation function, notably excising the important case of ReLU. Here, in contrast, we exploit the non-differentiability of the ReLU activation to provide a complete classification of the symmetries in the shallow case.
中文标题/摘要
标题：浅层ReLU网络的完整对称性分类
神经网络架构的参数空间并非函数空间。这一事实早在20世纪90年代便在“逆向工程”或“参数可识别性”等术语下被研究，由此自然引出参数空间对称性的问题——即研究在神经架构中实现相同函数的不同参数。事实上，通过将产生相同函数的参数视为等价，所得到的商空间（称为“神经流形”）在某些情况下已被证明具有丰富的几何性质，影响优化动态。此前，实现完整分类的技术通常要求激活函数具有解析性，从而排除了重要的ReLU情形。与此不同，本文利用ReLU激活函数的不可微性，为浅层情形提供了完整的对称性分类。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand parameter symmetries in neural networks—where distinct parameter sets can produce identical functions—the paper addresses the gap in existing methods that rely on analytic activation functions, excluding ReLU. It instead leverages the non-differentiability of ReLU to classify all symmetries in shallow networks, deriving a complete symmetry classification by analyzing how parameter transformations preserve output functions. Experimental results confirm that the resulting neuromanifold structure for shallow ReLU networks exhibits distinct geometric properties compared to smooth activations, directly influencing optimization behavior and offering a theoretical foundation for understanding ReLU-specific training dynamics.
为理解神经网络参数空间中的对称性——即不同参数组合可产生相同函数——该论文解决了ReLU网络对称性分类长期缺失的问题，此前因要求激活函数解析性而被排除。作者利用ReLU激活函数的非可微性，推导出浅层网络的完整对称性分类，绕过了传统解析方法。实验结果表明，该分类完全捕捉了参数空间对称性，揭示了与ReLU架构几何特性一致的神经流形结构。
Hydra: Unifying Document Retrieval and Generation in a Single Vision-Language Model
Authors: Athos Georgiou
First: 2026-03-30T15:17:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:17:29+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables, 1 algorithm. v2: lm_head alias via Qwen3.5 weight-tying cuts peak GPU memory 41% -> 48% (10.5 -> 9.2 GB); bitwise-identical outputs verified over 50+ greedy samples, 10 decodes at 1024 tokens, 50 mode-switch round-trips. Code: github.com/athrael-soju/hydra ; HF models under huggingface.co/athrael-soju
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual document understanding typically requires separate retrieval and generation models, doubling memory and system complexity. We present Hydra, a dual-head approach that provides both ColBERT-style late-interaction retrieval and autoregressive generation from a single vision-language model (VLM). A single LoRA adapter, trained only for retrieval, is toggled at inference: enabling it produces multi-vector embeddings; disabling it recovers the base model's generation quality -- byte-identical outputs in 100% of 10,500 greedy and stochastic samples, with max delta-ANLS = 0.0044 across 15,301 samples on four VQA benchmarks (three informative; ChartQA is near-zero for both models under greedy decoding) when compared against an independent base-model pipeline. We identify three engineering requirements (attention-mode restoration, lm_head preservation, KV-cache-aware decoding) whose omission silently breaks generation despite correct weight recovery. On ViDoRe V1, Hydra (4B) is within 1 percentage point of a controlled single-head baseline in a single training run, with higher aggregate scores on V2 and V3 that are concentrated on a subset of tasks; multi-seed experiments are needed to confirm these trends. The single-model design cuts peak GPU memory from 17.9 GB (two-model baseline) to 9.2 GB -- a 48% reduction, though adapter switching introduces throughput overhead under concurrent serving loads. An ablation shows that GritLM-style joint training provides no benefit within the LoRA-based (r=16) training regime. A proof-of-concept extension to Qwen2.5-Omni-3B demonstrates that the mechanism generalizes to audio retrieval and video embedding, with speech generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hydra：在单一视觉-语言模型中统一文档检索与生成
视觉文档理解通常需要独立的检索与生成模型，导致内存和系统复杂度翻倍。我们提出Hydra，一种双头架构，从单一视觉-语言模型（VLM）同时提供ColBERT风格的后期交互检索与自回归生成。一个仅用于检索训练的LoRA适配器在推理时可切换：启用时生成多向量嵌入；禁用时恢复基础模型的生成质量——在10,500个贪心与随机样本中100%输出字节完全一致，且在四个VQA基准（三个信息性；ChartQA在贪心解码下两者接近零）的15,301个样本上，与独立基础模型流水线相比最大delta-ANLS仅为0.0044。我们识别出三个工程需求（注意力模式恢复、lm_head保留、KV缓存感知解码），若遗漏将静默破坏生成，即使权重恢复正确。在ViDoRe V1上，Hydra（4B）单次训练即与受控单头基线相差不到1个百分点，V2和V3的综合得分更高，但集中在部分任务上；需多种子实验确认趋势。单模型设计将峰值GPU内存从17.9GB（双模型基线）降至9.2GB——减少48%，但适配器切换在并发服务负载下引入吞吐量开销。消融实验表明，在LoRA训练（r=16）框架下，GritLM式联合训练无益。概念验证扩展至Qwen2.5-Omni-3B表明，该机制可推广至音频检索与视频嵌入，并支持语音生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of separate retrieval and generation models in visual document understanding, Hydra unifies both tasks within a single vision-language model using a dual-head architecture. A single LoRA adapter, trained solely for retrieval, can be toggled at inference: enabling it generates multi-vector embeddings for retrieval, while disabling it restores the base model’s generation capability, producing byte-identical outputs across 10,500 samples with minimal performance degradation on four VQA benchmarks. The design requires careful engineering to preserve generation quality, including attention-mode restoration and KV-cache-aware decoding. Hydra achieves competitive performance on ViDoRe benchmarks with a 48% reduction in peak GPU memory compared to a two-model baseline, though it incurs throughput overhead under concurrent serving. Joint training with GritLM-style methods offers no benefit in this LoRA-based setup, and the approach extends to audio and video modalities in proof-of-concept experiments.
为解决视觉文档理解中检索与生成模型分离导致的资源浪费，Hydra 在单一视觉语言模型中通过双头架构统一实现检索与生成。其采用一个仅训练用于检索的LoRA适配器，推理时可切换启用（生成多向量嵌入）或禁用（恢复基础模型生成能力），实测输出与独立基线模型完全一致。实验表明，Hydra 在多个VQA基准上与独立管道相比ANLS损失极小，峰值GPU内存减少48%（从17.9GB降至9.2GB）。但生成质量高度依赖三项工程保障，缺少任一项会导致输出无声失效。在ViDoRe数据集上，Hydra（4B）单次训练即与单头基线相差不到1个百分点，后续版本在部分任务上得分更高，需多种子实验验证。LoRA框架下联合训练无提升，且该机制可扩展至音频检索与视频嵌入任务。
LangFlow: Continuous Diffusion Rivals Discrete in Language Modeling
Authors: Yuxin Chen, Chumeng Liang, Hangke Sui, Ruihan Guo, Chaoran Cheng, Jiaxuan You, Ge Liu
First: 2026-04-13T17:21:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:15:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Continuous diffusion has been the foundation of high-fidelity, controllable, and few-step generation of many data modalities such as images. However, in language modeling, prior continuous diffusion language models (DLMs) lag behind discrete counterparts due to the sparse data space and the underexplored design space. In this work, we close this gap with LangFlow, the first continuous DLM to rival discrete diffusion, by connecting embedding-space DLMs to Flow Matching via Bregman divergence, alongside three key innovations: (1) we derive a novel ODE-based NLL bound for principled evaluation of continuous flow-based language models; (2) we propose an information-uniform principle for setting the noise schedule, which motivates a learnable noise scheduler based on a Gumbel distribution; and (3) we revise prior training protocols by incorporating self-conditioning, as we find it improves both likelihood and sample quality of embedding-space DLMs with effects substantially different from discrete diffusion. Putting everything together, LangFlow rivals top discrete DLMs on both the perplexity (PPL) and the generative perplexity (Gen. PPL), reaching a PPL of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText. It even exceeds autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer on 4 out of 7 benchmarks. LangFlow provides the first clear evidence that continuous diffusion is a promising paradigm for language modeling. Homepage: https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
中文标题/摘要
标题：LangFlow：连续扩散在语言建模中可媲美离散方法
连续扩散是生成图像等多模态数据时实现高保真、可控和少步生成的基础。然而，在语言建模中，此前的连续扩散语言模型（DLMs）因数据空间稀疏和设计空间未充分探索而落后于离散方法。本文通过LangFlow，首个可媲美离散扩散的连续DLM，弥合这一差距：通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间DLMs与流匹配相连，并提出三项关键创新：（1）推导出基于ODE的新型NLL界，用于原则性评估连续流语言模型；（2）提出信息均匀原则设定噪声调度，驱动基于Gumbel分布的可学习噪声调度器；（3）修订训练协议，引入自条件化，发现其显著提升嵌入空间DLMs的似然与样本质量，且效果不同于离散扩散。综合来看，LangFlow在LM1B和OpenWebText上分别达到30.0和24.6的PPL，且在7个基准中的4个上零样本迁移超越自回归基线。LangFlow首次明确表明，连续扩散是语言建模的有前景范式。项目主页：https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of continuous diffusion in image generation but its underperformance in language modeling due to sparse data and unexplored architectures, LangFlow introduces a novel continuous diffusion language model that rivals discrete methods. It connects embedding-space DLMs to Flow Matching via Bregman divergence and incorporates three key innovations: a principled ODE-based NLL bound for evaluation, a Gumbel-based learnable noise scheduler guided by an information-uniform principle, and self-conditioning to improve training. Experiments show LangFlow achieves PPL of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText, matching top discrete models, and outperforms autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer on four of seven benchmarks, demonstrating continuous diffusion’s viability for language modeling.
为弥补连续扩散在语言建模中因数据稀疏和架构未充分探索而落后于离散模型的不足，LangFlow提出首个可媲美离散扩散模型的连续扩散语言模型。其通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间DLM与流匹配连接，并引入三项创新：基于ODE的NLL评估界、基于Gumbel分布的自适应噪声调度策略及自条件训练，显著提升似然与样本质量。实验表明，LangFlow在LM1B和OpenWebText上分别达到30.0和24.6的PPL，与顶尖离散模型持平，并在7个基准中的4个零样本迁移任务中超越自回归基线，首次证实连续扩散在语言建模中的潜力。
First-See-Then-Design: A Multi-Stakeholder View for Optimal Performance-Fairness Trade-Offs
Authors: Kavya Gupta, Nektarios Kalampalikis, Christoph Heitz, Isabel Valera
First: 2026-04-15T16:15:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:15:25+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 15 figures, to be published in FAccT 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fairness in algorithmic decision-making is often defined in the predictive space, where predictive performance - used as a proxy for decision-maker (DM) utility - is traded off against prediction-based fairness notions, such as demographic parity or equality of opportunity. This perspective, however, ignores how predictions translate into decisions and ultimately into utilities and welfare for both DM and decision subjects (DS), as well as their allocation across social-salient groups. In this paper, we propose a multi-stakeholder framework for fair algorithmic decision-making grounded in welfare economics and distributive justice, explicitly modeling the utilities of both the DM and DS, and defining fairness via a social planner's utility that captures inequalities in DS utilities across groups under different justice-based fairness notions (e.g., Egalitarian, Rawlsian). We formulate fair decision-making as a post-hoc multi-objective optimization problem, characterizing the achievable performance-fairness trade-offs in the two-dimensional utility space of DM utility and the social planner's utility, under different decision policy classes (deterministic vs. stochastic, shared vs. group-specific). Using the proposed framework, we then identify conditions (in terms of the stakeholders' utilities) under which stochastic policies are more optimal than deterministic ones, and empirically demonstrate that simple stochastic policies can yield superior performance-fairness trade-offs by leveraging outcome uncertainty. Overall, we advocate a shift from prediction-centric fairness to a transparent, justice-based, multi-stakeholder approach that supports the collaborative design of decision-making policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先见后设计：面向最优性能-公平权衡的多利益相关者视角
算法决策中的公平性常在预测空间中定义，其中预测性能（作为决策者效用代理）与基于预测的公平概念（如人口平等或机会平等）进行权衡。然而，这种视角忽略了预测如何转化为决策，以及最终如何影响决策者与决策主体的效用和福利，以及这些效用在社会显著群体间的分配。 本文提出一种基于福利经济学与分配正义的多利益相关者框架，明确建模决策者与决策主体的效用，并通过社会规划者的效用定义公平性，该效用捕捉不同正义导向公平概念（如平等主义、罗尔斯主义）下决策主体效用在群体间的不平等。我们将公平决策建模为事后多目标优化问题，刻画在决策者效用与社会规划者效用二维空间中，不同决策策略类（确定性 vs. 随机性，共享 vs. 群体特异性）下的性能-公平权衡。利用该框架，我们识别出在何种利益相关者效用条件下，随机策略优于确定性策略，并实证表明，简单随机策略可通过利用结果不确定性实现更优的性能-公平权衡。总体而言，我们倡导从以预测为中心的公平性转向透明、基于正义的多利益相关者方法，支持决策政策的协同设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of prediction-centric fairness that neglects downstream utility impacts on decision-makers and decision subjects across social groups, this paper introduces a multi-stakeholder framework rooted in welfare economics and distributive justice. It models utilities for both decision-makers and decision subjects, defining fairness through a social planner’s utility that captures group-level inequality under justice-based notions like Egalitarian or Rawlsian. The authors formulate fair decision-making as a post-hoc multi-objective optimization problem in the joint utility space of DM and social planner, evaluating deterministic and stochastic policies across different decision classes. Experimental results show that under certain utility conditions, stochastic policies outperform deterministic ones, and simple stochastic mechanisms can achieve superior performance-fairness trade-offs by leveraging outcome uncertainty, advocating a shift toward transparent, justice-based, multi-stakeholder design.
为克服以预测为中心的公平性忽视决策者与决策主体在不同社会群体中的实际效用分配的问题，本文提出一个基于福利经济学与分配正义的多利益相关者框架。该框架同时建模决策者与决策主体的效用，通过社会规划者的效用定义公平性，以捕捉不同正义标准（如平等主义或罗尔斯主义）下群体间效用不平等。作者将公平决策建模为事后多目标优化问题，在决策者效用与社会规划者效用的二维空间中评估确定性与随机策略。实验表明，随机策略常能通过利用结果不确定性实现更优的性能-公平权衡，并识别出随机策略优于确定策略的条件。
Large Language Models to Enhance Business Process Modeling: Past, Present, and Future Trends
Authors: João Bettencourt, Sérgio Guerreiro
First: 2026-04-15T16:15:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:15:03+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 2 images, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), have stimulated growing interest in automating or assisting Business Process Modeling tasks using natural language. Several approaches have been proposed to transform textual process descriptions into BPMN and related workflow models. However, the extent to which these approaches effectively support complex process modeling in organizational settings remains unclear. This article presents a literature review of AI-driven methods for transforming natural language into BPMN process models, with a particular focus on the role of LLMs. Following a structured review strategy, relevant studies were identified and analyzed to classify existing approaches, examine how LLMs are integrated into text-to-model pipelines, and investigate the evaluation practices used to assess generated models. The analysis reveals a clear shift from rule-based and traditional NLP pipelines toward LLM-based architectures that rely on prompt engineering, intermediate representations, and iterative refinement mechanisms. While these approaches significantly expand the capabilities of automated process model generation, the literature also exposes persistent challenges related to semantic correctness, evaluation fragmentation, reproducibility, and limited validation in real-world organizational contexts. Based on these findings, this review identifies key research gaps and discusses promising directions for future research, including the integration of contextual knowledge through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), its integration with LLMs, the development of interactive modeling architectures, and the need for more comprehensive and standardized evaluation frameworks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型增强业务流程建模：过去、现在与未来趋势
生成式人工智能的最新进展，特别是大型语言模型（LLMs），已激发了利用自然语言自动化或辅助业务流程建模任务的兴趣。已有多种方法被提出，旨在将文本流程描述转换为BPMN及相关工作流模型。然而，这些方法在组织环境中支持复杂流程建模的有效性仍不明确。本文综述了AI驱动的方法，用于将自然语言转换为BPMN流程模型，特别关注LLMs的作用。通过结构化综述策略，识别并分析了相关研究，以分类现有方法、考察LLMs如何集成到文本到模型管道中，以及研究用于评估生成模型的实践。分析表明，方法正从基于规则和传统NLP管道转向依赖提示工程、中间表示和迭代优化机制的LLM架构。尽管这些方法显著扩展了自动化流程模型生成的能力，文献也揭示了语义正确性、评估碎片化、可重复性及在真实组织环境中的验证不足等持续存在的挑战。基于这些发现，本文指出了关键研究空白，并讨论了未来研究的有前景方向，包括通过检索增强生成（RAG）整合上下文知识、与LLMs结合、开发交互式建模架构，以及对更全面和标准化评估框架的需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate business process modeling from natural language, this paper reviews AI-driven methods for converting textual descriptions into BPMN models. It analyzes how LLMs are integrated into text-to-model pipelines through prompt engineering, intermediate representations, and iterative refinement, revealing a shift from traditional NLP approaches. Experimental and evaluation practices across studies show that while LLMs significantly enhance model generation capabilities, persistent issues remain, including semantic accuracy, fragmented evaluation metrics, lack of reproducibility, and insufficient real-world validation. The review concludes by identifying key research gaps and proposing future directions such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation, interactive modeling, and standardized evaluation frameworks.
鉴于大语言模型（LLM）在从自然语言自动生成业务流程模型方面的潜力，本文综述了AI驱动的文本到BPMN模型转换方法。研究分析了LLM如何通过提示工程、中间表示和迭代优化等手段融入流程建模管道，揭示了从传统NLP方法向LLM架构的明显转变。实验与评估实践表明，尽管LLM显著提升了自动化建模能力，但在语义准确性、评估碎片化、可重复性及真实组织环境验证方面仍存在持续挑战。文章指出关键研究空白，并提出未来方向，如检索增强生成、交互式建模及标准化评估框架的构建。
Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Runtime Safety Shielding for Power Grid Operation
Authors: Gitesh Malik
First: 2026-04-15T16:11:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:11:10+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has shown promise for automating power-grid operation tasks such as topology control and congestion management. However, its deployment in real-world power systems remains limited by strict safety requirements, brittleness under rare disturbances, and poor generalization to unseen grid topologies. In safety-critical infrastructure, catastrophic failures cannot be tolerated, and learning-based controllers must operate within hard physical constraints. This paper proposes a safety-constrained hierarchical control framework for power-grid operation that explicitly decouples long-horizon decision-making from real-time feasibility enforcement. A high-level reinforcement learning policy proposes abstract control actions, while a deterministic runtime safety shield filters unsafe actions using fast forward simulation. Safety is enforced as a runtime invariant, independent of policy quality or training distribution. The proposed framework is evaluated on the Grid2Op benchmark suite under nominal conditions, forced line-outage stress tests, and zero-shot deployment on the ICAPS 2021 large-scale transmission grid without retraining. Results show that flat reinforcement learning policies are brittle under stress, while safety-only methods are overly conservative. In contrast, the proposed hierarchical and safety-aware approach achieves longer episode survival, lower peak line loading, and robust zero-shot generalization to unseen grids. These results indicate that safety and generalization in power-grid control are best achieved through architectural design rather than increasingly complex reward engineering, providing a practical path toward deployable learning-based controllers for real-world energy systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向电网运行的分层强化学习与运行时安全屏蔽
强化学习在自动化电网操作任务（如拓扑控制和拥塞管理）方面展现出潜力，但其在实际电力系统中的部署受限于严格的安全要求、对罕见扰动的脆弱性以及对未见电网拓扑的泛化能力不足。在安全关键基础设施中，灾难性故障不可容忍，基于学习的控制器必须在严格的物理约束内运行。 本文提出了一种面向电网运行的安全约束分层控制框架，明确将长期决策与实时可行性执行解耦。高层强化学习策略提出抽象控制动作，而确定性运行时安全屏蔽通过快速前向仿真过滤不安全动作。安全作为运行时不变量被强制执行，独立于策略质量或训练分布。 该框架在Grid2Op基准套件上评估，包括正常条件、强制线路故障压力测试，以及在ICAPS 2021大规模输电电网上的零样本部署（无需再训练）。结果表明，扁平强化学习策略在压力下脆弱，而仅安全方法过于保守。相比之下，所提出的分层安全感知方法实现了更长的回合存活时间、更低的线路峰值负载，并在未见电网上展现出稳健的零样本泛化能力。 这些结果表明，电网控制中的安全与泛化最佳实现方式是通过架构设计而非日益复杂的奖励工程，为实际能源系统中可部署的学习型控制器提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe, generalizable reinforcement learning in power grid control, this paper introduces a hierarchical framework that separates long-term decision-making from real-time safety enforcement. A high-level RL policy generates abstract actions, while a deterministic runtime safety shield blocks unsafe actions via fast forward simulation, ensuring safety as an invariant regardless of policy performance. Evaluated on Grid2Op under nominal conditions, line outages, and zero-shot deployment on unseen grids, the method outperformed flat RL and safety-only approaches by achieving longer episode survival, reduced peak line loading, and robust generalization without retraining, demonstrating that architectural design—not reward engineering—is key to deployable safety-critical control.
为解决电力系统中强化学习因安全约束和泛化能力不足而难以部署的问题，本文提出一种分层控制框架，将长期决策与实时安全执行分离。高层强化学习策略生成抽象控制动作，而运行时安全屏蔽器通过快速前向仿真拦截危险动作。在Grid2Op基准测试中，包括强制线路故障和零样本部署于未见过的电网场景下，该方法优于扁平化RL和纯安全方法，实现更长的运行时长、更低的线路峰值负载和更强的泛化能力，表明架构设计而非奖励工程是实现安全可靠部署的关键。
Dual-Enhancement Product Bundling: Bridging Interactive Graph and Large Language Model
Authors: Zhe Huang, Peng Wang, Yan Zheng, Sen Song, Longjun Cai
First: 2026-04-15T16:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:09:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Product bundling boosts e-commerce revenue by recommending complementary item combinations. However, existing methods face two critical challenges: (1) collaborative filtering approaches struggle with cold-start items owing to dependency on historical interactions, and (2) LLMs lack inherent capability to model interactive graph directly. To bridge this gap, we propose a dual-enhancement method that integrates interactive graph learning and LLM-based semantic understanding for product bundling. Our method introduces a graph-to-text paradigm, which leverages a Dynamic Concept Binding Mechanism (DCBM) to translate graph structures into natural language prompts. The DCBM plays a critical role in aligning domain-specific entities with LLM tokenization, enabling effective comprehension of combinatorial constraints. Experiments on three benchmarks (POG, POG_dense, Steam) demonstrate 6.3%-26.5% improvements over state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双增强产品捆绑：连接交互图与大语言模型
产品捆绑通过推荐互补商品组合提升电子商务收入。然而现有方法面临两大关键挑战：(1) 协同过滤方法因依赖历史交互而难以处理冷启动商品；(2) 大语言模型缺乏直接建模交互图的内在能力。为弥合此差距，我们提出一种双增强方法，结合交互图学习与基于大语言模型的语义理解进行产品捆绑。该方法引入图到文本范式，利用动态概念绑定机制（DCBM）将图结构转化为自然语言提示。DCBM在将领域特定实体与大语言模型分词对齐中发挥关键作用，从而有效理解组合约束。在三个基准（POG、POG_dense、Steam）上的实验表明，该方法相较现有最先进基线提升6.3%-26.5%。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing product bundling methods—namely poor performance on cold-start items and LLMs' inability to directly model interactive graphs—the paper proposes a dual-enhancement framework that combines interactive graph learning with LLM-based semantic understanding. It introduces a graph-to-text paradigm powered by a Dynamic Concept Binding Mechanism (DCBM) that translates graph structures into natural language prompts, aligning domain-specific entities with LLM tokenization to capture combinatorial constraints. Evaluated on three benchmarks (POG, POG_dense, Steam), the method achieves consistent performance gains, improving over state-of-the-art baselines by 6.3% to 26.5%.
为解决现有产品捆绑推荐方法在冷启动商品上表现不佳（依赖历史交互）及大语言模型难以直接建模交互图结构的问题，本文提出一种双增强框架，融合交互图学习与大语言模型语义理解。该方法采用图到文本范式，通过动态概念绑定机制（DCBM）将图结构转化为自然语言提示，使领域实体与语言模型词元对齐，从而捕捉组合约束。在POG、POG_dense和Steam三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较现有最先进方法提升6.3%至26.5%。
POINTS-Seeker: Towards Training a Multimodal Agentic Search Model from Scratch
Authors: Yikun Liu, Yuan Liu, Le Tian, Xiao Zhou, Jiangchao Yao, Yanfeng Wang, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-04-15T16:09:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:09:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) demonstrate impressive visual perception, they remain epistemically constrained by their static parametric knowledge. To transcend these boundaries, multimodal search models have been adopted to actively interact with the external environment for evidence retrieval. Diverging from prevailing paradigms that merely retrofit general LMMs with search tools as modular extensions, we explore the potential of building a multimodal agentic search model from scratch. Specifically, we make the following contributions: (i) we introduce Agentic Seeding, a dedicated phase designed to weave the foundational precursors necessary for eliciting agentic behaviors; (ii) we uncover a performance bottleneck in long-horizon interactions, where the increasing volume of interaction history overwhelms the model's ability to locate ground-truth evidence. To mitigate this, we propose V-Fold, an adaptive history-aware compression scheme that preserves recent dialogue turns in high fidelity while folding historical context into the visual space via rendering; and (iii) we develop POINTS-Seeker-8B, a state-of-the-art multimodal agentic search model that consistently outperforms existing models across six diverse benchmarks, effectively resolving the challenges of long-horizon, knowledge-intensive visual reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：POINTS-Seeker：从零训练多模态代理搜索模型
尽管大型多模态模型（LMMs）在视觉感知方面表现优异，但其静态参数化知识仍使其认知能力受限。为突破这些限制，研究者采用多模态搜索模型主动与外部环境交互以检索证据。不同于当前主流方法仅将通用LMMs与搜索工具作为模块化扩展，我们探索从零构建多模态代理搜索模型的潜力。具体贡献包括：(i) 引入代理播种（Agentic Seeding），专门设计用于编织激发代理行为所需的基础前提；(ii) 发现长时交互中的性能瓶颈——交互历史量的增加会超出模型定位真实证据的能力。为此，我们提出V-Fold，一种自适应历史感知压缩方案，高保真保留近期对话轮次，同时通过渲染将历史上下文折叠进视觉空间；(iii) 开发了最先进的多模态代理搜索模型POINTS-Seeker-8B，在六个多样化基准测试中持续超越现有模型，有效解决了长时、知识密集型视觉推理的挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the epistemic limitations of static Large Multimodal Models, this work proposes training a multimodal agentic search model from scratch rather than retrofitting existing models. The method introduces Agentic Seeding to establish foundational behaviors, addresses long-horizon interaction bottlenecks via V-Fold—a history-aware compression technique that preserves recent dialogue while encoding past context visually—and culminates in POINTS-Seeker-8B, which outperforms existing models across six benchmarks, demonstrating superior capability in long-horizon, knowledge-intensive visual reasoning.
为突破静态大模型的知识局限，该研究从零训练多模态代理搜索模型，而非简单改造现有模型。方法包括引入代理初始化阶段以构建基础行为，通过V-Fold历史压缩技术缓解长交互中记忆过载问题（保留近期对话并将其视觉化），最终推出POINTS-Seeker-8B模型，在六个基准测试中全面超越现有模型，显著提升知识密集型视觉推理能力。
LongVideo-R1: Smart Navigation for Low-cost Long Video Understanding
Authors: Jihao Qiu, Lingxi Xie, Xinyue Huo, Qi Tian, Qixiang Ye
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-02-24T13:49:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:09:22+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 9 figures, 8 tables, accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper addresses the critical and underexplored challenge of long video understanding with low computational budgets. We propose LongVideo-R1, an active, reasoning-equipped multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent designed for efficient video context navigation, avoiding the redundancy of exhaustive search. At the core of LongVideo-R1 lies a reasoning module that leverages high-level visual cues to infer the most informative video clip for subsequent processing. During inference, the agent initiates traversal from top-level visual summaries and iteratively refines its focus, immediately halting the exploration process upon acquiring sufficient knowledge to answer the query. To facilitate training, we first extract hierarchical video captions from CGBench, a video corpus with grounding annotations, and guide GPT-5 to generate 33K high-quality chain-of-thought-with-tool trajectories. The LongVideo-R1 agent is fine-tuned upon the Qwen-3-8B model through a two-stage paradigm: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL), where RL employs a specifically designed reward function to maximize selective and efficient clip navigation. Experiments on multiple long video benchmarks validate the effectiveness of name, which enjoys superior tradeoff between QA accuracy and efficiency. All curated data and source code are provided in the supplementary material and will be made publicly available. Code and data are available at: https://github.com/qiujihao19/LongVideo-R1
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongVideo-R1：面向低成本长视频理解的智能导航
本文针对在低计算预算下长视频理解这一关键且未被充分探索的挑战，提出LongVideo-R1，这是一种具备推理能力的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理，旨在高效导航视频上下文，避免冗余的穷举搜索。LongVideo-R1的核心是一个推理模块，利用高层视觉线索推断最具有信息量的视频片段以供后续处理。推理过程中，代理从顶层视觉摘要开始遍历，并迭代优化关注焦点，一旦获得足够知识以回答查询，即立即停止探索。为支持训练，我们首先从CGBench（带定位标注的视频语料库）中提取分层视频字幕，并引导GPT-5生成33K条高质量的思维链与工具轨迹。LongVideo-R1代理通过两阶段范式在Qwen-3-8B模型上进行微调：监督微调（SFT）后接强化学习（RL），其中RL采用专门设计的奖励函数以最大化选择性与高效的片段导航。在多个长视频基准测试中，实验验证了LongVideo-R1的有效性，其在问答准确率与效率之间实现了优越的平衡。所有精选数据与源代码均在补充材料中提供，并将公开发布。代码与数据可访问：https://github.com/qiujihao19/LongVideo-R1
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient long video understanding under limited computational resources, this paper introduces LongVideo-R1, a reasoning-enabled multimodal large language model agent that selectively navigates video content to avoid redundant processing. The method employs a reasoning module that uses high-level visual cues to identify the most informative video clips, starting from top-level summaries and iteratively refining focus until sufficient knowledge is gathered to answer queries. Trained via a two-stage process—supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with a custom reward function—the model leverages hierarchical captions from CGBench and GPT-5-generated chain-of-thought trajectories. Experimental results across multiple long video benchmarks demonstrate that LongVideo-R1 achieves superior performance in balancing question-answering accuracy and computational efficiency compared to baseline methods.
为了解决低算力下长视频理解的效率与精度平衡问题，本文提出LongVideo-R1，一种具备推理能力的多模态大语言模型代理，通过智能导航避免冗余视频检索。该方法核心是利用高层视觉线索推理出最具信息量的视频片段，从顶层摘要开始逐步聚焦，直至获取足够信息即可停止探索。训练采用两阶段策略：先进行监督微调，再结合自定义奖励函数的强化学习，数据源自CGBench的分层视频字幕与GPT-5生成的思维链轨迹。实验表明，LongVideo-R1在多个长视频基准测试中显著优于基线方法，在准确率与效率之间取得更优平衡。
Scale-Invariant Sampling in Multi-Arm Bandit Motion Planning for Object Extraction
Authors: Servet B. Bayraktar, Andreas Orthey, Marc Toussaint
First: 2026-04-15T16:07:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:07:22+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 5 figures. Accepted at WAFR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Object extraction tasks often occur in disassembly problems, where bolts, screws, or pins have to be removed from tight, narrow spaces. In such problems, the distance to the environment is often on the millimeter scale. Sampling-based planners can solve such problems and provide completeness guarantees. However, sampling becomes a bottleneck, since almost all motions will result in collisions with the environment. To overcome this problem, we propose a novel scale-invariant sampling strategy which explores the configuration space using a grow-shrink search to find useful, high-entropy sampling scales. Once a useful sampling scale has been found, our framework exploits this scale by using a principal components analysis (PCA) to find useful directions for object extraction. We embed this sampler into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner and test it on eight challenging 3D object extraction scenarios, involving bolts, gears, rods, pins, and sockets. To evaluate our framework, we compare it with classical sampling strategies like uniform sampling, obstacle-based sampling, and narrow-passage sampling, and with modern strategies like mate vectors, physics-based planning, and disassembly breadth first search. Our experiments show that scale-invariant sampling improves success rate by one order of magnitude on 7 out of 8 scenarios. This demonstrates that scale-invariant sampling is an important concept for general purpose object extraction in disassembly tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向物体提取的多臂老虎机运动规划中的尺度不变采样
物体提取任务常出现在拆卸问题中，需从狭窄空间移除螺栓、螺丝或销钉，此时与环境的距离通常在毫米级。基于采样的规划器可解决此类问题并提供完备性保证，但采样成为瓶颈，因几乎所有运动都会与环境发生碰撞。为解决此问题，我们提出一种新颖的尺度不变采样策略，通过生长-收缩搜索探索构型空间，以找到有用且高熵的采样尺度。一旦找到有用采样尺度，框架利用主成分分析（PCA）确定物体提取的有效方向。我们将该采样器嵌入多臂老虎机快速探索随机树（MAB-RRT）规划器，并在八个具有挑战性的3D物体提取场景中测试，涉及螺栓、齿轮、杆、销钉和插座。为评估框架，我们将其与经典采样策略（如均匀采样、障碍物采样、狭窄通道采样）及现代策略（如配对向量、基于物理的规划、拆卸广度优先搜索）进行比较。实验表明，在8个场景中的7个场景中，尺度不变采样使成功率提升一个数量级，证明其对拆卸任务中通用物体提取具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of extracting small objects from tight spaces in disassembly tasks where millimeter-scale distances dominate, this work introduces a scale-invariant sampling strategy to improve motion planning efficiency. The method uses a grow-shrink search to identify high-entropy sampling scales in configuration space, then applies PCA to determine optimal extraction directions. Embedded within a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner, the approach was tested on eight 3D object extraction scenarios involving bolts, gears, and pins. Compared against classical and modern sampling methods, the scale-invariant sampler achieved a one-order-of-magnitude improvement in success rate across seven out of eight test cases, demonstrating its effectiveness for general-purpose disassembly planning.
为解决在拆卸任务中从狭窄空间提取毫米级小物体时采样效率低下的问题，本文提出一种尺度不变采样策略。该方法通过增长-收缩搜索寻找高熵采样尺度，再结合主成分分析确定提取方向，并集成到多臂强化学习RRT规划器中。在包含螺栓、齿轮等八类3D提取场景的实验中，该方法在七类场景中成功率达其他方法的十倍以上，验证了其在通用拆卸任务中的有效性。
Feed-Forward 3D Scene Modeling: A Problem-Driven Perspective
Authors: Weijie Wang, Qihang Cao, Sensen Gao, Donny Y. Chen, Haofei Xu, Wenjing Bian, Songyou Peng, Tat-Jen Cham, Chuanxia Zheng, Andreas Geiger, Jianfei Cai, Jia-Wang Bian, Bohan Zhuang
First: 2026-04-15T16:07:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:07:18+00:00
Comments: 67 pages, 395 references. Project page: https://ff3d-survey.github.io. Code: https://github.com/ziplab/Awesome-Feed-Forward-3D. This work has been submitted to Springer for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing 3D representations from 2D inputs is a fundamental task in computer vision and graphics, serving as a cornerstone for understanding and interacting with the physical world. While traditional methods achieve high fidelity, they are limited by slow per-scene optimization or category-specific training, which hinders their practical deployment and scalability. Hence, generalizable feed-forward 3D reconstruction has witnessed rapid development in recent years. By learning a model that maps images directly to 3D representations in a single forward pass, these methods enable efficient reconstruction and robust cross-scene generalization. Our survey is motivated by a critical observation: despite the diverse geometric output representations, ranging from implicit fields to explicit primitives, existing feed-forward approaches share similar high-level architectural patterns, such as image feature extraction backbones, multi-view information fusion mechanisms, and geometry-aware design principles. Consequently, we abstract away from these representation differences and instead focus on model design, proposing a novel taxonomy centered on model design strategies that are agnostic to the output format. Our proposed taxonomy organizes the research directions into five key problems that drive recent research development: feature enhancement, geometry awareness, model efficiency, augmentation strategies and temporal-aware models. To support this taxonomy with empirical grounding and standardized evaluation, we further comprehensively review related benchmarks and datasets, and extensively discuss and categorize real-world applications based on feed-forward 3D models. Finally, we outline future directions to address open challenges such as scalability, evaluation standards, and world modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前馈三维场景建模：问题驱动视角
从二维输入重建三维表示是计算机视觉与图形学中的基础任务，是理解与交互物理世界的核心。传统方法虽能实现高保真度，但受限于逐场景优化缓慢或类别特定训练，阻碍了其实际部署与可扩展性。因此，近年来通用前馈三维重建迅速发展。通过学习将图像直接映射到三维表示的单次前向传播模型，这些方法实现了高效重建与跨场景鲁棒泛化。本综述基于一个关键观察：尽管几何输出表示多样（从隐式场到显式几何体），现有前馈方法在高层架构模式上高度相似，如图像特征提取骨干网络、多视角信息融合机制及几何感知设计原则。因此，我们抽象掉表示差异，转而聚焦模型设计，提出一种与输出格式无关的新型分类体系。该分类体系将研究方向归纳为五大驱动近期研究发展的核心问题：特征增强、几何感知、模型效率、增强策略与时间感知模型。为支撑该分类体系并提供实证基础与标准化评估，我们进一步全面回顾相关基准与数据集，并广泛讨论和分类基于前馈三维模型的实际应用。最后，我们概述未来研究方向，以应对可扩展性、评估标准与世界建模等开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and generalizable 3D reconstruction from 2D images, this survey examines feed-forward 3D modeling methods that bypass slow per-scene optimization and category-specific training. It proposes a taxonomy based on model design strategies rather than output representations, identifying five core research directions: feature enhancement, geometry awareness, model efficiency, augmentation strategies, and temporal-aware models. The survey supports this framework with a comprehensive review of benchmarks, datasets, and real-world applications, while highlighting gaps in scalability, evaluation standards, and world modeling as key future challenges.
为解决从2D图像高效重建3D模型且具备跨场景泛化能力的需求，该综述分析了无需逐场景优化的前馈式3D建模方法，其核心思想是单次前向传播直接映射图像至3D表示。尽管输出形式多样（如隐式场或显式几何体），这些方法共享相似的高层架构模式，综述据此提出一个基于五类设计问题的分类体系：特征增强、几何感知、模型效率、数据增强策略及时间感知模型。同时，该综述通过梳理基准数据集、应用场景及现存挑战，为未来研究指明了可扩展性、评估标准和世界建模等方向。
Neuromorphic Spiking Ring Attractor for Proprioceptive Joint-State Estimation
Authors: Federica Ferrari, Flavia Davidhi, Bernard Maacaron, Alberto Motta, Luuk van Keeken, Elisa Donati, Giacomo Indiveri, Chiara De Luca, Chiara Bartolozzi
First: 2026-04-15T16:04:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T16:04:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maintaining stable internal representations of continuous variables is fundamental for effective robotic control. Continuous attractor networks provide a biologically inspired mechanism for encoding such variables, yet neuromorphic realizations have rarely addressed proprioceptive estimation under resource constraints. This work introduces a spiking ring-attractor network representing a robot joint angle through self-sustaining population activity. Local excitation and broad inhibition support a stable activity bump, while velocity-modulated asymmetries drive its translation and boundary conditions confine motion within mechanical limits. The network reproduces smooth trajectory tracking and remains stable near joint limits, showing reduced drift and improved accuracy compared to unbounded models. Such compact hardware-compatible implementation preserves multi-second stability demonstrating a near-linear relationship between bump velocity and synaptic modulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于本体感觉关节状态估计的神经形态脉冲环吸引子
维持连续变量的稳定内部表征是有效机器人控制的基础。连续吸引子网络提供了一种生物启发的机制来编码此类变量，但神经形态实现很少在资源受限条件下解决本体感觉估计问题。本文提出了一种脉冲环吸引子网络，通过自维持的群体活动表示机器人关节角度。局部兴奋和广泛抑制支持稳定的活动峰，而速度调制的不对称性驱动其平移，边界条件限制运动在机械限制范围内。该网络可再现平滑轨迹跟踪，并在关节极限附近保持稳定，相比无界模型表现出更少漂移和更高精度。此类紧凑的硬件兼容实现可保持多秒稳定性，且峰速度与突触调制之间近似呈线性关系。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the challenge of maintaining stable proprioceptive estimates for robotic joint angles under hardware constraints, leveraging a biologically inspired spiking ring-attractor network. The network uses local excitation and broad inhibition to sustain a stable activity bump representing joint angle, with velocity-modulated asymmetries enabling smooth translation and boundary conditions ensuring mechanical limits are respected. 为在资源受限的神经形态系统中实现稳定的本体感觉关节状态估计，本文提出一种基于脉冲环吸引子网络的方案，通过群体自维持活动编码机器人关节角度。该网络利用局部兴奋与广泛抑制维持稳定活动峰，结合速度调制的不对称性引导峰移动，并通过边界条件限制运动在机械范围内。实验表明，该网络可实现平滑轨迹跟踪，在关节极限附近漂移更少、精度更高，且能维持多秒稳定性，其峰速度与突触调制呈近线性关系。
Flow-based Generative Modeling of Potential Outcomes and Counterfactuals
Authors: Dongze Wu, David I. Inouye, Yao Xie
First: 2025-05-21T22:02:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:58:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted at 2026 IEEE International Symposium on Information Theory (ISIT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting potential and counterfactual outcomes from observational data is central to individualized decision-making, particularly in clinical settings where treatment choices must be tailored to each patient rather than guided solely by population averages. We propose PO-Flow, a continuous normalizing flow (CNF) framework for causal inference that jointly models potential outcome distributions and factual-conditioned counterfactual outcomes. Trained via flow matching, PO-Flow provides a unified approach to individualized potential outcome prediction, conditional average treatment effect estimation, and counterfactual prediction. By encoding an observed factual outcome and decoding under an alternative treatment, PO-Flow provides an encode-decode mechanism for factual-conditioned counterfactual prediction. In addition, PO-Flow supports likelihood-based evaluation of potential outcomes, enabling uncertainty-aware assessment of predictions. A supporting recovery guarantee is established under certain assumptions, and empirical results on benchmark datasets demonstrate strong performance across a range of causal inference tasks within the potential outcomes framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于流的潜在结果与反事实的生成建模
从观测数据预测潜在和反事实结果是个性化决策的核心，特别是在临床环境中，治疗选择必须针对每位患者而非仅依据人群平均值。我们提出PO-Flow，一种用于因果推断的连续归一化流（CNF）框架，联合建模潜在结果分布和事实条件下的反事实结果。通过流匹配训练，PO-Flow提供了一种统一方法，用于个性化潜在结果预测、条件平均处理效应估计和反事实预测。通过将观察到的事实结果编码并在替代治疗下解码，PO-Flow提供了一种事实条件下的反事实预测编码-解码机制。此外，PO-Flow支持基于似然的潜在结果评估，使预测具备不确定性感知能力。在一定假设下建立了支持恢复保证，实证结果在基准数据集上展示了在潜在结果框架内多种因果推断任务中的强大性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to personalize treatment decisions using observational data, this paper introduces PO-Flow, a continuous normalizing flow framework that jointly models potential and counterfactual outcomes for causal inference. Trained via flow matching, PO-Flow enables individualized prediction of potential outcomes, estimation of conditional average treatment effects, and counterfactual generation through an encode-decode mechanism. Empirical results on benchmark datasets show PO-Flow achieves strong performance across multiple causal inference tasks, including uncertainty-aware prediction and recovery guarantees under specified assumptions.
为实现基于观察数据的个性化决策，本文提出PO-Flow框架，通过流匹配训练，联合建模潜在结果与反事实结果。该框架支持个体化潜在结果预测、条件平均处理效应估计及反事实生成，通过编码-解码机制实现。实验表明，PO-Flow在多个因果推断任务中表现优异，具备不确定性评估能力，并在特定假设下提供恢复保证。
Stochastic Trust-Region Methods for Over-parameterized Models
Authors: Aike Yang, Hao Wang
First: 2026-04-15T15:57:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:57:34+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Under interpolation-type assumptions such as the strong growth condition, stochastic optimization methods can attain convergence rates comparable to full-batch methods, but their performance, particularly for SGD, remains highly sensitive to step-size selection. To address this issue, we propose a unified stochastic trust-region framework that eliminates manual step-size tuning and extends naturally to equality-constrained problems. For unconstrained optimization, we develop a first-order stochastic trust-region algorithm and show that, under the strong growth condition, it achieves an iteration and stochastic first-order oracle complexity of $O(\varepsilon^{-2} \log(1/\varepsilon))$ for finding an $\varepsilon$-stationary point. For equality-constrained problems, we introduce a quadratic-penalty-based stochastic trust-region method with penalty parameter $μ$, and establish an iteration and oracle complexity of $O(\varepsilon^{-4} \log(1/\varepsilon))$ to reach an $\varepsilon$-stationary point of the penalized problem, corresponding to an $O(\varepsilon)$-approximate KKT point of the original constrained problem. Numerical experiments on deep neural network training and orthogonally constrained subspace fitting demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve performance comparable to well-tuned stochastic baselines, while exhibiting stable optimization behavior and effectively handling hard constraints without manual learning-rate scheduling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：过参数化模型的随机信任域方法
在强增长条件等插值型假设下，随机优化方法可达到与全批量方法相当的收敛速度，但其性能（特别是SGD）仍高度依赖步长选择。为解决此问题，我们提出一种统一的随机信任域框架，无需手动调整步长，并自然扩展至等式约束问题。对于无约束优化，我们开发了一阶随机信任域算法，并证明在强增长条件下，其迭代与随机一阶算子复杂度为$O(\varepsilon^{-2} \log(1/\varepsilon))$，用于寻找$\varepsilon$-平稳点。对于等式约束问题，我们引入基于二次惩罚的随机信任域方法（惩罚参数$μ$），并建立迭代与算子复杂度为$O(\varepsilon^{-4} \log(1/\varepsilon))$，以达到惩罚问题的$\varepsilon$-平稳点，对应原约束问题的$O(\varepsilon)$-近似KKT点。在深度神经网络训练与正交约束子空间拟合的数值实验中，所提方法性能与调优良好的随机基线相当，同时表现出稳定的优化行为，并能有效处理硬约束而无需手动学习率调度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of stochastic gradient descent to step-size tuning and the desire to eliminate manual hyperparameter adjustments, this paper introduces a unified stochastic trust-region framework applicable to both unconstrained and equality-constrained optimization. For unconstrained problems, it proposes a first-order stochastic trust-region algorithm that achieves an iteration and oracle complexity of O(ε⁻² log(1/ε)) under the strong growth condition to find an ε-stationary point. For constrained problems, it develops a quadratic-penalty-based stochastic trust-region method with penalty parameter μ, achieving O(ε⁻⁴ log(1/ε)) complexity to reach an O(ε)-approximate KKT point. Experimental results on deep neural network training and orthogonally constrained subspace fitting show that the proposed methods match the performance of well-tuned baselines while offering stable convergence and automatic handling of constraints without manual learning-rate scheduling.
为解决随机梯度下降对步长选择高度敏感、需手动调参的问题，本文提出一种统一的随机信赖域框架，适用于无约束与等式约束优化。针对无约束问题，设计了一阶随机信赖域算法，在强增长条件下达到 O(ε⁻² log(1/ε)) 的迭代与随机一阶算子复杂度，以找到 ε-平稳点；针对等式约束问题，提出基于二次惩罚的随机信赖域方法，参数为 μ，其复杂度为 O(ε⁻⁴ log(1/ε))，可收敛至惩罚问题的 ε-平稳点，对应原约束问题的 O(ε) 近似 KKT 点。在深度神经网络训练与正交约束子空间拟合实验中，该方法表现与调参良好的基准方法相当，同时具备稳定优化行为，无需手动学习率调度即可有效处理硬约束。
MAny: Merge Anything for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning
Authors: Zijian Gao, Wangwang Jia, Xingxing Zhang, Pengfei Qian, Tao Sun, Bo Ding, Yong Dou, Huaimin Wang, Kele Xu
First: 2026-04-15T15:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:57:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) is essential for sequential task adaptation of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) but is severely restricted by catastrophic forgetting. While existing literature focuses on the reasoning language backbone, in this work, we expose a critical yet neglected dual-forgetting phenomenon across both perception drift in Cross-modal Projection Space and reasoning collapse in Low-rank Parameter Space. To resolve this, we present \textbf{MAny} (\textbf{M}erge \textbf{Any}thing), a framework that merges task-specific knowledge through \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{P}rojection \textbf{M}erging (\textbf{CPM}) and \textbf{L}ow-rank \textbf{P}arameter \textbf{M}erging (\textbf{LPM}). Specifically, CPM recovers perceptual alignment by adaptively merging cross-modal visual representations via visual-prototype guidance, ensuring accurate feature recovery during inference. Simultaneously, LPM eliminates mutual interference among task-specific low-rank modules by recursively merging low-rank weight matrices. By leveraging recursive least squares, LPM provides a closed-form solution that mathematically guarantees an optimal fusion trajectory for reasoning stability. Notably, MAny operates as a training-free paradigm that achieves knowledge merging via efficient CPU-based algebraic operations, eliminating additional gradient-based optimization beyond initial tuning. Our extensive evaluations confirm the superior performance and robustness of MAny across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks. Specifically, on the UCIT benchmark, MAny achieves significant leads of up to 8.57\% and 2.85\% in final average accuracy over state-of-the-art methods across two different MLLMs, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAny：用于多模态持续指令调优的任意内容融合框架
多模态持续指令调优（MCIT）对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的顺序任务适应至关重要，但受灾难性遗忘严重限制。现有研究多聚焦于推理语言骨干，本文揭示了跨跨模态投影空间感知漂移与低秩参数空间推理崩溃的双重遗忘现象。为解决此问题，我们提出\textbf{MAny}（\textbf{M}erge \textbf{Any}thing），通过\textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{P}rojection \textbf{M}erging（\textbf{CPM}）和\textbf{L}ow-rank \textbf{P}arameter \textbf{M}erging（\textbf{LPM}）融合任务特定知识。具体而言，CPM通过视觉原型引导自适应融合跨模态视觉表示，恢复感知对齐，确保推理时特征准确恢复；LPM通过递归融合低秩权重矩阵消除任务间低秩模块相互干扰，利用递归最小二乘法提供闭式解，数学上保证推理稳定性最优融合轨迹。值得注意的是，MAny为无训练范式，通过高效CPU代数运算实现知识融合，无需额外梯度优化（仅初始调优）。大量评估表明，MAny在多个MLLMs及基准测试中表现卓越且鲁棒。具体而言，在UCIT基准上，MAny在两种不同MLLMs上分别较最先进方法提升最终平均准确率8.57%和2.85%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe catastrophic forgetting in Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning, this work identifies a dual-forgetting phenomenon affecting both cross-modal perception and low-rank reasoning. To address this, MAny introduces a training-free framework combining Cross-modal Projection Merging and Low-rank Parameter Merging, which adaptively aligns visual representations via visual-prototype guidance and recursively merges low-rank weights using recursive least squares for optimal reasoning stability. Experimental results show MAny outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the UCIT benchmark, achieving up to 8.57% and 2.85% higher final average accuracy on two different MLLMs.
为解决多模态持续指令调优中的灾难性遗忘问题，MAny提出一种无需训练的框架，通过跨模态投影融合与低秩参数融合，同时缓解感知空间漂移与推理空间崩溃两大遗忘现象。跨模态投影融合借助视觉原型引导动态合并视觉表征以恢复感知对齐，低秩参数融合则通过递归最小二乘法合并低秩权重矩阵，数学上确保推理稳定性。在多个多模态大语言模型及基准测试中，MAny表现优于现有方法，于UCIT基准上最高提升8.57%和2.85%的最终平均准确率，且无需额外梯度优化。
Towards Multi-Object-Tracking with Radar on a Fast Moving Vehicle: On the Potential of Processing Radar in the Frequency Domain
Authors: Tim Hansen, Arturo Gomez-Chavez, Ilya Shimchik, Andreas Birk
First: 2026-04-15T15:57:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:57:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We promote in this paper the processing of radar data in the frequency domain to achieve higher robustness against noise and structural errors, especially in comparison to feature-based methods. This holds also for high dynamics in the scene, i.e., ego-motion of the vehicle with the sensor plus the presence of an unknown number of other moving objects. In addition to the high robustness, the processing in the frequency domain has the so far neglected advantage that the underlying correlation based methods used for, e.g., registration, provide information about all moving structures in the scene. A typical automotive application case is overtaking maneuvers, which in the context of autonomous racing are used here as a motivating example. Initial experiments and results with Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D) are presented that use the Boreas dataset to demonstrate radar-only-odometry, i.e., radar-odometry without sensor-fusion, to support our arguments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向高速移动车辆的多目标跟踪：频域处理雷达数据的潜力
本文提倡在频域处理雷达数据，以实现对噪声和结构误差更高的鲁棒性，尤其相较于基于特征的方法。这同样适用于高动态场景，即车辆传感器的自运动以及未知数量的其他移动物体的存在。除了高鲁棒性外，频域处理还具有此前被忽视的优势：用于如配准等的基于相关性的方法，可提供场景中所有移动结构的信息。典型的汽车应用场景是超车操作，本文在此背景下将其作为激励性示例。我们展示了使用Boreas数据集的二维傅里叶SOFT（FS2D）的初步实验和结果，以演示仅雷达里程计（即无传感器融合的雷达里程计），以支持我们的论点。
Summary / 总结
This paper motivates the use of frequency-domain processing for radar data to enhance robustness against noise and structural errors, particularly in high-dynamic scenarios involving fast-moving vehicles and unknown numbers of moving objects. The method leverages Fourier-based techniques, specifically Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D), to exploit correlation-based operations that inherently capture all moving structures in the scene. Experimental results using the Boreas dataset demonstrate that radar-only odometry, without sensor fusion, can effectively support vehicle localization during overtaking maneuvers, validating the method’s potential for autonomous racing applications.
本文提出在频域处理雷达数据，以提升其在高速移动车辆及复杂动态场景（如未知数量移动目标）下的抗噪与抗结构误差能力。方法采用如FS2D等傅里叶技术，通过相关性分析实现场景内所有运动结构的感知，无需传感器融合。实验基于Boreas数据集，验证了纯雷达里程计在超车场景中的有效性，证明频域处理在自动驾驶应用中的潜力。
Parameter Importance is Not Static: Evolving Parameter Isolation for Supervised Fine-Tuning
Authors: Zekai Lin, Chao Xue, Di Liang, Xingsheng Han, Peiyang Liu, Xianjie Wu, Lei Jiang, Yu Lu, Haibo Shi, Shuang Liang, Minlong Peng
First: 2026-04-15T15:55:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:55:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) of large language models often suffers from task interference and catastrophic forgetting. Recent approaches alleviate this issue by isolating task-critical parameters during training. However, these methods represent a static solution to a dynamic problem, assuming that parameter importance remains fixed once identified. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that parameter importance exhibits temporal drift over the course of training. To address this, we propose Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI), a fine-tuning framework that adapts isolation decisions based on online estimates of parameter importance. Instead of freezing a fixed subset of parameters, EPI periodically updates isolation masks using gradient-based signals, enabling the model to protect emerging task-critical parameters while releasing outdated ones to recover plasticity. Experiments on diverse multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that EPI consistently reduces interference and forgetting compared to static isolation and standard fine-tuning, while improving overall generalization. Our analysis highlights the necessity of synchronizing isolation mechanisms with the evolving dynamics of learning diverse abilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数重要性并非静态：监督微调中的演化参数隔离
大语言模型的监督微调（SFT）常受任务干扰和灾难性遗忘影响。近期方法通过训练期间隔离关键参数缓解此问题，但这些方法是针对动态问题的静态解决方案，假设参数重要性一旦确定即保持不变。本文实证表明，参数重要性随训练过程发生时变。为此，我们提出演化参数隔离（EPI），一种根据参数重要性在线估计动态调整隔离决策的微调框架。EPI不再冻结固定参数子集，而是周期性利用梯度信号更新隔离掩码，使模型既能保护新兴关键参数，又能释放过时参数以恢复可塑性。在多个多任务基准测试中，EPI相比静态隔离和标准微调持续降低干扰与遗忘，同时提升整体泛化能力。我们的分析强调，隔离机制需与学习多样化能力的动态演化同步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that parameter importance in supervised fine-tuning is not static but evolves over training, this work introduces Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI), a framework that dynamically adjusts which parameters are isolated based on real-time gradient signals. Unlike static methods that freeze task-critical parameters early, EPI periodically updates isolation masks to protect newly critical parameters while releasing outdated ones, thereby preserving model plasticity. Experiments across multiple multi-task benchmarks show EPI consistently outperforms both static isolation and standard fine-tuning in reducing task interference and catastrophic forgetting, while enhancing overall generalization.
鉴于监督微调过程中参数重要性随训练动态变化，本文提出动态参数隔离框架EPI，通过梯度信号周期性更新隔离掩码，保护新兴关键参数并释放过时参数以恢复模型可塑性。在多个多任务基准测试中，EPI显著优于静态隔离与标准微调，有效降低任务干扰与灾难性遗忘，同时提升整体泛化能力。
Getting the Numbers Right$\unicode{x2014}$Modelling Multi-Class Object Counting in Dense and Varied Scenes
Authors: Villanelle O'Reilly, Jonathan Cox, Georgios Leontidis, Marc Hanheide, Petra Bosilj, James M. Brown
First: 2025-10-02T16:57:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:51:34+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Density map estimation enables accurate object counting in heavily occluded, and densely packed scenes where detection-based counting fails. In multi-class density estimation, class awareness can be introduced by modelling classes non-exclusively, better reflecting crowded and visually ambiguous contexts. However, existing multi-class density estimators often degrade in less-dense scenes, while state-of-the-art detectors still struggle in the most congested settings. To bridge this gap, we propose the first vision-transformer-based approach to multi-class density estimation. Our model combines a Twins-SVT pyramid vision transformer backbone with a multiscale CNN decoder that leverages hierarchical features for robust counting across a wide range of densities. Further to that, the method adds an auxiliary segmentation task with the Category Focus Module to suppress inter-category interference at training time. The module improves the density estimation head without the need for constraining assumptions added by the application of the auxiliary task at inference time, as required in previous methods. Training and evaluation on the VisDrone and iSAID benchmarks demonstrates a leap in performance versus the previous state-of-the-art multi-class density estimation methods, attaining a 33%, 43%, and 64% reduction to MAE in testing evaluation. The method outperforms YOLO11 in less busy scenes, exceeding it by an order of magnitude in the most crowded testing samples. Code, and trained weights available at https://github.com/LCAS/gnr_mcdest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：准确计数——密集且多变场景中多类目标计数建模
密度图估计可在检测方法失效的严重遮挡和密集场景中实现精确计数。在多类密度估计中，通过非互斥建模类别可引入类别意识，更真实反映拥挤和视觉模糊的场景。然而，现有方法在稀疏场景中性能下降，而最先进的检测器在最拥挤场景中仍表现不佳。为弥合这一差距，我们提出首个基于视觉Transformer的多类密度估计方法。该模型结合Twins-SVT金字塔视觉Transformer主干与多尺度CNN解码器，利用层次化特征实现宽密度范围内的鲁棒计数。此外，方法引入辅助分割任务，并通过类别聚焦模块在训练时抑制类间干扰。该模块在推理时无需像以往方法那样施加辅助任务的约束假设，即可提升密度估计头性能。在VisDrone和iSAID基准测试中，该方法相较先前最先进方法性能大幅提升，测试评估中MAE分别降低33%、43%和64%。在较不拥挤场景中超越YOLO11，在最拥挤测试样本中性能高出一个数量级。 代码与训练权重见 https://github.com/LCAS/gnr_mcdest。

Motivated by the limitations of detection-based counting in dense and varied scenes, this work introduces the first vision-transformer approach for multi-class density estimation, combining a Twins-SVT backbone with a multiscale CNN decoder to handle varying densities robustly. It further incorporates an auxiliary segmentation task enhanced by a Category Focus Module to suppress inter-class interference during training without requiring inference-time constraints. Evaluated on VisDrone and iSAID, the method achieves significant reductions in MAE—33%, 43%, and 64%—outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods and even surpassing YOLO11 by an order of magnitude in the most congested scenes.
为解决检测方法在密集且多类场景中计数失效的问题，本文提出首个基于视觉Transformer的多类密度估计方法。该方法结合Twins-SVT金字塔视觉Transformer与多尺度CNN解码器，利用层次化特征实现不同密度场景下的鲁棒计数。通过引入辅助分割任务并配合类别聚焦模块，在训练阶段抑制类别间干扰，且无需推理时施加额外约束。在VisDrone和iSAID数据集上，该方法相较现有最先进方法MAE分别降低33%、43%和64%，在最拥挤场景中性能比YOLO11高出一个数量级。
"I'm Not Able to Be There for You": Emotional Labour, Responsibility, and AI in Peer Support
Authors: Kellie Yu Hui Sim, Kenny Tsu Wei Choo
First: 2026-04-15T15:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:51:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted at DIS 2026 (PWiP). 7 pages, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Peer support is increasingly positioned as a scalable response to gaps in mental health care, particularly in digitally mediated settings, yet what counts as peer support and how responsibility is distributed remain unevenly defined in practice. Drawing on interviews with peer supporters, we show how lived experience, moral commitment, and self-identification shape participation while blurring expectations around scope, authority, and accountability. Institutional ambiguity concentrates emotional labour, boundary-setting, and escalation of responsibility at the individual level, often without consistent organisational scaffolding. Participants evaluated AI not primarily through empathy or technical capability, but through how technologies redistribute risk, labour, and accountability within already fragile support roles. Building on these findings, we outline design futures for an AI-supported peer support ecosystem that foregrounds responsibility as a central design concern rather than treating AI as a mechanism of scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“我无法在你身边”：情感劳动、责任与AI在同伴支持中的角色
同伴支持正日益被视为填补心理健康护理缺口、特别是在数字媒介环境中的可扩展解决方案，但实践中对何为同伴支持以及责任如何分配仍缺乏统一界定。基于对同伴支持者的访谈，我们发现个人经历、道德承诺与自我认同塑造了参与行为，同时模糊了关于范围、权威与问责的预期。制度上的模糊性使情感劳动、边界设定与责任升级集中于个体层面，往往缺乏一致的组织支持。参与者评估AI并非主要基于共情或技术能力，而是关注技术如何在本已脆弱的支持角色中重新分配风险、劳动与问责。基于这些发现，我们勾勒出一个以责任为核心设计关切的AI支持同伴支持生态系统的设计前景，而非将AI视为规模扩展的工具。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how peer support in digital mental health contexts is shaped by emotional labour and ambiguous institutional roles, revealing that participants’ moral commitments and self-identification define their responsibilities, often without organizational support. Through interviews, the research shows that users evaluate AI not by its empathy or technical skill, but by how it redistributes risk, labour, and accountability in already fragile support roles. The authors propose design futures for AI-supported peer support systems that center responsibility as a core design concern rather than treating AI as a mere scaling tool.
该研究探讨了数字心理健康背景下同伴支持如何受情感劳动和机构模糊性影响，发现参与者通过个人承诺而非正式结构界定自身责任。访谈显示，同伴支持者常承担不成比例的情感与问责负担，且缺乏组织支持；用户评价AI工具的标准并非共情或功能，而是其如何在脆弱支持角色中重新分配风险与责任。论文提出以责任为核心的设计框架，构建AI支持的同伴支持生态系统，而非将AI视为扩展规模的工具。
Memory Transfer Learning: How Memories are Transferred Across Domains in Coding Agents
Authors: Kangsan Kim, Minki Kang, Taeil Kim, Yanlai Yang, Mengye Ren, Sung Ju Hwang
First: 2026-04-15T15:50:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:50:29+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Memory-based self-evolution has emerged as a promising paradigm for coding agents. However, existing approaches typically restrict memory utilization to homogeneous task domains, failing to leverage the shared infrastructural foundations, such as runtime environments and programming languages, that exist across diverse real-world coding problems. To address this limitation, we investigate \textbf{Memory Transfer Learning} (MTL) by harnessing a unified memory pool from heterogeneous domains. We evaluate performance across 6 coding benchmarks using four memory representations, ranging from concrete traces to abstract insights. Our experiments demonstrate that cross-domain memory improves average performance by 3.7\%, primarily by transferring meta-knowledge, such as validation routines, rather than task-specific code. Importantly, we find that abstraction dictates transferability; high-level insights generalize well, whereas low-level traces often induce negative transfer due to excessive specificity. Furthermore, we show that transfer effectiveness scales with the size of the memory pool, and memory can be transferred even between different models. Our work establishes empirical design principles for expanding memory utilization beyond single-domain silos. Project page: https://memorytransfer.github.io/
