标题：记忆迁移学习：编码代理如何在不同领域间转移记忆
基于记忆的自演化已成为编码代理的有前景范式。然而，现有方法通常将记忆利用限制在同质任务领域，未能利用跨多样真实世界编码问题所共有的基础设施，如运行时环境和编程语言。为解决此局限，我们研究了\textbf{记忆迁移学习}（MTL），通过从异构领域共享统一记忆池。我们在六个编码基准上评估了四种记忆表示形式（从具体轨迹到抽象洞见）的效果。实验表明，跨领域记忆平均性能提升3.7%，主要通过迁移元知识（如验证程序），而非任务特定代码。重要的是，我们发现抽象程度决定迁移效果：高层洞见泛化性好，而低层轨迹常因过度具体导致负迁移。此外，我们证明迁移效果随记忆池规模增大而提升，且记忆可在不同模型间迁移。本工作确立了扩展记忆利用超越单一领域孤岛的实证设计原则。项目页面：https://memorytransfer.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generalize coding agent memory across diverse domains, this study introduces Memory Transfer Learning (MTL) to leverage shared infrastructural knowledge like runtime environments and programming languages. It evaluates four memory representations across six coding benchmarks and finds that cross-domain memory improves average performance by 3.7%, primarily through transferring meta-knowledge such as validation routines rather than task-specific code. Additionally, transfer effectiveness scales with memory pool size and can occur even between different models, establishing empirical guidelines for expanding memory use beyond single-domain constraints.

Diffusion Language Models for Speech Recognition
Authors: Davyd Naveriani, Albert Zeyer, Ralf Schlüter, Hermann Ney
First: 2026-04-15T15:46:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:46:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion language models have recently emerged as a leading alternative to standard language models, due to their ability for bidirectional attention and parallel text generation. In this work, we explore variants for their use in speech recognition. Specifically, we introduce a comprehensive guide to incorporating masked diffusion language models (MDLM) and uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) for rescoring ASR hypotheses. Additionally, we design a new joint-decoding method that combines CTC and USDM by integrating the framewise probability distributions derived from CTC with the labelwise probability distributions computed by USDM at each decoding step, thereby generating new candidates that combine strong language knowledge from USDM and acoustic information from CTC. Our findings reveal that USDM, as well as MDLM, can significantly improve the accuracy of recognized text. We publish all our code and recipes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于语音识别的扩散语言模型
由于其双向注意力和并行文本生成能力，扩散语言模型最近已成为标准语言模型的领先替代方案。在本研究中，我们探讨了其在语音识别中的变体应用。具体而言，我们提出了一套全面指南，用于将掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）和均匀状态扩散模型（USDM）应用于ASR假设重排序。此外，我们设计了一种新的联合解码方法，通过在每个解码步骤中整合CTC生成的帧级概率分布与USDM计算的标签级概率分布，结合CTC的声学信息与USDM的语言知识，生成新的候选结果。我们的研究发现，USDM和MDLM均可显著提升识别文本的准确性。我们公开了所有代码和配置方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of diffusion language models to enhance speech recognition through bidirectional attention and parallel generation, this work investigates their application in ASR rescoring and joint decoding. It introduces masked diffusion language models (MDLM) and uniform-state diffusion models (USDM) for rescoring hypotheses, and proposes a novel joint-decoding method that fuses CTC’s framewise probabilities with USDM’s labelwise distributions to generate hybrid candidates. Experimental results show that both MDLM and USDM substantially improve recognition accuracy, with the joint-decoding approach yielding the best performance.
鉴于扩散语言模型在双向注意力和平行生成方面的潜力，本研究探索其在语音识别中的应用，特别是用于ASR假说重排序。论文引入了掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）和均匀状态扩散模型（USDM），并设计了一种新的联合解码方法，将CTC的帧级概率与USDM的标签级概率融合，生成结合语言知识与声学信息的混合候选。实验表明，MDLM和USDM均显著提升识别准确率，联合解码方法表现最优。
Online Navigation Planning for Long-term Autonomous Operation of Underwater Gliders
Authors: Victor-Alexandru Darvariu, Charlotte Z. Reed, Jan Stratmann, Bruno Lacerda, Benjamin Allsup, Stephen Woodward, Elizabeth Siddle, Trishna Saeharaseelan, Owain Jones, Dan Jones, Tobias Ferreira, Chloe Baker, Kevin Chaplin, James Kirk, Ashley Iceton-Morris, Ryan D. Patmore, Jeff Polton, Charlotte Williams, Christopher D. J. Auckland, Rob A. Hall, Alexandra Kokkinaki, Alvaro Lorenzo Lopez, Justin J. H. Buck, Nick Hawes
First: 2026-02-22T19:34:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:42:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Underwater glider robots have become indispensable for ocean sampling, yet fully autonomous long-term operation remains rare in practice. Although stakeholders are calling for tools to manage increasingly large fleets of gliders, existing methods have seen limited adoption due to their inability to account for environmental uncertainty and operational constraints. In this work, we demonstrate that uncertainty-aware online navigation planning can be deployed in real-world glider missions at scale. We formulate the problem as a stochastic shortest-path Markov Decision Process and propose a sample-based online planner based on Monte Carlo Tree Search. Samples are generated by a physics-informed simulator calibrated on real-world glider data that captures uncertain execution of controls and ocean current forecasts while remaining computationally tractable. Our methodology is integrated into an autonomous system for Slocum gliders that performs closed-loop replanning at each surfacing. The system was validated in two North Sea deployments totalling approximately 3 months and 1000 km, representing the longest fully autonomous glider campaigns in the literature to date. Results demonstrate improvements of up to 9.88% in dive duration and 16.51% in path length compared to standard straight-to-goal navigation, including a statistically significant path length reduction of 9.55% in a field deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：水下滑翔机长期自主运行的在线导航规划
水下滑翔机机器人已成为海洋采样不可或缺的工具，但其完全自主的长期运行在实践中仍较为罕见。尽管利益相关方呼吁管理日益庞大的滑翔机编队，现有方法因无法考虑环境不确定性与操作约束而应用有限。本文证明，具备不确定性的在线导航规划可大规模部署于真实滑翔机任务中。我们将问题建模为随机最短路径马尔可夫决策过程，并提出基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的采样式在线规划器。采样由基于物理模型的模拟器生成，该模拟器以真实滑翔机数据校准，能捕捉控制执行的不确定性与海洋洋流预报，同时保持计算可行性。该方法集成于Slocum滑翔机自主系统中，每次浮出水面即进行闭环重规划。系统在北海两次部署中得到验证，总时长约3个月、总航程约1000公里，为迄今文献中持续时间最长的完全自主滑翔机任务。结果表明，与标准直航导航相比，潜水时长提升最高达9.88%，路径长度缩短最高达16.51%，其中实地部署中路径长度显著减少9.55%。
Summary / 总结
To enable long-term autonomous operation of underwater gliders amid environmental uncertainty, this work introduces an uncertainty-aware online navigation planner based on Monte Carlo Tree Search, guided by a physics-informed simulator calibrated on real-world data. The method formulates navigation as a stochastic Markov Decision Process and replans in real-time at each surfacing, adapting to ocean currents and control execution errors. Field tests over 3 months and 1000 km in the North Sea showed the system outperformed standard navigation by 9.88% in dive duration and 16.51% in path length, with a statistically significant 9.55% reduction in path length during deployment.
为实现水下滑翔机在环境不确定性与操作约束下的长期自主运行，本文提出一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的不确定性感知在线导航规划方法，其采样器由结合真实滑翔机数据的物理信息模拟器驱动。该方法将导航建模为随机最短路径马尔可夫决策过程，并在每次浮出水面时闭环重规划。在北海两次实地部署中（总计3个月、1000公里），该系统验证了显著性能提升：相比直行至目标导航，最大可延长9.88%的下潜时长、缩短16.51%的路径长度，其中实地部署中路径长度减少9.55%具有统计显著性。
Acts of Configuration: Rethinking Provenance, Temporality and Legitimacy in Post-Mortem Agents
Authors: Kellie Yu Hui Sim, Pin Sym Foong, Darryl Lim, John-Henry Lim, Kenny Tsu Wei Choo
First: 2026-04-15T15:41:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:41:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at DIS 2026 (PWiP). 5 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Work on persona-persistent post-mortem agents typically frames design around a life/death binary. This framing neglects a consequential yet under-theorised condition: when individuals remain alive but have impaired decisional capacity. Drawing on a multi-phase workshop in which participants trained and reflected on an AI agent for Advance Care Planning, we examined how people reason about agentic delegation post-capacity loss. Initially, participants favoured bounded agents grounded in first-party authorship and representational fidelity over autonomous or evolving stand-ins. However, temporality introduced novel ideas like adjacent use driven by persona persistence over functional expansion: agents should evolve while users retain capacity, remain static once capacity is lost, but somehow inform adjacent post-mortem uses. We discuss the implications of these findings and propose that the configuration of agents for post-capacity use reshapes our understanding of provenance, temporality, and legitimacy for post-mortem agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：配置行为：重新思考事后代理中的溯源、时间性和合法性
关于人格持久型事后代理的设计研究通常围绕生命/死亡二元框架展开。这种框架忽视了一个重要但尚未充分理论化的条件：当个体虽仍存活但决策能力受损时。我们基于一个分阶段工作坊，参与者训练并反思了用于预先照护规划的AI代理，探讨了人们在能力丧失后如何思考代理委托。最初，参与者偏好基于第一方创作且具表征忠实度的有限代理，而非自主或演进型替代者。然而，时间性引入了新概念：人格持久性驱动的相邻使用，而非功能扩展——代理应在用户能力存续时演进，能力丧失后保持静态，但需以某种方式支持后续的相邻用途。我们讨论了这些发现的意义，并提出，为能力丧失后使用配置代理，将重塑我们对事后代理的溯源、时间性和合法性的理解。

本文挑战了将后遗代理视为‘生’或‘死’二元框架的常见做法，指出在个体虽存活但决策能力受损的情况下，现有设计忽视了这一关键情境。通过组织多阶段工作坊，参与者共同设计并反思用于预先照护规划的AI代理，研究者探讨了人们在能力丧失后如何理解代理权委托。参与者最初偏好基于第一方作者身份和表征忠实度的有限代理，但当时间维度成为核心时，他们提出代理应在能力存续期间演化，能力丧失后保持静态，但仍需支持后续的后遗用途。研究认为，为能力丧失后使用而配置代理，需重新理解后遗代理的溯源性、时间性与合法性。
Pruning Long Chain-of-Thought of Large Reasoning Models via Small-Scale Preference Optimization
Authors: Bin Hong, Jiayu Liu, Kai Zhang, Jianwen Sun, Mengdi Zhang, Zhenya Huang
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-08-13T20:00:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:39:48+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 8 figures, ICLR'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated strong performance on complex tasks through long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, their lengthy outputs increase computational costs and may lead to overthinking, raising challenges in balancing reasoning effectiveness and efficiency. Current solutions often compromise reasoning quality or require extensive resources. In this paper, we investigate how to reduce the generation length of LRMs with limited tuning. We analyze generation path distributions and filter generated trajectories through difficulty estimation. Subsequently, we analyze the convergence characteristics of various preference optimization objectives under a unified Bradley-Terry loss based framework. Based on the analysis, we propose Length Controlled Preference Optimization (LCPO) that directly balances the implicit reward related to NLL loss. LCPO can effectively learn length preference with limited data and training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly reduces the average output length of LRMs by over 50\% across multiple benchmarks while maintaining the reasoning performance. Our work highlights the potential for computationally efficient approaches in guiding LRMs toward efficient reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过小规模偏好优化修剪大型推理模型的长链式思维
近期大型推理模型（LRMs）在复杂任务上通过长链式思维（CoT）推理展现出强大性能。然而，其冗长输出增加了计算成本，可能导致过度思考，给推理效果与效率的平衡带来挑战。现有方案常牺牲推理质量或需大量资源。本文研究如何在有限调优下缩短LRMs的生成长度。我们分析生成路径分布，并通过难度估计过滤生成轨迹。随后，在统一的Bradley-Terry损失框架下，分析各类偏好优化目标的收敛特性。基于分析，我们提出长度控制偏好优化（LCPO），直接平衡与NLL损失相关的隐式奖励。LCPO可在有限数据和训练下有效学习长度偏好。大量实验表明，该方法在多个基准上将LRMs的平均输出长度减少超过50%，同时保持推理性能。本工作凸显了计算高效方法引导LRMs实现高效推理的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and potential overthinking in long Chain-of-Thought reasoning by Large Reasoning Models, this paper proposes Length Controlled Preference Optimization (LCPO), a method that reduces output length with minimal tuning by leveraging preference optimization under a Bradley-Terry loss framework. LCPO directly balances implicit reward signals tied to NLL loss to learn length preferences efficiently. Experiments show it cuts average output length by over 50% across multiple benchmarks while preserving reasoning performance.
为解决大推理模型长链思维推理带来的高计算开销与过度思考问题，本文提出长度控制偏好优化（LCPO）方法，通过在布拉德利-特里损失框架下进行小规模偏好优化，直接平衡NLL损失隐含奖励与输出长度，实现高效推理而不损失性能。实验表明，LCPO在多个基准上使平均输出长度降低超50%，同时保持推理性能不变。
Depth-Aware Image and Video Orientation Estimation
Authors: Muhammad Z. Alam, Larry Stetsiuk, M. Umair Mukati, Zeeshan Kaleem
Venue: IEEE Access, vol. 13, pp. 198458-198470, 2025
First: 2026-04-15T15:38:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:38:56+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces a novel approach for image and video orientation estimation by leveraging depth distribution in natural images. The proposed method estimates the orientation based on the depth distribution across different quadrants of the image, providing a robust framework for orientation estimation suited for applications such as virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), autonomous navigation, and interactive surveillance systems. To further enhance fine-scale perceptual alignment, we incorporate depth gradient consistency (DGC) and horizontal symmetry analysis (HSA), enabling precise orientation correction. This hybrid strategy effectively exploits depth cues to support spatial coherence and perceptual stability in immersive visual content. Qualitative and quantitative evaluations demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of the proposed approach, outperforming existing techniques across diverse scenarios.

Experiments show the approach achieves superior accuracy and stability compared to existing methods across diverse image and video scenarios, particularly in VR, AR, and surveillance applications.
为满足沉浸式与自主视觉系统对可靠姿态估计的需求，本文提出一种基于深度分布的姿态估计方法，通过分析图像四象限的深度分布来推断方向，并结合深度梯度一致性与水平对称性分析以校正细微感知偏差。实验结果表明，该方法在多种场景下均优于现有技术，展现出良好的空间一致性与感知稳定性。
Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution for Imbalanced Textures: A Texture-Aware Diffusion Framework
Authors: Enzhuo Zhang, Sijie Zhao, Dilxat Muhtar, Zhenshi Li, Xueliang Zhang, Pengfeng Xiao
First: 2026-04-15T15:36:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:36:49+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures, 9 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generative diffusion priors have recently achieved state-of-the-art performance in natural image super-resolution, demonstrating a powerful capability to synthesize photorealistic details. However, their direct application to remote sensing image super-resolution (RSISR) reveals significant shortcomings. Unlike natural images, remote sensing images exhibit a unique texture distribution where ground objects are globally stochastic yet locally clustered, leading to highly imbalanced textures. This imbalance severely hinders the model's spatial perception. To address this, we propose TexADiff, a novel framework that begins by estimating a Relative Texture Density Map (RTDM) to represent the texture distribution. TexADiff then leverages this RTDM in three synergistic ways: as an explicit spatial conditioning to guide the diffusion process, as a loss modulation term to prioritize texture-rich regions, and as a dynamic adapter for the sampling schedule. These modifications are designed to endow the model with explicit texture-aware capabilities. Experiments demonstrate that TexADiff achieves superior or competitive quantitative metrics. Furthermore, qualitative results show that our model generates faithful high-frequency details while effectively suppressing texture hallucinations. This improved reconstruction quality also results in significant gains in downstream task performance. The source code of our method can be found at https://github.com/ZezFuture/TexAdiff.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向纹理不平衡遥感图像的超分辨率：一种纹理感知扩散框架
生成式扩散先验最近在自然图像超分辨率中取得了最先进性能，展现出强大的合成逼真细节的能力。然而，其直接应用于遥感图像超分辨率（RSISR）时暴露出显著缺陷。与自然图像不同，遥感图像具有独特的纹理分布：地物在全球上随机分布，但在局部区域聚集，导致纹理高度不平衡。这种不平衡严重阻碍了模型的空间感知能力。为此，我们提出TexADiff，一种新颖框架，首先估计相对纹理密度图（RTDM）以表征纹理分布。TexADiff随后以三种协同方式利用RTDM：作为显式空间条件引导扩散过程，作为损失调制项优先处理纹理丰富区域，以及作为采样时间表的动态适配器。这些修改旨在赋予模型显式的纹理感知能力。实验表明，TexADiff在定量指标上达到领先或竞争水平。此外，定性结果表明，我们的模型能生成忠实的高频细节，同时有效抑制纹理幻觉。这种重建质量的提升也显著提升了下游任务性能。本方法的源代码可在https://github.com/ZezFuture/TexAdiff获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of generative diffusion models on remote sensing image super-resolution due to imbalanced textures, this paper introduces TexADiff, a texture-aware diffusion framework. The method first estimates a Relative Texture Density Map (RTDM) to capture the global stochastic yet locally clustered texture distribution in remote sensing images. It then uses this RTDM to guide the diffusion process via spatial conditioning, modulate loss to prioritize texture-rich areas, and adapt the sampling schedule dynamically. Experimental results show that TexADiff achieves superior or competitive quantitative metrics and produces high-fidelity details while suppressing hallucinations, leading to improved performance in downstream tasks.
针对生成扩散模型在遥感图像超分辨率任务中因纹理分布失衡导致性能不佳的问题，本文提出TexADiff纹理感知扩散框架。该方法首先构建相对纹理密度图（RTDM）以表征遥感图像中全局随机但局部聚集的纹理分布，随后将RTDM用于三方面改进：空间条件化引导扩散过程、损失调制强化纹理区域训练、动态调整采样策略。实验表明，TexADiff在定量指标上优于或媲美现有方法，生成结果更准确地保留高频细节并抑制纹理伪影，同时显著提升下游任务性能。
Reward Design for Physical Reasoning in Vision-Language Models
Authors: Derek Lilienthal, Manisha Mukherjee, Sameera Horawalavithana
First: 2026-04-15T15:36:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:36:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physical reasoning over visual inputs demands tight integration of visual perception, domain knowledge, and multi-step symbolic inference. Yet even state-of-the-art Vision Language Models (VLMs) fall far short of human performance on physics benchmarks. While post-training algorithms such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) have demonstrated strong reasoning gains in language models, how reward design shapes VLM physical reasoning behavior remains poorly understood. We present a systematic reward ablation study for GRPO-based VLM training on physical reasoning. We compare four reward signals of increasing semantic richness: format compliance, answer accuracy, a composite rubric reward (answer correctness, physics principle identification, and unit consistency), and a novel internal reward derived from model attention weights over input image regions. We evaluate on PhyX, a 3,000-problem benchmark spanning six physics domains and six reasoning types across multiple-choice and open-ended formats, using IBM Granite Vision 3.3 (2B). Across both formats, GRPO with accuracy-based rewards outperforms SFT on most domains, though gains vary substantially by reward type and domain. Reward design does not uniformly improve performance. Instead, it induces domain-specific reasoning behaviors. Accuracy-based rewards provide the strongest overall gains. Rubric rewards improve structured reasoning quality without consistent accuracy improvements. Attention-based rewards enhance spatial reasoning while degrading performance in symbolic domains. Our internal attention-weight reward requires no spatial annotations and improves spatial relation accuracy from 0.27 to 0.50, suggesting that supervising where the model attends during generation is a promising direction for visually grounded physical reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉语言模型中物理推理的奖励设计
视觉输入上的物理推理需要视觉感知、领域知识和多步符号推理的紧密结合。然而，即使是最先进的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在物理基准测试中的表现仍远低于人类水平。虽然监督微调（SFT）和组相对策略优化（GRPO）等后训练算法已在语言模型中展现出强大的推理能力，但奖励设计如何塑造VLM的物理推理行为仍不甚明了。我们针对基于GRPO的VLM物理推理训练，系统性地研究了四种语义丰富度递增的奖励信号：格式合规性、答案准确性、综合评分奖励（答案正确性、物理原理识别和单位一致性），以及一种新颖的内部奖励，该奖励源自模型对输入图像区域的注意力权重。我们在PhyX基准上进行评估，该基准包含3000个问题，覆盖六个物理领域和六种推理类型，支持多选题和开放式问题两种格式，使用IBM Granite Vision 3.3（2B）模型。在两种格式下，基于准确性的GRPO奖励在大多数领域均优于SFT，但不同奖励类型和领域下的收益差异显著。奖励设计并非普遍提升性能，而是诱导特定领域的推理行为。基于准确性的奖励提供最强的整体收益。评分奖励提升结构化推理质量，但未带来一致的准确率提升。基于注意力的奖励增强空间推理能力，但在符号领域导致性能下降。我们的内部注意力权重奖励无需空间标注，将空间关系准确率从0.27提升至0.50，表明在生成过程中监督模型关注的位置是视觉接地物理推理的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of enhancing physical reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) by systematically analyzing how different reward designs affect model behavior during GRPO-based training. Motivated by the gap between VLMs and human performance on physics benchmarks, the authors compare four reward signals—format compliance, answer accuracy, a composite rubric reward, and an attention-weight-derived internal reward—on the PhyX benchmark. Results show that accuracy-based rewards yield the strongest overall gains across domains, while rubric rewards improve structured reasoning without boosting accuracy, and attention-based rewards enhance spatial reasoning but hurt symbolic reasoning. Notably, the attention-weight reward, which requires no spatial annotations, boosts spatial relation accuracy from 0.27 to 0.50, indicating that guiding model attention during generation is a promising approach for visually grounded physical reasoning.
该研究旨在通过系统分析不同奖励设计对视觉语言模型（VLM）物理推理能力的影响，弥合当前模型与人类水平之间的差距。研究在GRPO微调框架下，对比了四种递进式语义丰富度的奖励信号——格式合规性、答案准确性、多维度评分标准及基于注意力权重的内部奖励——在PhyX物理基准测试中的表现。结果显示，基于准确性的奖励在多数领域显著优于SFT，而评分标准奖励虽提升结构化推理质量但未稳定提高准确率，注意力奖励则增强空间推理但损害符号推理。特别地，无需空间标注的注意力权重奖励将空间关系准确率从0.27提升至0.50，表明引导模型生成过程中的注意力聚焦是视觉接地物理推理的重要方向。
Mini-batch Estimation for Deep Cox Models: Statistical Foundations and Practical Guidance
Authors: Lang Zeng, Weijing Tang, Zhao Ren, Ying Ding
First: 2024-08-05T21:25:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:36:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The stochastic gradient descent (SGD) algorithm has been widely used to optimize deep Cox neural network (Cox-NN) by updating model parameters using mini-batches of data. We show that SGD aims to optimize the average of mini-batch partial-likelihood, which is different from the standard partial-likelihood. This distinction requires developing new statistical properties for the global optimizer, namely, the mini-batch maximum partial-likelihood estimator (mb-MPLE). We establish that mb-MPLE for Cox-NN is consistent and achieves the optimal minimax convergence rate up to a polylogarithmic factor. For Cox regression with linear covariate effects, we further show that mb-MPLE is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal with asymptotic variance approaching the information lower bound as batch size increases, which is confirmed by simulation studies. Additionally, we offer practical guidance on using SGD, supported by theoretical analysis and numerical evidence. For Cox-NN, we demonstrate that the ratio of the learning rate to the batch size is critical in SGD dynamics, offering insight into hyperparameter tuning. For Cox regression, we characterize the iterative convergence of SGD, ensuring that the global optimizer, mb-MPLE, can be approximated with sufficiently many iterations. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of mb-MPLE in a large-scale real-world application where the standard MPLE is intractable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度Cox模型的Mini-batch估计：统计基础与实用指南
随机梯度下降（SGD）算法广泛用于通过使用数据的Mini-batch更新模型参数来优化深度Cox神经网络（Cox-NN）。我们表明，SGD旨在优化Mini-batch部分似然的平均值，这与标准部分似然不同。这一区别要求为全局优化器（即Mini-batch最大部分似然估计量，mb-MPLE）开发新的统计性质。我们证明，Cox-NN的mb-MPLE具有一致性，并在对数多项式因子内达到最优最小最大收敛速率。对于具有线性协变量效应的Cox回归，我们进一步证明mb-MPLE具有√n一致性，并在批大小增加时渐近正态分布，其渐近方差趋近于信息下界，这一结论由模拟研究证实。此外，我们提供了基于理论分析和数值证据的SGD实用指南。对于Cox-NN，我们表明学习率与批大小的比值在SGD动态中至关重要，为超参数调优提供见解。对于Cox回归，我们刻画了SGD的迭代收敛性，确保在足够多的迭代后可近似全局优化器mb-MPLE。最后，我们在一个大规模真实世界应用中展示了mb-MPLE的有效性，其中标准MPLE不可行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently train deep Cox neural networks using stochastic gradient descent, this paper establishes the statistical properties of the mini-batch maximum partial-likelihood estimator (mb-MPLE), which optimizes the average of mini-batch partial-likelihoods rather than the full-data partial-likelihood. The authors prove that mb-MPLE is consistent and achieves the optimal minimax convergence rate up to a polylogarithmic factor, and for linear Cox models, it is \sqrt{n}-consistent and asymptotically normal with variance approaching the information lower bound as batch size grows. Theoretical results are supported by simulations and real-world applications, showing that the learning rate to batch size ratio governs SGD dynamics, and sufficient iterations ensure convergence to the global optimizer, even when the standard maximum partial-likelihood estimator is computationally intractable.
为了解决深度Cox神经网络中广泛使用的随机梯度下降（SGD）优化方法所隐含的统计理论问题，本文建立了迷你批最大部分似然估计量（mb-MPLE）的统计性质，该估计量与标准Cox模型中的部分似然不同。研究证明mb-MPLE具有一致性，并在近似最优的最小最大收敛速率下，当协变量为线性时，其收敛速度为$\sqrt{n}$，且渐近正态，其渐近方差随批大小增加趋近信息下界。仿真和实证结果验证了这些理论性质，同时提供了SGD超参数调优的实用建议，强调学习率与批大小比例的重要性。该方法在大规模实际应用中被证明有效，解决了标准最大部分似然估计不可行的问题。
Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Methane Sorption: Cross-Gas Transfer Learning, Ensemble Collapse Under Physics Constraints, and Monte Carlo Dropout Uncertainty Quantification
Authors: Mohammad Nooraiepour, Zezhang Song, Wei Li, Sarah Perez
First: 2026-04-15T15:35:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:35:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate methane sorption prediction across heterogeneous coal ranks requires models that combine thermodynamic consistency, efficient knowledge transfer across data-scarce geological systems, and calibrated uncertainty estimates, capabilities that are rarely addressed together in existing frameworks. We present a physics-informed transfer learning framework that adapts a hydrogen sorption PINN to methane sorption prediction via Elastic Weight Consolidation, coal-specific feature engineering, and a three-phase curriculum that progressively balances transfer preservation with thermodynamic fine-tuning. Trained on 993 equilibrium measurements from 114 independent coal experiments spanning lignite to anthracite, the framework achieves R2 = 0.932 on held-out coal samples, a 227% improvement over pressure-only classical isotherms, while hydrogen pre-training delivers 18.9% lower RMSE and 19.4% faster convergence than random initialization. Five Bayesian uncertainty quantification approaches reveal a systematic divergence in performance across physics-constrained architectures. Monte Carlo Dropout achieves well-calibrated uncertainty at minimal overhead, while deep ensembles, regardless of architectural diversity or initialization strategy, exhibit performance degradation because shared physics constraints narrow the admissible solution manifold. SHAP and ALE analyses confirm that learned representations remain physically interpretable and aligned with established coal sorption mechanisms: moisture-volatile interactions are most influential, pressure-temperature coupling captures thermodynamic co-dependence, and features exhibit non-monotonic effects. These results identify Monte Carlo Dropout as the best-performing UQ method in this physics-constrained transfer learning framework, and demonstrate cross-gas transfer learning as a data-efficient strategy for geological material modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息神经网络的甲烷吸附：跨气体迁移学习、物理约束下的集成坍缩与蒙特卡洛dropout不确定性量化
准确预测不同煤阶煤层中甲烷吸附需结合热力学一致性、跨数据稀缺地质系统的高效知识迁移及校准的不确定性估计能力，而现有框架极少同时兼顾这些能力。本文提出一种物理信息迁移学习框架，通过弹性权重固化、煤种特异性特征工程及三阶段课程学习，将氢气吸附PINN适配至甲烷吸附预测。该框架在114个独立煤样实验的993组平衡测量数据上训练，对未见煤样测试R²达0.932，较仅压力的经典等温线提升227%；氢气预训练使RMSE降低18.9%，收敛速度加快19.4%。五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法揭示物理约束架构间性能系统性差异。蒙特卡洛dropout在极小开销下实现良好校准不确定性，而深度集成无论架构多样性或初始化策略如何，均因共享物理约束缩小可接受解空间而性能下降。SHAP与ALE分析证实所学表征保持物理可解释性并符合已知煤吸附机制：水分-挥发性相互作用影响最大，压力-温度耦合体现热力学协同依赖，特征呈现非单调效应。结果表明蒙特卡洛dropout是该物理约束迁移学习框架最优不确定性量化方法，并验证跨气体迁移学习作为地质材料建模的数据高效策略。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of accurately predicting methane sorption across diverse coal ranks with limited data, the study develops a physics-informed neural network framework that leverages hydrogen sorption pre-training, Elastic Weight Consolidation, coal-specific feature engineering, and a three-phase curriculum to balance transfer learning with thermodynamic fine-tuning. Trained on 993 equilibrium measurements from 114 coal experiments, the model achieves an R2 of 0.932 on unseen coal samples, outperforming classical isotherms by 227%, with hydrogen pre-training reducing RMSE by 18.9% and accelerating convergence by 19.4%. SHAP and ALE analyses confirm that the model’s learned features remain physically interpretable, with moisture-volatile interactions and pressure-temperature coupling being most influential, demonstrating cross-gas transfer learning as a data-efficient strategy for geological modeling.
为解决在数据稀缺的地质条件下准确预测不同煤阶甲烷吸附的问题，本研究提出一种结合氢吸附预训练、煤特异性特征工程及三阶段课程学习的物理信息神经网络框架，以平衡跨气体迁移学习与热力学微调。该模型在114组煤实验的993个平衡测量数据上训练，对未见样本的R2达0.932，较传统等温线提升227%，且氢吸附预训练显著降低RMSE并加速收敛。五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法中，蒙特卡洛Dropout在最小开销下提供良好校准的不确定性，而深度集成模型因物理约束缩小解空间而性能下降。SHAP与ALE分析证实模型保持物理可解释性，突出水分-挥发性相互作用与压力-温度耦合为关键机制，验证跨气体迁移学习在地质材料建模中的高效性。
Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Improving Factuality of Generations by Large Language Models
Authors: Aleksandr Rubashevskii, Dzianis Piatrashyn, Preslav Nakov, Maxim Panov
First: 2026-04-15T15:35:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:35:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are prone to generating factually incorrect outputs. Recent work has applied conformal prediction to provide uncertainty estimates and statistical guarantees for the factuality of LLM generations. However, existing approaches are typically not prompt-adaptive, limiting their ability to capture input-dependent variability. As a result, they may filter out too few items (leading to over-coverage) or too many (under-coverage) for a given task or prompt. We propose an adaptive conformal prediction approach that extends conformal score transformation methods to LLMs, with applications to long-form generation and multiple-choice question answering. This enables prompt-dependent calibration, retaining marginal coverage guarantees while improving conditional coverage. In addition, the approach naturally supports selective prediction, allowing unreliable claims or answer choices to be filtered out in downstream applications. We evaluate our approach on multiple white-box models across diverse domains and show that it significantly outperforms existing baselines in terms of conditional coverage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于提升大语言模型生成事实准确性的自适应置信预测
大语言模型（LLMs）易生成事实错误的输出。近期研究已将置信预测应用于提供不确定性估计和LLM生成事实性的统计保证。然而，现有方法通常不具备提示自适应性，限制了其捕捉输入依赖性变异的能力。因此，对于特定任务或提示，它们可能过滤出过少（导致过度覆盖）或过多（不足覆盖）的内容。我们提出一种自适应置信预测方法，将置信分数变换方法扩展至LLMs，适用于长文本生成和多项选择题问答。该方法实现提示依赖校准，在保持边际覆盖保证的同时提升条件覆盖。此外，该方法天然支持选择性预测，允许在下游应用中过滤掉不可靠的声明或答案选项。我们在多个白盒模型和多样化领域中评估该方法，结果表明其在条件覆盖方面显著优于现有基线。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of factually incorrect outputs from large language models, this paper proposes an adaptive conformal prediction method that calibrates uncertainty estimates based on input prompts, improving the precision of factuality guarantees. The method extends conformal score transformation techniques to LLMs, enabling prompt-dependent calibration while preserving marginal coverage, and supports selective prediction to filter unreliable outputs. Experiments across multiple white-box models and domains demonstrate that the approach significantly improves conditional coverage compared to existing baselines.
为解决大语言模型生成事实错误内容的问题，本文提出一种自适应置信预测方法，根据输入提示动态校准不确定性估计，提升事实性保障的精确度。该方法扩展了置信分数变换技术，使模型能在保持边际覆盖率的同时实现提示依赖的校准，并支持通过过滤不可靠输出进行选择性预测。在多个白盒模型和不同领域中的实验表明，该方法相比现有基线显著提升了条件覆盖率。
Unsupervised domain transfer: Overcoming signal degradation in sleep monitoring by increasing scoring realism
Authors: Mohammad Ahangarkiasari, Andreas Tind Damgaard, Casper Haurum, Kaare B. Mikkelsen
First: 2026-04-15T15:35:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:35:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Objective: Investigate whether hypnogram 'realism' can be used to guide an unsupervised method for handling arbitrary types of signal degradation in mobile sleep monitoring. Approach: Combining a pretrained, state-of-the-art 'u-sleep' model with a 'discriminator' network, we align features from a target domain with a feature space learned during pretraining. To test the approach, we distort the source domain with realistic signal degradations, to see how well the method can adapt to different types of degradation. We compare the performance of the resulting model with best-case models designed in a supervised manner for each type of transfer. Main Results: Depending on the type of distortion, we find that the unsupervised approach can increase Cohen's kappa with as little as 0.03 and up to 0.29, and that for all transfers, the method does not decrease performance. However, the approach never quite reaches the estimated theoretical optimal performance, and when tested on a real-life domain mismatch between two sleep studies, the benefit was insignificant. Significance: 'Discriminator-guided fine tuning' is an interesting approach to handling signal degradation for 'in the wild' sleep monitoring, with some promise. In particular, what it says about sleep data in general is interesting. However, more development will be necessary before using it 'in production'.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无监督域迁移：通过提升评分真实性克服移动睡眠监测中的信号退化

Motivated by the need to improve sleep monitoring in real-world settings where signals are degraded, this study explores whether unsupervised domain transfer can enhance hypnogram realism. Experimental results show that the unsupervised approach can boost Cohen’s kappa by up to 0.29 for certain degradations without reducing performance, though it consistently falls short of theoretical optimal performance and showed minimal benefit in a real-world domain mismatch scenario.
为解决移动睡眠监测中无标签数据下信号退化问题，该研究将预训练的u-sleep模型与判别器网络结合，通过特征空间对齐实现目标域信号的自适应。在人为引入多种退化类型后，该方法在不同退化场景下均能稳定提升Cohen’s kappa值（最低0.03，最高0.29），且从未降低性能。但其表现未达理论最优，且在真实跨研究域匹配测试中收益微弱，表明尚需进一步优化方可投入实际应用。
PRiMeFlow: Capturing Complex Expression Heterogeneity in Perturbation Response Modelling
Authors: Zichao Yan, Yan Wu, Mica Xu Ji, Chaitra Agrahar, Esther Wershof, Marcel Nassar, Mehrshad Sadria, Ridvan Eksi, Vladimir Trifonov, Ignacio Ibarra, Telmo Felgueira, Błażej Osiński, Rory Stark
First: 2026-04-15T15:33:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:33:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting the effects of perturbations in-silico on cell state can identify drivers of cell behavior at scale and accelerate drug discovery. However, modeling challenges remain due to the inherent heterogeneity of single cell gene expression and the complex, latent gene dependencies. Here, we present PRiMeFlow, an end-to-end flow matching based approach to directly model the effects of genetic and small molecule perturbations in the gene expression space. The distribution-fitting approach taken by PRiMeFlow enables it to accurately approximate the empirical distribution of single-cell gene expression, which we demonstrate through extensive benchmarking inside PerturBench. Through ablation studies, we also validate important model design choices such as operating in gene expression space and parameterizing the velocity field with a U-Net architecture. The PRiMeFlow architecture was used as the basis for the model that won the Generalist Prize in the first ARC Virtual Cell Challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRiMeFlow：在扰动响应建模中捕捉复杂的表达异质性
预测扰动对细胞状态的体外影响，可大规模识别细胞行为驱动因素并加速药物发现。然而，由于单细胞基因表达的固有异质性及复杂的潜在基因依赖关系，建模仍面临挑战。本文提出PRiMeFlow，这是一种端到端的流匹配方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动的影响。PRiMeFlow采用分布拟合方法，能够准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布，我们在PerturBench中的大量基准测试中验证了这一点。通过消融研究，我们还验证了重要模型设计选择，如在基因表达空间中操作，并使用U-Net架构参数化速度场。PRiMeFlow架构被用作赢得首届ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛“通用奖”的模型基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict perturbation effects on cell states to identify biological drivers and accelerate drug discovery, PRiMeFlow introduces an end-to-end flow matching method that models genetic and small molecule perturbations directly in gene expression space. By fitting distributions of single-cell gene expression data, it captures complex heterogeneity and latent gene dependencies. Experimental results across PerturBench benchmarks show high accuracy in approximating empirical expression distributions, and ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of operating in gene expression space and using a U-Net to parameterize the velocity field, with the model winning the Generalist Prize in the ARC Virtual Cell Challenge.
为预测扰动对细胞状态的影响以识别细胞驱动因子并加速药物发现，PRiMeFlow提出了一种端到端的流匹配方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动。通过拟合单细胞基因表达数据的分布，该方法捕捉了复杂的异质性和潜在基因依赖关系。在PerturBench上的广泛基准测试验证了其准确性，消融实验也证实了关键设计选择，如在基因表达空间中操作和使用U-Net参数化速度场。该模型在首届ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛中赢得通用奖。
HiProto: Hierarchical Prototype Learning for Interpretable Object Detection Under Low-quality Conditions
Authors: Jianlin Xiang, Linhui Dai, Xue Yang, Chaolei Yang, Yanshan Li
First: 2026-04-15T15:26:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:26:21+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Interpretability is essential for deploying object detection systems in critical applications, especially under low-quality imaging conditions that degrade visual information and increase prediction uncertainty. Existing methods either enhance image quality or design complex architectures, but often lack interpretability and fail to improve semantic discrimination. In contrast, prototype learning enables interpretable modeling by associating features with class-centered semantics, which can provide more stable and interpretable representations under degradation. Motivated by this, we propose HiProto, a new paradigm for interpretable object detection based on hierarchical prototype learning. By constructing structured prototype representations across multiple feature levels, HiProto effectively models class-specific semantics, thereby enhancing both semantic discrimination and interpretability. Building upon prototype modeling, we first propose a Region-to-Prototype Contrastive Loss (RPC-Loss) to enhance the semantic focus of prototypes on target regions. Then, we propose a Prototype Regularization Loss (PR-Loss) to improve the distinctiveness among class prototypes. Finally, we propose a Scale-aware Pseudo Label Generation Strategy (SPLGS) to suppress mismatched supervision for RPC-Loss, thereby preserving the robustness of low-level prototype representations. Experiments on ExDark, RTTS, and VOC2012-FOG demonstrate that HiProto achieves competitive results while offering clear interpretability through prototype responses, without relying on image enhancement or complex architectures. Our code will be available at https://github.com/xjlDestiny/HiProto.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiProto：面向低质量条件下的可解释目标检测的层次原型学习
可解释性对于在关键应用中部署目标检测系统至关重要，尤其是在图像质量差、视觉信息退化且预测不确定性增高的条件下。现有方法要么增强图像质量，要么设计复杂架构，但往往缺乏可解释性，且未能提升语义判别能力。相比之下，原型学习通过将特征与类中心语义关联，可提供更稳定且可解释的表示，从而在退化条件下增强模型性能。受此启发，我们提出HiProto，一种基于层次原型学习的可解释目标检测新范式。通过在多个特征层级构建结构化原型表示，HiProto有效建模类特定语义，从而提升语义判别能力和可解释性。在原型建模基础上，我们首先提出区域到原型对比损失（RPC-Loss），以增强原型对目标区域的语义聚焦；接着提出原型正则化损失（PR-Loss），以提升各类原型间的区分度；最后提出尺度感知伪标签生成策略（SPLGS），用于抑制RPC-Loss中的不匹配监督，从而保持低层原型表示的鲁棒性。在ExDark、RTTS和VOC2012-FOG数据集上的实验表明，HiProto在保持原型响应清晰可解释的同时，无需图像增强或复杂架构，即可取得具有竞争力的性能。代码将开源于https://github.com/xjlDestiny/HiProto.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable object detection under degraded imaging conditions, HiProto introduces a hierarchical prototype learning framework that models class-specific semantics across multiple feature levels to enhance both discrimination and interpretability. It employs a Region-to-Prototype Contrastive Loss to sharpen prototype focus on target regions, a Prototype Regularization Loss to enforce distinctiveness among class prototypes, and a Scale-aware Pseudo Label Generation Strategy to suppress noisy supervision and preserve low-level prototype robustness. Experiments on ExDark, RTTS, and VOC2012-FOG show that HiProto achieves competitive detection performance without image enhancement or complex architectures, while providing clear, interpretable prototype responses.
为解决低质量图像条件下目标检测缺乏可解释性的问题，HiProto提出一种分层原型学习框架，通过多层级特征建模类特定语义。该方法结合区域到原型对比损失增强原型对目标区域的关注，原型正则化损失提升类间原型区分度，并采用尺度感知伪标签生成策略抑制噪声监督，保持低层原型表示的鲁棒性。在ExDark、RTTS和VOC2012-FOG数据集上的实验表明，HiProto在保持高检测精度的同时，提供清晰可解释的原型响应，无需图像增强或复杂架构。
BOAT: Navigating the Sea of In Silico Predictors for Antibody Design via Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization
Authors: Jackie Rao, Ferran Gonzalez Hernandez, Leon Gerard, Alexandra Gessner
First: 2026-04-15T15:25:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:25:52+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the 29th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Antibody lead optimization is inherently a multi-objective challenge in drug discovery. Achieving a balance between different drug-like properties is crucial for the development of viable candidates, and this search becomes exponentially challenging as desired properties grow. The ever-growing zoo of sophisticated in silico tools for predicting antibody properties calls for an efficient joint optimization procedure to overcome resource-intensive sequential filtering pipelines. We present BOAT, a versatile Bayesian optimization framework for multi-property antibody engineering. Our `plug-and-play' framework couples uncertainty-aware surrogate modeling with a genetic algorithm to jointly optimize various predicted antibody traits while enabling efficient exploration of sequence space. Through systematic benchmarking against genetic algorithms and newer generative learning approaches, we demonstrate competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods for multi-objective protein optimization. We identify clear regimes where surrogate-driven optimization outperforms expensive generative approaches and establish practical limits imposed by sequence dimensionality and oracle costs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BOAT：通过多目标贝叶斯优化在体外预测器海洋中导航以设计抗体
抗体先导优化在药物发现中本质上是多目标挑战。在不同药物样性质之间取得平衡对于开发可行候选物至关重要，而随着期望性质的增加，该搜索难度呈指数级增长。随着日益增多的用于预测抗体性质的复杂体外工具，亟需一种高效的联合优化程序以克服资源密集型的串行筛选流程。我们提出了BOAT，一种用于多性质抗体工程的通用贝叶斯优化框架。我们的“即插即用”框架结合了不确定性感知的代理建模与遗传算法，可同时优化多种预测的抗体特性，并高效探索序列空间。通过与遗传算法和新兴生成学习方法的系统基准测试，我们展示了其在多目标蛋白质优化中与最先进方法具有竞争力的表现。我们识别出代理驱动优化优于昂贵生成方法的明确场景，并确立了由序列维度和查询成本所施加的实用限制。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to efficiently optimize multiple conflicting antibody properties in drug discovery, BOAT introduces a Bayesian optimization framework that combines uncertainty-aware surrogate models with a genetic algorithm to jointly optimize predicted antibody traits. By enabling parallel exploration of sequence space without requiring expensive generative sampling, BOAT outperforms or matches state-of-the-art methods in multi-objective protein optimization benchmarks. Experimental results show that surrogate-based optimization excels in regimes with limited oracle access and high sequence dimensionality, establishing practical boundaries for generative approaches.
为应对抗体设计中多目标属性平衡的挑战，BOAT提出一种结合不确定性感知代理模型与遗传算法的贝叶斯优化框架，可并行优化多种预测抗体特性。实验表明，该方法在多目标蛋白优化任务中表现优于或媲美当前最先进方法，尤其在代理模型成本低于生成模型的场景下优势明显，但其性能受限于序列维度和查询成本。
Leveraging LLM-GNN Integration for Open-World Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs
Authors: Hussein Abdallah, Ibrahim Abdelaziz, Panos Kalnis, Essam Mansour
First: 2026-04-15T15:25:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:25:25+00:00
Comments: 18 pages,6 figures,10 tables. https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.26/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-world Question Answering (OW-QA) over knowledge graphs (KGs) aims to answer questions over incomplete or evolving KGs. Traditional KGQA assumes a closed world where answers must exist in the KG, limiting real-world applicability. In contrast, open-world QA requires inferring missing knowledge based on graph structure and context. Large language models (LLMs) excel at language understanding but lack structured reasoning. Graph neural networks (GNNs) model graph topology but struggle with semantic interpretation. Existing systems integrate LLMs with GNNs or graph retrievers. Some support open-world QA but rely on structural embeddings without semantic grounding. Most assume observed paths or complete graphs, making them unreliable under missing links or multi-hop reasoning. We present GLOW, a hybrid system that combines a pre-trained GNN and an LLM for open-world KGQA. The GNN predicts top-k candidate answers from the graph structure. These, along with relevant KG facts, are serialized into a structured prompt (e.g., triples and candidates) to guide the LLM's reasoning. This enables joint reasoning over symbolic and semantic signals, without relying on retrieval or fine-tuning. To evaluate generalization, we introduce GLOW-BENCH, a 1,000-question benchmark over incomplete KGs across diverse domains. GLOW outperforms existing LLM-GNN systems on standard benchmarks and GLOW-BENCH, achieving up to 53.3% and an average 38% improvement. GitHub code and data are available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型与图神经网络融合实现知识图谱上的开放世界问答
知识图谱上的开放世界问答（OW-QA）旨在回答不完整或动态变化的知识图谱中的问题。传统知识图谱问答（KGQA）假设封闭世界，即答案必须存在于图谱中，限制了实际应用。相比之下，开放世界问答需基于图结构和上下文推断缺失知识。大语言模型（LLMs）擅长语言理解但缺乏结构化推理能力，图神经网络（GNNs）擅长建模图拓扑但难以处理语义解释。现有系统将LLMs与GNNs或图检索器结合，部分支持开放世界问答但依赖结构化嵌入而缺乏语义基础，多数假设观察到的路径或完整图谱，导致在缺失边或多跳推理场景下可靠性不足。本文提出GLOW，一种结合预训练GNN与LLM的混合系统，用于开放世界KGQA。GNN从图结构预测Top-k候选答案，这些答案与相关KG事实序列化为结构化提示（如三元组和候选），引导LLM进行推理，实现符号与语义信号的联合推理，无需依赖检索或微调。为评估泛化能力，我们构建GLOW-BENCH，包含1000个问题的跨多领域不完整KG基准。GLOW在标准基准和GLOW-BENCH上均优于现有LLM-GNN系统，最高提升53.3%，平均提升38%。GitHub代码与数据已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional knowledge graph question answering systems that assume a closed world, this work proposes GLOW, a hybrid model integrating a pre-trained graph neural network (GNN) and a large language model (LLM) to handle open-world QA over incomplete or evolving knowledge graphs. The GNN generates top-k candidate answers based on graph structure, which are then combined with relevant KG facts into a structured prompt to guide the LLM’s reasoning, enabling joint symbolic and semantic inference without retrieval or fine-tuning. Evaluated on GLOW-BENCH, a new 1,000-question benchmark over incomplete KGs, GLOW outperforms existing LLM-GNN systems by up to 53.3% and achieves an average 38% improvement across tasks.
为解决传统知识图谱问答系统假设世界封闭、无法处理不完整或动态图谱的问题，本文提出GLOW模型，融合预训练图神经网络（GNN）与大语言模型（LLM），用于开放世界知识图谱问答。GNN根据图结构生成候选答案，结合相关图谱事实构造结构化提示，引导LLM进行联合符号与语义推理，无需检索或微调。在新构建的GLOW-BENCH基准（1000道题，覆盖多种领域不完整图谱）上，GLOW在部分任务中提升达53.3%，平均提升38%，显著优于现有LLM-GNN系统，展现出更强的泛化能力和对缺失边与多跳推理的鲁棒性。
How Can We Synthesize High-Quality Pretraining Data? A Systematic Study of Prompt Design, Generator Model, and Source Data
Authors: Joel Niklaus, Atsuki Yamaguchi, Michal Štefánik, Guilherme Penedo, Hynek Kydlíček, Elie Bakouch, Lewis Tunstall, Edward Emanuel Beeching, Thibaud Frere, Colin Raffel, Leandro von Werra, Thomas Wolf
First: 2026-04-15T15:24:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:24:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic data is a standard component in training large language models, yet systematic comparisons across design dimensions, including rephrasing strategy, generator model, and source data, remain absent. We conduct extensive controlled experiments, generating over one trillion tokens, to identify critical factors in rephrasing web text into synthetic pretraining data. Our results reveal that structured output formats, such as tables, math problems, FAQs, and tutorials, consistently outperform both curated web baselines and prior synthetic methods. Notably, increasing the size of the generator model beyond 1B parameters provides no additional benefit. Our analysis also demonstrates that the selection of the original data used for mixing substantially influences performance. By applying our findings, we develop \textbf{\textsc{FinePhrase}}, a 486-billion-token open dataset of rephrased web text. We show that \textsc{FinePhrase} outperforms all existing synthetic data baselines while reducing generation costs by up to 30 times. We provide the dataset, all prompts, and the generation framework to the research community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何合成高质量的预训练数据？提示设计、生成模型与源数据的系统性研究
合成数据是训练大语言模型的标准组成部分，但关于提示设计策略、生成模型和源数据等维度的系统性比较仍缺失。我们进行了广泛的受控实验，生成超过一万亿个token，以识别将网页文本重写为合成预训练数据的关键因素。结果表明，结构化输出格式（如表格、数学题、FAQ和教程）始终优于人工精选的网页基线和以往的合成方法。值得注意的是，将生成模型规模超过10亿参数并不会带来额外收益。我们的分析还表明，混合所用原始数据的选择对性能有显著影响。基于这些发现，我们开发了\textbf{\textsc{FinePhrase}}，一个包含4860亿token的重写网页文本公开数据集。我们证明，\textsc{FinePhrase}在性能上优于所有现有合成数据基线，同时将生成成本降低高达30倍。我们向研究社区提供该数据集、所有提示和生成框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic evaluation of synthetic pretraining data generation, this study conducts large-scale controlled experiments generating over one trillion tokens to analyze how prompt design, generator model size, and source data selection affect quality. Results show that structured formats like tables, math problems, and FAQs yield superior performance over both curated web data and prior synthetic methods, while generator models larger than 1B parameters offer no benefit. The choice of source data significantly impacts outcomes, leading to the creation of FinePhrase—a 486-billion-token dataset that outperforms existing synthetic baselines and reduces generation costs by up to 30x.
为填补合成预训练数据生成方法系统性评估的空白，该研究通过生成超一万亿token的受控实验，分析提示设计、生成器模型规模与源数据对质量的影响。结果表明，表格、数学题、FAQ和教程等结构化格式表现显著优于精选网页文本或以往合成方法，且生成器模型规模超过10亿参数无额外收益。研究还发现源数据选择对性能影响重大，由此构建了FinePhrase数据集——包含4860亿token的重写网页文本，性能超越现有合成数据基线，同时生成成本降低30倍以上。
MApLe: Multi-instance Alignment of Diagnostic Reports and Large Medical Images
Authors: Felicia Bader, Philipp Seeböck, Anastasia Bartashova, Ulrike Attenberger, Georg Langs
First: 2026-04-15T15:19:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:19:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted for MIDL 2026; Reviews available at https://openreview.net/forum?id=M8OO3CRbL9#discussion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In diagnostic reports, experts encode complex imaging data into clinically actionable information. They describe subtle pathological findings that are meaningful in their anatomical context. Reports follow relatively consistent structures, expressing diagnostic information with few words that are often associated with tiny but consequential image observations. Standard vision language models struggle to identify the associations between these informative text components and small locations in the images. Here, we propose "MApLe", a multi-task, multi-instance vision language alignment approach that overcomes these limitations. It disentangles the concepts of anatomical region and diagnostic finding, and links local image information to sentences in a patch-wise approach. Our method consists of a text embedding trained to capture anatomical and diagnostic concepts in sentences, a patch-wise image encoder conditioned on anatomical structures, and a multi-instance alignment of these representations. We demonstrate that MApLe can successfully align different image regions and multiple diagnostic findings in free-text reports. We show that our model improves the alignment performance compared to state-of-the-art baseline models when evaluated on several downstream tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/cirmuw/MApLe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MApLe：诊断报告与大型医学图像的多实例对齐
在诊断报告中，专家将复杂的影像数据编码为临床可操作的信息，描述在解剖学背景下具有重要意义的细微病理发现。报告结构相对一致，用少量词汇表达诊断信息，这些词汇常与图像中微小但关键的观察相关。标准视觉语言模型难以识别这些信息性文本成分与图像中小区域之间的关联。本文提出“MApLe”，一种多任务、多实例视觉语言对齐方法，克服上述局限。该方法将解剖区域与诊断发现概念分离，并以图像块方式将局部图像信息与句子关联。我们的方法包括：用于捕捉句子中解剖与诊断概念的文本嵌入、受解剖结构条件约束的图像块编码器，以及这些表示的多实例对齐。实验表明，MApLe可成功对齐不同图像区域与自由文本报告中的多个诊断发现。在多个下游任务评估中，我们的模型相比现有最先进基线模型显著提升对齐性能。代码开源于 https://github.com/cirmuw/MApLe。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that standard vision-language models fail to link sparse, clinically significant text in diagnostic reports to small image regions, MApLe introduces a multi-task, multi-instance alignment framework that separately encodes anatomical regions and diagnostic findings, then aligns them patch-wise. The method combines a text encoder capturing diagnostic concepts, a structure-conditioned image encoder, and a multi-instance alignment mechanism. Experimental results show MApLe outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple downstream tasks, demonstrating stronger alignment between textual descriptions and corresponding image patches.
为了解决现有视觉语言模型难以将诊断报告中稀疏但关键的文本与图像中小区域对齐的问题，MApLe提出了一种多任务、多实例对齐框架，将解剖区域与诊断发现解耦。该方法包含用于捕捉语义概念的文本嵌入、基于解剖结构条件化的图像块编码器，并以实例级方式对齐二者表征。实验表明，MApLe在多个下游任务上优于当前最优基线模型，展现出更强的文本与局部图像块对齐能力。
Provably Efficient Offline-to-Online Value Adaptation with General Function Approximation
Authors: Shangzhe Li, Weitong Zhang
First: 2026-04-15T15:17:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:17:40+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study value adaptation in offline-to-online reinforcement learning under general function approximation. Starting from an imperfect offline pretrained $Q$-function, the learner aims to adapt it to the target environment using only a limited amount of online interaction. We first characterize the difficulty of this setting by establishing a minimax lower bound, showing that even when the pretrained $Q$-function is close to optimal $Q^\star$, online adaptation can be no more efficient than pure online RL on certain hard instances. On the positive side, under a novel structural condition on the offline-pretrained value functions, we propose O2O-LSVI, an adaptation algorithm with problem-dependent sample complexity that provably improves over pure online RL. Finally, we complement our theory with neural-network experiments that demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有通用函数逼近的离线到在线价值自适应的可证明高效方法
我们研究在通用函数逼近下，离线到在线强化学习中的价值自适应问题。从一个不完美的离线预训练Q函数出发，学习者仅通过有限的在线交互将其适应到目标环境。我们首先通过建立最小最大下界，刻画该设置的难度，表明即使预训练Q函数接近最优Q*，在线自适应在某些困难实例上也无法比纯在线强化学习更高效。另一方面，在离线预训练价值函数满足一种新颖结构条件的前提下，我们提出了O2O-LSVI算法，该算法具有问题依赖的样本复杂度，并可证明优于纯在线强化学习。最后，我们通过神经网络实验补充理论，展示所提方法的实际有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of adapting offline-pretrained Q-functions to new online environments with minimal interaction, this work establishes a minimax lower bound proving that adaptation can be no more efficient than pure online RL in worst-case scenarios. Under a novel structural assumption on the pretrained value function, the authors propose O2O-LSVI, an algorithm that achieves problem-dependent sample complexity, provably outperforming pure online RL. Experimental results using neural networks confirm the algorithm’s practical efficiency in real-world settings.
为了解决在有限在线交互下将离线预训练Q函数适配到新环境的难题，本文首先建立最小最大下界，证明在某些困难实例中，适应过程无法优于纯在线强化学习。在预训练值函数满足新颖结构条件的前提下，作者提出O2O-LSVI算法，其样本复杂度具有问题依赖性，可证优于纯在线强化学习。神经网络实验结果验证了该方法在实际场景中的有效性。
Learning Dynamics from Input-Output Data with Hamiltonian Gaussian Processes
Authors: Jan-Hendrik Ewering, Robin E. Herrmann, Niklas Wahlström, Thomas B. Schön, Thomas Seel
Venue: 8th Annual Learning for Dynamics & Control Conference (L4DC), 2026
First: 2025-11-07T15:28:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:17:28+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 8 figures,
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embedding non-restrictive prior knowledge, such as energy conservation laws, into learning methods is a key motive to construct physically consistent dynamics models from limited data, relevant for, e.g., model-based control. Recent work incorporates Hamiltonian dynamics into Gaussian Processes (GPs) to obtain uncertainty-quantifying, energy-consistent models, but these methods rely on -- rarely available -- velocity or momentum data. In this paper, we study dynamics learning using Hamiltonian GPs and focus on learning solely from input-output data, without relying on velocity or momentum measurements. Adopting a non-conservative formulation, energy exchange with the environment, e.g., through external forces or dissipation, can be captured. We provide a fully Bayesian scheme for estimating probability densities of unknown hidden states, GP hyperparameters, as well as structural hyperparameters, such as damping coefficients. The proposed method is evaluated in a nonlinear simulation case study and compared to a state-of-the-art approach that relies on momentum measurements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从输入输出数据中学习动力学模型：哈密顿高斯过程
将非限制性先验知识（如能量守恒定律）嵌入学习方法，是利用有限数据构建物理一致动力学模型的关键动机，这在模型驱动控制等领域尤为重要。近期工作将哈密顿动力学融入高斯过程（GPs），以获得能量化不确定性和保持能量一致性的模型，但这些方法依赖于——通常难以获得——速度或动量数据。本文研究使用哈密顿高斯过程进行动力学学习，专注于仅从输入输出数据中学习，而不依赖速度或动量测量。采用非保守形式，可捕捉与环境的能量交换，例如通过外力或耗散。我们提供了一种完全贝叶斯方案，用于估计未知隐藏状态的概率密度、GP超参数以及结构超参数（如阻尼系数）。所提方法在非线性仿真案例中进行了评估，并与依赖动量测量的最先进方法进行了比较。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to build physically consistent dynamics models from limited data without velocity measurements, this paper proposes a Hamiltonian Gaussian Process framework that learns dynamics solely from input-output data by incorporating non-conservative energy exchanges. The method employs a fully Bayesian approach to jointly estimate hidden state densities, GP hyperparameters, and structural parameters like damping coefficients. Experimental results on a nonlinear simulation show that the proposed method achieves comparable or better performance than a state-of-the-art momentum-dependent method, while being applicable in scenarios where momentum data is unavailable.
为在缺乏速度测量的情况下从输入输出数据构建物理一致的动力学模型，本文提出一种哈密顿高斯过程框架，通过引入非保守能量交换机制，仅利用输入输出数据学习动力学。该方法采用全贝叶斯框架估计隐藏状态密度、高斯过程超参数及结构参数（如阻尼系数）。在非线性仿真实验中，该方法有效捕捉能量守恒的动力学行为，并优于依赖动量测量的现有最先进方法。
[Emerging Ideas] Artificial Tripartite Intelligence: A Bio-Inspired, Sensor-First Architecture for Physical AI
Authors: You Rim Choi, Subeom Park, Hyung-Sin Kim
First: 2026-04-15T15:10:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:10:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI moves from data centers to robots and wearables, scaling ever-larger models becomes insufficient. Physical AI operates under tight latency, energy, privacy, and reliability constraints, and its performance depends not only on model capacity but also on how signals are acquired through controllable sensors in dynamic environments. We present Artificial Tripartite Intelligence (ATI), a bio-inspired, sensor-first architectural contract for physical AI. ATI is tripartite at the systems level: a Brainstem (L1) provides reflexive safety and signal-integrity control, a Cerebellum (L2) performs continuous sensor calibration, and a Cerebral Inference Subsystem spanning L3/L4 supports routine skill selection and execution, coordination, and deep reasoning. This modular organization allows sensor control, adaptive sensing, edge-cloud execution, and foundation model reasoning to co-evolve within one closed-loop architecture, while keeping time-critical sensing and control on device and invoking higher-level inference only when needed. We instantiate ATI in a mobile camera prototype under dynamic lighting and motion. In our routed evaluation (L3-L4 split inference), compared to the default auto-exposure setting, ATI (L1/L2 adaptive sensing) improves end-to-end accuracy from 53.8% to 88% while reducing remote L4 invocations by 43.3%. These results show the value of co-designing sensing and inference for embodied AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：新兴理念：人工三元智能：一种面向物理AI的生物启发式、以传感器为核心的架构
随着AI从数据中心迁移到机器人和可穿戴设备，单纯扩展更大模型已显不足。物理AI需在严格的延迟、能耗、隐私和可靠性约束下运行，其性能不仅取决于模型容量，更取决于如何通过可控传感器在动态环境中采集信号。我们提出人工三元智能（ATI），一种面向物理AI的生物启发式、以传感器为核心的架构。ATI在系统层面分为三部分：脑干（L1）提供反射式安全与信号完整性控制，小脑（L2）执行持续传感器校准，大脑推理子系统（L3/L4）支持常规技能选择与执行、协调及深度推理。该模块化结构使传感器控制、自适应感知、边缘-云执行与基础模型推理可在同一闭环架构内协同演化，同时确保关键感知与控制在设备端实时运行，仅在必要时调用更高层推理。我们在动态光照与运动条件下，于移动相机原型中实现ATI。在路由评估（L3-L4推理拆分）中，相比默认自动曝光设置，ATI（L1/L2自适应感知）将端到端准确率从53.8%提升至88%，同时减少远程L4调用43.3%。结果表明，为具身AI协同设计感知与推理具有重要价值。
Summary / 总结
As AI transitions from data centers to physical devices like robots and wearables, scaling large models alone is inadequate due to strict constraints on latency, energy, and reliability. To address this, the paper introduces Artificial Tripartite Intelligence (ATI), a bio-inspired architecture that prioritizes sensor control and adaptive sensing. ATI divides the system into three layers: a Brainstem for safety and signal integrity, a Cerebellum for sensor calibration, and a Cerebral Inference Subsystem for reasoning and execution. Evaluated on a mobile camera under dynamic lighting and motion, ATI improved end-to-end accuracy from 53.8% to 88% while reducing remote inference calls by 43.3%, demonstrating that co-designing sensing and inference enhances performance in embodied AI.
随着AI从数据中心迁移到机器人和可穿戴设备，单纯扩大模型规模已无法满足低延迟、低功耗、高隐私和高可靠性的物理环境约束。为此，本文提出受生物启发的“人工三元智能”（ATI）架构，以传感器优先为核心，将系统划分为三部分：脑干层负责反射式安全与信号完整性，小脑层负责持续传感器校准，大脑推理子系统负责技能执行与深度推理。在动态光照与运动场景下的移动相机原型测试中，ATI将端到端准确率从53.8%提升至88%，同时减少远程推理调用43.3%，验证了感知与推理协同设计对具身AI的有效性。
SiLVR: A Simple Language-based Video Reasoning Framework
Authors: Ce Zhang, Yan-Bo Lin, Ziyang Wang, Mohit Bansal, Gedas Bertasius
First: 2025-05-30T17:59:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:09:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR (01/2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in test-time optimization have led to remarkable reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models (LLMs), enabling them to solve highly complex problems in math and coding. However, the reasoning capabilities of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) still significantly lag, especially for complex video-language tasks. To address this issue, we present SILVR, a Simple Language-based Video Reasoning framework that decomposes complex video understanding into two stages. In the first stage, SILVR transforms raw video into language-based representations using multisensory inputs, such as short clip captions and audio/speech subtitles. In the second stage, language descriptions are fed into a powerful reasoning LLM to solve complex video-language understanding tasks. To handle long-context multisensory inputs, we use an Adaptive Context Reduction scheme, which dynamically determines the temporal granularity with which to sample the tokens. Our simple, modular, and training-free video reasoning framework achieves the best-reported results on Video-MME (long), Video-MMMU (comprehension), Video-MMLU, CGBench, and EgoLife. Furthermore, our empirical study focused on video reasoning capabilities shows that, despite not being explicitly trained on video, strong reasoning LLMs can effectively aggregate multisensory input information from video, speech, and audio for complex temporal, causal, long-context, and knowledge acquisition reasoning tasks in video. More details can be found at https://sites.google.com/cs.unc.edu/silvr.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SiLVR：一种基于语言的简单视频推理框架
近期测试时优化的进展显著提升了大语言模型（LLMs）的推理能力，使其能够解决复杂的数学和编程问题。然而，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的推理能力仍明显滞后，尤其是在处理复杂视频-语言任务时。为解决此问题，我们提出了SiLVR，一种基于语言的简单视频推理框架，将复杂视频理解分解为两个阶段：第一阶段，SiLVR利用多感官输入（如短片段字幕、音频/语音字幕）将原始视频转化为语言表示；第二阶段，将语言描述输入强大的推理LLM以解决复杂视频-语言理解任务。为处理长上下文多感官输入，我们采用自适应上下文缩减方案，动态确定采样令牌的时间粒度。该简单、模块化、无需训练的视频推理框架在Video-MME（长时序）、Video-MMMU（理解）、Video-MMLU、CGBench和EgoLife上取得了最佳报告结果。此外，我们的实证研究表明，即使未在视频上显式训练，强大的推理LLM也能有效聚合来自视频、语音和音频的多感官输入信息，以完成复杂的时序、因果、长上下文和知识获取推理任务。更多详情请见 https://sites.google.com/cs.unc.edu/silvr。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the superior reasoning abilities of LLMs in text-based tasks but their underperformance in video-language reasoning, SILVR introduces a simple, training-free framework that decomposes video understanding into two stages: first, converting raw video into language representations via multisensory inputs like clip captions and subtitles; second, feeding these descriptions into a reasoning LLM. To manage long-context inputs, it employs an Adaptive Context Reduction scheme that dynamically adjusts temporal granularity. Experimental results show SILVR achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks including Video-MME (long), Video-MMMU (comprehension), Video-MMLU, CGBench, and EgoLife, demonstrating that strong LLMs can effectively reason over complex video tasks without explicit video training.
为解决多模态大语言模型在视频语言任务中推理能力不足的问题，SILVR提出一个两阶段框架：首先将视频通过多感官输入（如短片段字幕和音频字幕）转化为语言表示，再输入到强大的推理大语言模型中处理。为应对长上下文输入，采用自适应上下文压缩机制动态调整时间粒度。实验表明，SILVR在多个视频理解基准（如Video-MME、Video-MMMU、Video-MMLU、CGBench、EgoLife）上达到当前最优性能，证明即使未经视频训练，强推理大语言模型也能有效整合视频、语音和音频信息，完成复杂的时序、因果、长上下文与知识推理任务。
On the Fundamental Limitations of Dual Static CVaR Decompositions in Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Mathieu Godbout, Audrey Durand
First: 2025-07-18T15:18:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:09:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It was recently shown that dynamic programming (DP) methods for finding static CVaR-optimal policies in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) can fail when based on the dual formulation, yet the root cause of this failure remains unclear. We expand on these findings by shifting focus from policy optimization to the seemingly simpler task of policy evaluation. We show that evaluating the static CVaR of a given policy can be framed as two distinct minimization problems. We introduce a set of ``risk-assignment consistency constraints'' that must be satisfied for their solutions to match and we demonstrate that an empty intersection of these constraints is the source of previously observed evaluation errors. Quantifying the evaluation error as the \emph{CVaR evaluation gap}, we demonstrate that the issues observed when optimizing over the dual-based CVaR DP are explained by the returned policy having a non-zero CVaR evaluation gap. Finally, we leverage our proposed risk-assignment constraints perspective to prove that the search for a single, uniformly optimal policy on the dual CVaR decomposition is fundamentally limited, identifying an MDP where no single policy can be optimal across all initial risk levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于马尔可夫决策过程中双重静态CVaR分解的基本局限性
最近研究表明，基于对偶形式的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中寻找静态CVaR最优策略的动态规划（DP）方法可能失效，但其根本原因尚不明确。我们通过将研究重点从策略优化转向看似更简单的策略评估任务，进一步拓展了这些发现。我们表明，评估给定策略的静态CVaR可被表述为两个不同的最小化问题，并引入一组“风险分配一致性约束”，其解需满足这些约束才能一致；我们证明，这些约束集合的空交集正是之前观察到的评估错误的根源。我们将评估误差量化为“CVaR评估间隙”，并证明在基于对偶CVaR的DP优化中观察到的问题，可归因于返回策略具有非零的CVaR评估间隙。最后，我们利用所提出的风险分配约束视角证明，在对偶CVaR分解中寻找单一全局最优策略在本质上是受限的，并识别出一个MDP，其中不存在能在所有初始风险水平下均最优的单一策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed failure of dual-based CVaR optimization in MDPs, this work investigates the root cause by shifting focus to policy evaluation. It frames static CVaR evaluation as two distinct minimization problems and introduces risk-assignment consistency constraints that must be satisfied for their solutions to align; an empty intersection of these constraints explains prior evaluation errors. The authors quantify this mismatch as the CVaR evaluation gap and show that dual-based DP failures stem from policies exhibiting non-zero gaps. 本文探讨了基于对偶形式的动态规划方法在马尔可夫决策过程中求解静态CVaR最优策略时为何常失败，转而聚焦于策略评估问题。研究指出，评估策略的静态CVaR可建模为两个不同的最小化问题，并引入“风险分配一致性约束”，只有当这些约束有共同解时评估才正确；若约束无交集，则出现评估误差。作者将该误差量化为CVaR评估间隙，并证明对偶方法失败源于所生成策略存在非零间隙。最后，作者从风险分配视角证明：在某些MDP中，不存在一个能在所有风险水平下均最优的单一策略，因风险分配存在根本性不兼容。
Block-Based Pathfinding: A Minecraft System for Visualizing Graph Algorithms
Authors: Luca-Stefan Pirvu, Bogdan-Alexandru Maciuca, Andrei-Ciprian Rabu, Adrian-Marius Dumitran
First: 2026-04-15T15:07:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:07:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CSEDU 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph theory is a cornerstone of Computer Science education, yet entry-level students often struggle to map abstract node-edge relationships to practical applications. This paper presents the design and architecture of a Minecraft-based educational tool specifically built to visualize graph traversal and shortest-path algorithms. We propose a three-layer system: (1) a Grid Traversal module where terrain types (e.g., soul sand, ice) represent edge weights, allowing for the gamified study of shortest path algorithms; (2) a "Sky Graph" module for interactive 3D manipulation of both directed and undirected graphs; and (3) lessons and quizzes available through books. The system grounds its design in Constructionist learning theory, transitioning students from passive observers to active protagonists who physically manipulate algorithmic behavior. We additionally present a planned empirical evaluation using NASA-TLX and in-game telemetry to validate the system's pedagogical efficacy.

Creo: From One-Shot Image Generation to Progressive, Co-Creative Ideation
Authors: Zoe De Simone, Angie Boggust, Fredo Durand, Ashia Wilson, Arvind Satyanarayan
First: 2026-04-15T15:06:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:06:46+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image (T2I) systems enable rapid generation of high-fidelity imagery but are misaligned with how visual ideas develop. T2I systems generate outputs that make implicit visual decisions on behalf of the user, often introduce fine-grained details that can anchor users prematurely and limit their ability to keep options open early on, and cause unintended changes during editing that are difficult to correct and reduce users' sense of control. To address these concerns, we present Creo, a multi-stage T2I system that scaffolds image generation by progressing from rough sketches to high-resolution outputs, exposing intermediary abstractions where users can make incremental changes. Sketch-like abstractions invite user editing and allow users to keep design options open when ideas are still forming due to their provisional nature. Each stage in Creo can be modified with manual changes and AI-assisted operations, enabling fine-grained, step-wise control through a locking mechanism that preserves prior decisions so subsequent edits affect only specified regions or attributes. Users remain in the loop, making and verifying decisions across stages, while the system applies diffs instead of regenerating full images, reducing drift as fidelity increases. A comparative study with a one-shot baseline shows that participants felt stronger ownership over Creo outputs, as they were able to trace their decisions in building up the image. Furthermore, embedding-based analysis indicates that Creo outputs are less homogeneous than one-shot results. These findings suggest that multi-stage generation, combined with intermediate control and decision locking, is a key design principle for improving controllability, user agency, creativity, and output diversity in generative systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Creo：从单次图像生成到渐进式协同创意构思
文本到图像（T2I）系统虽能快速生成高保真图像，但其生成方式与视觉创意的实际发展过程不匹配。T2I系统会代用户做出隐含的视觉决策，常引入细粒度细节，导致用户过早锁定选项，限制早期设计灵活性；编辑时还可能引发难以修正的意外变化，削弱用户控制感。为解决这些问题，我们提出Creo——一种多阶段T2I系统，通过从粗略草图逐步生成高分辨率图像，向用户暴露中间抽象层，支持渐进式修改。草图式抽象便于用户编辑，因其临时性特点，有助于在创意尚未定型时保持设计选项开放。Creo各阶段均可通过手动修改或AI辅助操作调整，配合锁定机制，保留前期决策，确保后续编辑仅影响指定区域或属性。用户全程参与决策并验证，系统仅应用差异更新而非重生成完整图像，从而在保真度提升过程中减少漂移。与单次生成基线的对比研究表明，用户对Creo输出的归属感更强，因其能追溯自身决策过程。嵌入式分析亦表明，Creo输出比单次生成结果更具多样性。这些发现表明，结合中间控制与决策锁定的多阶段生成，是提升生成系统可控性、用户自主性、创造力及输出多样性的关键设计原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of one-shot text-to-image systems that limit user control and creativity through premature detail fixation and hard-to-reverse edits, Creo introduces a multi-stage generation framework that progresses from rough sketches to high-resolution images, allowing users to iteratively refine and lock decisions at each stage. By exposing provisional, sketch-like abstractions, Creo enables incremental user input and preserves prior choices via a locking mechanism that restricts subsequent edits to specific regions or attributes, reducing drift and enhancing control. Experimental results show users felt greater ownership over Creo outputs and were able to trace their design decisions more clearly, while embedding analysis revealed higher output diversity compared to one-shot baselines, indicating improved creativity and controllability.
为了解决单次生成图像系统中用户控制力弱、创意受限、细节过早固化及编辑不可逆等问题，Creo提出一种多阶段生成框架，从草图逐步演化至高分辨率图像，允许用户在各阶段进行迭代修改并锁定先前决策，确保后续编辑仅影响指定区域或属性。实验表明，用户对Creo生成结果拥有更强的控制感与归属感，能清晰追踪创作过程，且输出结果更具多样性，证明分阶段生成结合中间控制机制可有效提升可控性、创意表达与结果多样性。
HINTBench: Horizon-agent Intrinsic Non-attack Trajectory Benchmark
Authors: Jiacheng Wang, Jinchang Hou, Fabian Wang, Ping Jian, Chenfu Bao, Zhonghou Lv
First: 2026-04-15T15:06:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:06:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing agent-safety evaluation has focused mainly on externally induced risks. Yet agents may still enter unsafe trajectories under benign conditions. We study this complementary but underexplored setting through the lens of \emph{intrinsic} risk, where intrinsic failures remain latent, propagate across long-horizon execution, and eventually lead to high-consequence outcomes. To evaluate this setting, we introduce \emph{non-attack intrinsic risk auditing} and present \textbf{HINTBench}, a benchmark of 629 agent trajectories (523 risky, 106 safe; 33 steps on average) supporting three tasks: risk detection, risk-step localization, and intrinsic failure-type identification. Its annotations are organized under a unified five-constraint taxonomy. Experiments reveal a substantial capability gap: strong LLMs perform well on trajectory-level risk detection, but their performance drops to below 35 Strict-F1 on risk-step localization, while fine-grained failure diagnosis proves even harder. Existing guard models transfer poorly to this setting. These findings establish intrinsic risk auditing as an open challenge for agent safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HINTBench：面向水平代理内在非攻击轨迹的基准测试
现有代理安全评估主要关注外部诱导的风险。然而，在良性条件下，代理仍可能进入不安全轨迹。我们通过“内在风险”的视角研究这一互补但尚未充分探索的场景：内在故障潜伏、在长时程执行中传播，最终导致高后果结果。为评估此场景，我们提出“非攻击内在风险审计”，并发布**HINTBench**，包含629条代理轨迹（523条高风险，106条安全；平均33步），支持三项任务：风险检测、风险步骤定位及内在故障类型识别。其标注基于统一的五约束分类体系。实验揭示显著能力差距：强LLM在轨迹级风险检测表现良好，但在风险步骤定位任务中性能降至35 Strict-F1以下，而细粒度故障诊断则更难。现有防护模型难以迁移到此场景。这些发现确立了内在风险审计作为代理安全领域的开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate agent safety under benign conditions where intrinsic failures can lead to unsafe outcomes, the paper introduces HINTBench, a benchmark of 629 agent trajectories annotated for intrinsic risk. The method involves auditing trajectories for risk detection, risk-step localization, and failure-type identification using a unified five-constraint taxonomy. Experimental results show that while large language models perform well at detecting risky trajectories, their performance on localizing risky steps drops below 35 Strict-F1, and fine-grained failure diagnosis remains particularly challenging, indicating a significant capability gap and highlighting the difficulty of transferring existing guard models to this setting.
为评估在无外部攻击条件下仍可能引发危险的内在风险，该研究构建了HINTBench基准数据集，包含629条轨迹（523条高风险，106条安全），每条平均33步，支持风险检测、风险步骤定位与内在故障类型识别三项任务，采用统一五约束分类体系标注。实验表明，尽管大语言模型在轨迹级风险检测上表现良好，但在风险步骤定位任务中准确率低于35 Strict-F1，精细故障诊断难度更大，揭示出内在风险审计能力存在显著差距，凸显其作为开放性安全挑战的重要性。
Restless Bandits with Individual Penalty Constraints: A New Near-Optimal Index Policy and How to Learn It
Authors: Nida Zamir, I-Hong Hou
First: 2026-04-05T12:53:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:05:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates the Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (RMAB) framework under individual penalty constraints to address resource allocation challenges in dynamic wireless networked environments. Unlike conventional RMAB models, our model allows each user (arm) to have distinct and stringent performance constraints, such as energy limits, activation limits, or age of information minimums, enabling the capture of diverse objectives including fairness and efficiency. To find the optimal resource allocation policy, we propose a new Penalty-Optimal Whittle (POW) index policy. The POW index of an user only depends on the user's transition kernel and penalty constraints, and remains invariable to system-wide features such as the number of users present and the amount of resource available. This makes it computationally tractable to calculate the POW indices offline without any need for online adaptation. Moreover, we theoretically prove that the POW index policy is asymptotically optimal while satisfying all individual penalty constraints. We also introduce a deep reinforcement learning algorithm to efficiently learn the POW index on the fly. Simulation results across various applications and system configurations further demonstrate that the POW index policy not only has near-optimal performance but also significantly outperforms other existing policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带个体惩罚约束的不安分多臂老虎机：一种新的近最优索引策略及其学习方法
本文研究了在个体惩罚约束下的不安分多臂老虎机（RMAB）框架，以解决动态无线网络环境中资源分配的挑战。与传统RMAB模型不同，我们的模型允许每个用户（臂）具有不同且严格的性能约束，如能量限制、激活限制或信息年龄最小值，从而能够捕捉包括公平性和效率在内的多种目标。为寻找最优资源分配策略，我们提出了一种新的惩罚最优惠特尔（POW）索引策略。用户的POW索引仅依赖于其转移核和惩罚约束，且与系统全局特征（如用户数量和资源量）无关，因此可离线计算POW索引而无需在线调整。此外，我们从理论上证明了POW索引策略在满足所有个体惩罚约束的前提下渐近最优。我们还引入了一种深度强化学习算法，以高效地在线学习POW索引。在多种应用场景和系统配置下的仿真结果进一步表明，POW索引策略不仅具有近最优性能，而且显著优于其他现有策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by resource allocation challenges in dynamic wireless networks with diverse user constraints, this paper proposes a new Penalty-Optimal Whittle (POW) index policy for Restless Multi-Armed Bandits under individual penalty constraints. The POW index, derived from each user’s transition kernel and penalty limits, is independent of global system parameters, enabling offline computation and eliminating the need for online adaptation. Theoretically, the POW policy is proven asymptotically optimal while respecting all individual constraints. Experimentally, it achieves near-optimal performance across multiple scenarios and significantly outperforms existing policies when learned via deep reinforcement learning.
为解决动态无线网络中用户异构约束下的资源分配问题，本文提出了一种新的惩罚最优惠特尔（POW）索引策略，用于满足个体惩罚约束的 restless 多臂老虎机问题。POW 索引仅依赖于各用户自身的转移核与惩罚约束，与全局系统参数无关，支持离线计算。理论上证明该策略在满足所有约束条件下渐近最优。实验中引入深度强化学习算法实现在线学习 POW 索引，仿真结果表明其性能接近最优，显著优于现有策略。
Quantum Machine Learning for Colorectal Cancer Data: Anastomotic Leak Classification and Risk Factors
Authors: Vojtěch Novák, Ivan Zelinka, Lenka Přibylová, Lubomír Martínek, Vladimír Benčurík, Martin Beseda
First: 2026-04-15T15:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-15T15:03:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study evaluates colorectal risk factors and compares classical models against Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) for anastomotic leak prediction. Analyzing clinical data with 14\% leak prevalence, we tested ZZFeatureMap encodings with RealAmplitudes and EfficientSU2 ansatze under simulated noise. $F_β$-optimized quantum configurations yielded significantly higher sensitivity (83.3\%) than classical baselines (66.7\%). This demonstrates that quantum feature spaces better prioritize minority class identification, which is critical for low-prevalence clinical risk prediction. Our work explores various optimizers under noisy conditions, highlighting key trade-offs and future directions for hardware deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于结直肠癌数据的量子机器学习：吻合口漏分类与风险因素
本研究评估结直肠癌风险因素，并将经典模型与量子神经网络（QNNs）进行比较，以预测吻合口漏。分析14%漏发生率的临床数据，我们在模拟噪声环境下测试了ZZFeatureMap编码与RealAmplitudes及EfficientSU2结构。经$F_β$优化的量子配置显著高于经典基线（敏感度83.3% vs 66.7%）。这表明量子特征空间更优地优先识别少数类，这对低发生率临床风险预测至关重要。本研究在噪声条件下探索了多种优化器，突出了关键权衡并指明了硬件部署的未来方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately predicting rare anastomotic leaks in colorectal cancer patients, this study compares classical machine learning models with Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) using clinical data with 14% leak prevalence. The QNNs employed ZZFeatureMap encodings with RealAmplitudes and EfficientSU2 ansatze under simulated noise, and were optimized using $F_β$ metrics to prioritize sensitivity. Results showed QNNs achieved 83.3% sensitivity versus 66.7% for classical models, indicating quantum feature representations better capture minority class patterns critical for low-prevalence medical risk assessment.
为解决结直肠癌手术中罕见吻合口漏的预测难题，本研究在仅14%漏诊率的临床数据上，对比了经典机器学习模型与量子神经网络（QNN），采用ZZFeatureMap编码和RealAmplitudes或EfficientSU2结构，并在模拟噪声环境下测试。实验表明，Fβ优化的量子配置敏感度达83.3%，显著高于经典基线（66.7%），说明量子特征空间更擅长识别少数类，对低发生率临床风险预测至关重要。研究还评估了不同优化器在噪声条件下的表现，揭示了硬件部署的关键权衡点。
